# Vietnam Sips & Sounds: cafés, bars, music, games and books in Hanoi, Ninh Bình, Sa Pa, Phú Quốc and Saigon

Researched on 5 October 2026 by 80 AI agents in two waves. 56 researchers each covered one beat (egg coffee, specialty roasters, cocktail bars, bia hơi, jazz, phòng trà, ca trù, karaoke, esports centres, laser tag and shooting ranges, bookshops, literary landmarks, Ninh Bình's xẩm and more). 21 curators merged the finds, checked that each place is still operating and ranked them, and 3 checkers re-verified the places with weaker evidence. 271 places made the cut. Venues in Vietnam open, move and close fast, so check Google Maps before you go.

Interactive version with city tabs, filters and map links: https://claude.ai/artifact/6cxVpTNY3yLf6Dh4vepLh6

**Key:** ★ = must-go. Cafés and bars, price per drink: $ under 50k₫ · $$ 50–150k₫ · $$$ 150–300k₫ · $$$$ over 300k₫. Games, books and shows, price per person: $ free or under 100k₫ · $$ 100–300k₫ · $$$ 300–700k₫ · $$$$ more (25,000₫ ≈ US$1). Status: *checked 2026*, *checked 2025*, *likely open* or *unverified*.

Vietnam merged its provinces and abolished city districts in 2025, so official addresses may show new ward names. Locals and Grab drivers still use the old district names used here.

## Contents

- [Hanoi](#hanoi): 19 cafés, 19 bars, 24 music, 12 games, 14 books & literary
- [Ninh Bình](#ninh-bình): 8 cafés, 6 bars, 6 music, 3 games, 5 books & literary
- [Sa Pa](#sa-pa): 9 cafés, 8 bars, 10 music, 3 games, 2 books & literary
- [Phú Quốc](#phú-quốc): 7 cafés, 13 bars, 9 music, 3 games, 3 books & literary
- [Saigon](#saigon): 19 cafés, 20 bars, 25 music, 11 games, 13 books & literary

## Hanoi

*Hà Nội · the capital, in the north*

Hanoi rewards slow days and late nights, most of it within a walk of Hoàn Kiếm Lake. Start with egg coffee up a dim staircase, drink bia hơi on plastic stools at dusk, then choose between cocktails at two of Asia's 50 Best Bars and saxophone at the long-running Binh Minh Jazz Club. It is the best city in Vietnam for northern traditional music and for literary history, from ca trù to the Temple of Literature, and its big gaming chains rent 240–500 Hz PCs for about 10,000–25,000₫ an hour.

### Hanoi: Cafés (Cà phê)

Hanoi takes its coffee strong, sweet and slow: dripped through a phin onto condensed milk, drunk on low plastic stools facing the street, or topped with the whipped egg foam invented here in 1946, when milk was scarce. Beyond the family institutions serving egg, yogurt and coconut coffee, a young wave of roasters is showing what Vietnamese beans can do, from Fine Robusta in ceramic phins to flights of Sơn La and Đà Lạt arabica. The city also hides its best seats up dim staircases behind silk shops, in 1930s French villas and Soviet-era housing blocks, and on rooftops and balconies over Hoàn Kiếm Lake and Long Biên Bridge.

#### Egg coffee & classics

- **★ Café Giảng** · Old Quarter (Hoàn Kiếm) · $ · *checked 2026*
  - This is where egg coffee was born. In 1946, with milk scarce, Metropole Hotel bartender Nguyễn Văn Giảng whipped egg yolk with sugar and condensed milk over strong coffee, and his family still serves it down this Old Quarter alley. The custard-thick foam of the original is the yardstick for every copy in town.
  - Tip: Order the hot egg coffee (cà phê trứng nóng), which arrives standing in a bowl of hot water, or the egg cocoa if you like it sweeter. Go between 9:30 and 11:00 on a weekday to dodge the queues. The alley original has far more atmosphere than the Tràng Tiền and Lotte Mall branches.
  - Hours: 7:00–22:00 daily (Nguyễn Hữu Huân, per official site)
  - Address: 39 Nguyễn Hữu Huân (down a narrow alley); branches at 57 Tràng Tiền and Lotte Mall West Lake, 3rd floor
  - Price: Egg coffee about 35–60k VND (2025–26 sources vary; a Mar 2026 guide quotes ~35k hot, ~40k iced)
  - [Google Maps](https://www.google.com/maps/search/?api=1&query=Cafe%20Giang%2C%2039%20Nguy%E1%BB%85n%20H%E1%BB%AFu%20Hu%C3%A2n%2C%20Ho%C3%A0n%20Ki%E1%BA%BFm%2C%20Hanoi) · [Source](https://cafegiang.vn)
- **★ Café Đinh** · Hoàn Kiếm Lake, east shore (Hoàn Kiếm) · $ · *checked 2026*
  - Mrs Bích, daughter of egg-coffee inventor Nguyễn Văn Giảng, opened this upstairs café in 1987, and it still serves the family's 1946 recipe; plenty of fans rate its lighter foam above Giảng's. A dim staircase leads to a worn, photo-hung room whose tiny balcony looks straight out over Hoàn Kiếm Lake.
  - Tip: Find the narrow passage between the shopfronts at No. 13 and climb to the 2nd floor. The balcony holds only a handful of people, so come before 9am or in the late afternoon. If you've already had egg coffee at Giảng, order egg cocoa (cacao trứng) here.
  - Hours: About 7:00–22:00 daily (2025–26 listings)
  - Address: 2nd floor, 13 Đinh Tiên Hoàng
  - Price: Egg coffee about 30–55k VND (2025–26 sources vary)
  - [Google Maps](https://www.google.com/maps/search/?api=1&query=Cafe%20Dinh%2C%2013%20%C4%90inh%20Ti%C3%AAn%20Ho%C3%A0ng%2C%20Ho%C3%A0n%20Ki%E1%BA%BFm%2C%20Hanoi) · [Source](https://www.asiatouradvisor.com/get-inspired/vietnam/hanoi-egg-coffee-on-cnn-the-secret-of-a-70-year-old-coffee/)
- **Café Duy Trí** · Yên Phụ, between Trúc Bạch and West Lake · $ · *checked 2025*
  - One family has run this café since 1936 in a narrow three-storey house with ceilings so low you have to stoop, and it roasts its own beans by the door. It's Hanoi's reference for yogurt coffee (sữa chua cà phê): house-made yogurt is semi-frozen and phin coffee is dripped over it, so it's closer to a coffee slushie than a drink.
  - Tip: Order sữa chua cà phê, since the semi-frozen yogurt is the whole point, and climb to the top floor. Bring cash. Search the café's name on Google Maps rather than trusting listicle addresses, as some 2026 lists wrongly put it on Từ Hoa.
  - Hours: About 6:00–22:00 (Google-sourced listing)
  - Address: 43A Yên Phụ
  - Price: Yogurt coffee about 30k VND (recent guide); 25–40k is typical at traditional cafés
  - [Google Maps](https://www.google.com/maps/search/?api=1&query=Cafe%20Duy%20Tri%2C%2043A%20Y%C3%AAn%20Ph%E1%BB%A5%2C%20Hanoi) · [Source](https://saigoneer.com/hanoi-street-food-restaurants/21341-h%E1%BA%BBm-gems-duy-tri-one-of-hanoi%E2%80%99s-original-cafes)
- **Café Lâm** · Old Quarter (Hoàn Kiếm) · $ · *unverified*
  - Since 1952 this has been where Hanoi's painters, poets and musicians drink. Founder Nguyễn Văn Lâm gave struggling artists such as Bùi Xuân Phái and Văn Cao coffee on credit. Work by Phái, Nguyễn Sáng and Dương Bích Liên still hangs above a dark-roast phin that has barely changed in decades.
  - Tip: Order a cà phê đen or nâu (black or milk phin) and take a street-side stool to watch Nguyễn Hữu Huân go by; Giảng is a few doors along at No. 39. There's no air-con, and evenings bring mosquitoes.
  - Hours: 7:00–22:00 (listings)
  - Address: 60 Nguyễn Hữu Huân
  - Price: 15–50k VND (Foody listing)
  - [Google Maps](https://www.google.com/maps/search/?api=1&query=Cafe%20Lam%2C%2060%20Nguy%E1%BB%85n%20H%E1%BB%AFu%20Hu%C3%A2n%2C%20Ho%C3%A0n%20Ki%E1%BA%BFm%2C%20Hanoi) · [Source](https://www.tatlerasia.com/dining/drinks/4-hanoi-legendary-coffee-shops-for-politicians-vn)
- **Cộng Cà Phê (Trích Sài, West Lake)** (Cộng Cà Phê) · West Lake (Tây Hồ) · $$ · *checked 2026*
  - Cộng turned cốt dừa cà phê, strong coffee blended with icy coconut-milk slush, into a national staple, served amid army-green, subsidy-era (bao cấp) décor. This roomy, light-filled branch on the West Lake shore road faces the water and makes a dependable sunset stop.
  - Tip: Order cốt dừa cà phê (add 'ít ngọt' for less sweet) and arrive for the golden half-hour before sunset. In the centre, the 27 Nhà Thờ branch by the Cathedral pours the same drink. Older guides still send you to the 2007 original at 152D Triệu Việt Vương or to 35A Nguyễn Hữu Huân, but neither is on Cộng's 2026 store list. West Lake road-widening (2026–30) may bring roadworks on Trích Sài.
  - Hours: 7:15–23:00 daily (Cộng store list)
  - Address: 75 Trích Sài (central branches: 27 Nhà Thờ, 116 Cầu Gỗ, 5 Trần Phú)
  - Price: Coconut coffee about 55–70k VND (2026 guides)
  - [Google Maps](https://www.google.com/maps/search/?api=1&query=C%E1%BB%99ng%20C%C3%A0%20Ph%C3%AA%2C%2075%20Tr%C3%ADch%20S%C3%A0i%2C%20T%C3%A2y%20H%E1%BB%93%2C%20Hanoi) · [Source](https://congcaphe.com/stores)

#### Specialty roasters

- **★ RAAW Coffee (Hàng Buồm)** · Old Quarter (Hoàn Kiếm) · $$ · *checked 2026*
  - Founded in 2019 by Q Arabica Grader Nguyễn Phương Linh, RAAW roasts only Vietnamese coffee bought directly from smallholders: Sơn La arabica, Đà Lạt Honey Typica, Đắk Lắk fine robusta, even Quảng Trị liberica. Its two airy floors on Hàng Buồm are the best single stop in Hanoi for tasting what Vietnam's own specialty beans can do.
  - Tip: Order the Phượt Set ('Vietnam Road Trip'), four Vietnamese coffees side by side, and ask to try the liberica (cà phê mít), Vietnam's rare third species. Because it's open until 11pm, it's the specialty bar that still works after dinner. Its 2-hour English brewing class (phin, V60, AeroPress, syphon; morning slots, max 10 people) is booked through Airbnb Experiences.
  - Hours: 8:00–23:00 daily (official site)
  - Address: 15 Hàng Buồm (roastery café: B3.7 Hoàng Đạo Thúy, Thanh Xuân)
  - Price: Beans 108–216k VND a bag; brewing class about US$35 per person (Airbnb listing)
  - [Google Maps](https://www.google.com/maps/search/?api=1&query=RAAW%20Coffee%2C%2015%20H%C3%A0ng%20Bu%E1%BB%93m%2C%20Ho%C3%A0n%20Ki%E1%BA%BFm%2C%20Hanoi) · [Source](https://raawcoffee.com/hanoi-hang-buom)
- **★ refined. specialty coffee** · St Joseph's Cathedral (Hoàn Kiếm) · $$ · *checked 2026*
  - Vũ Đình Tú opened refined. in 2021 to defend Vietnamese robusta. It brews specialty-grade Fine Robusta through its own ceramic phin filters, made in the Bát Tràng pottery village. The all-black slow bar tucked into a menswear store by the Cathedral is where Hanoi's coffee nerds find out how good a phin can be.
  - Tip: Start with a black Fine Robusta through the ceramic phin, then have an arabica pour-over to compare. Some listings give the address as 5 Nhà Thờ, so search 'refined. specialty coffee' on Google Maps. refined.coffee is not the café's website.
  - Address: 17 Nhà Thờ, Hàng Trống (slow bar inside the No Concept store); original Phin bar near Văn Miếu
  - [Google Maps](https://www.google.com/maps/search/?api=1&query=refined.%20specialty%20coffee%2C%20Nh%C3%A0%20Th%E1%BB%9D%2C%20Ho%C3%A0n%20Ki%E1%BA%BFm%2C%20Hanoi) · [Source](https://sprudge.com/in-hanoi-roaster-retailer-cafes-offer-a-glimpse-into-vietnams-changing-coffee-culture-468966.html)
- **Donkey Coffee Roastery** · Red River bank, Chương Dương (Hoàn Kiếm) · $$ · *checked 2026*
  - In a quiet alley on the Red River bank, with a shady garden and DIY industrial-vintage décor, a former graphic designer roasts a wide range of Vietnamese and imported specialty lots, including a natural-processed Gesha from neighbouring Laos. Its regular public cuppings are the easiest way to taste alongside Hanoi's coffee crowd.
  - Tip: Check the café's social pages for the next public cupping. Ask for whatever natural or Gesha lot is on the pour-over menu; in summer, the orange cold brew is the crowd favourite. Search by name on Maps, because the ward now shows as Hồng Hà.
  - Address: 37 ngõ 139 Chương Dương Độ (Hồng Hà ward)
  - [Google Maps](https://www.google.com/maps/search/?api=1&query=Donkey%20Coffee%20Roastery%2C%2037%20ng%C3%B5%20139%20Ch%C6%B0%C6%A1ng%20D%C6%B0%C6%A1ng%20%C4%90%E1%BB%99%2C%20Hanoi) · [Source](https://sprudge.com/in-hanoi-roaster-retailer-cafes-offer-a-glimpse-into-vietnams-changing-coffee-culture-468966.html)
- **VULAB Specialty Coffee** · French Quarter edge, off Trần Hưng Đạo (Hoàn Kiếm) · $$ · *checked 2026*
  - Pharmacist-turned-roaster Lê Quang Vũ, a Q Grader with SCA sensory certification, opened this lab-like white-and-grey bar with a small courtyard in 2023. Beans run from Vietnamese highland lots to Panama, Costa Rica and Ethiopia, tiered from 'common' to 'legendary', and are brewed on syphon, phin, AeroPress or moka pot.
  - Tip: Choose a higher-tier lot on syphon and take it out to the courtyard. ACID8 (34 Xóm Hà Hồi), a red-walled in-house roaster known for its whipped, foam-capped iced Americano, is a few doors down the same lane, so it's an easy two-stop crawl.
  - Address: 30B Xóm Hà Hồi, Trần Hưng Đạo
  - [Google Maps](https://www.google.com/maps/search/?api=1&query=VULAB%20Specialty%20Coffee%2C%2030B%20X%C3%B3m%20H%C3%A0%20H%E1%BB%93i%2C%20Ho%C3%A0n%20Ki%E1%BA%BFm%2C%20Hanoi) · [Source](https://www.thewaytocoffee.com/hanoi/)
- **30S Coffea Roastery** · Kim Liên collective housing (Kim Liên ward, formerly Đống Đa) · $$ · *checked 2026*
  - With Cư Xá closed since August 2026, this is the best way to drink coffee inside a real Soviet-era tập thể. The roastery sits in one of Hanoi's oldest collective-housing estates, and its brew counter wraps around the roaster so you drink while watching the roasting. The line-up includes a Fine Robusta ranked Top 1 at the Vietnam Amazing Cup 2025.
  - Tip: Ask for the award-winning Fine Robusta as a pour-over (Sprudge notes wine, tropical fruit, nut and brown sugar), then wander the estate's stairwells and courtyards. The founder, who has more than ten years in the trade, also runs barista training; message ahead to ask about English.
  - Address: 108B6 Kim Liên collective housing (tập thể Kim Liên), ngõ 43 Phạm Ngọc Thạch
  - [Google Maps](https://www.google.com/maps/search/?api=1&query=30S%20Coffea%20Roastery%2C%20ng%C3%B5%2043%20Ph%E1%BA%A1m%20Ng%E1%BB%8Dc%20Th%E1%BA%A1ch%2C%20Kim%20Li%C3%AAn%2C%20Hanoi) · [Source](https://sprudge.com/in-hanoi-roaster-retailer-cafes-offer-a-glimpse-into-vietnams-changing-coffee-culture-468966.html)
- **Kafeville** · Trúc Bạch Lake (Ba Đình) · $$ · *likely open*
  - Opened in March 2016, Kafeville was Hanoi's first café built around in-house-roasted pour-overs. It is still a calm, coffee-first room where the loudest sounds are the grinder and the scale.
  - Tip: Order a V60 of a light-roast single origin (Ethiopia, Kenya and Costa Rica sit alongside Đà Lạt on the bean list), then walk round Trúc Bạch Lake; Duy Trí's yogurt coffee on Yên Phụ is close by. It closes at 6pm.
  - Hours: 8:00–18:00 daily (Kafeville website)
  - Address: 23 Yên Ninh, Trúc Bạch (also 101-B3 Trần Huy Liệu, Giảng Võ)
  - Price: Retail beans about 60–170k VND per 100g (Vietcetera)
  - [Google Maps](https://www.google.com/maps/search/?api=1&query=Kafeville%2C%2023%20Y%C3%AAn%20Ninh%2C%20Ba%20%C4%90%C3%ACnh%2C%20Hanoi) · [Source](https://www.kafeville.com/about)

#### Hidden & view cafés

- **★ Café Phố Cổ** · Old Quarter, by Hoàn Kiếm Lake (Hoàn Kiếm) · $$ · *checked 2026*
  - You walk through a silk shop and down a lantern-lit passage into a century-old family house with a courtyard of antiques, songbirds and an ancestral altar. Narrow stairs then climb to a rooftop that looks across Hoàn Kiếm Lake. This is the classic Hanoi café behind a secret staircase.
  - Tip: Head straight for the top floor and order egg coffee or a phin. Arrive before 9am or in the hour before sunset for a terrace seat and the best light. The foam here is a little sweeter than Giảng's.
  - Hours: 8:00–23:00 daily
  - Address: 11 Hàng Gai (enter through the silk shop)
  - Price: Egg coffee about 40–55k VND (2026 guides)
  - [Google Maps](https://www.google.com/maps/search/?api=1&query=Cafe%20Pho%20Co%2C%2011%20H%C3%A0ng%20Gai%2C%20Ho%C3%A0n%20Ki%E1%BA%BFm%2C%20Hanoi) · [Source](https://vietnamnews.vn/sunday/restaurant-review/1535192/ha-noi-s-tranquil-hidden-cafes-with-excellent-coffee.html)
- **Loading T Café** · St Joseph's Cathedral area (Hoàn Kiếm) · $$ · *checked 2026*
  - An old staircase climbs into an early-1930s villa that was built for one family and later split into 16 homes, and it keeps its geometric floor tiles, bare brick and olive-green shutters. The egg coffee is lighter and less sweet, with a hint of cinnamon, and Food & Wine (Apr 2026) singled out both it and the yogurt coffee.
  - Tip: Look for the small 'Loading T' sign and take the stairs on the left of the building. Go on a weekday morning to beat the queues and ask for a window seat over the street. The website says 9:00–22:00 but some listings show an 18:00 close, so don't leave it late. If it's full, Blackbird Coffee is across the street at 5 Chân Cầm.
  - Hours: 9:00–22:00 daily per café website (some listings show 8:00–18:00)
  - Address: 2nd floor, 8 Chân Cầm
  - Price: Egg coffee about 45–50k VND (Mar 2026 guide)
  - [Google Maps](https://www.google.com/maps/search/?api=1&query=Loading%20T%20Cafe%2C%208%20Ch%C3%A2n%20C%E1%BA%A7m%2C%20Ho%C3%A0n%20Ki%E1%BA%BFm%2C%20Hanoi) · [Source](https://loadingtcafe.com)
- **Hidden Gem Coffee** · Old Quarter (Hoàn Kiếm) · *checked 2025*
  - Owner Nguyễn Văn Thơ, a former motorbike-tour guide, built this multi-storey hideaway almost entirely from salvaged glass, ceramics, tyres and motorbike parts, and it stays surprisingly quiet in the middle of the Old Quarter. King Philippe and Queen Mathilde of Belgium dropped in for egg coffee on 1 April 2025.
  - Tip: Push your bike down the alley with the engine off, as the sign asks. Order at the counter upstairs, then climb to the rooftop for glimpses of Long Biên Bridge. Bring empty plastic bottles, because the café gives a discount or buys them back.
  - Hours: About 8:00–23:00 (older listing; check Maps)
  - Address: End of alley 3B Hàng Tre
  - [Google Maps](https://www.google.com/maps/search/?api=1&query=Hidden%20Gem%20Coffee%2C%203B%20H%C3%A0ng%20Tre%2C%20Ho%C3%A0n%20Ki%E1%BA%BFm%2C%20Hanoi) · [Source](https://saigoneer.com/hanoi-street-food-restaurants/24981-ngõ-nooks-hidden-gem,-the-cafe-that-recycled-knick-knacks-built)
- **Serein Café & Lounge** · Long Biên Bridge, north edge of the Old Quarter (Hoàn Kiếm) · $$ · *likely open*
  - Up a spiral staircase in the old railway housing beside Long Biên station, Serein's top floors look straight onto the steel spans of the French-built Long Biên Bridge, which opened in 1903. It's the best bridge view you'll get from a café seat in Hanoi.
  - Tip: Come for the view, not the coffee: reviewers find the drinks average and the food pricey. Late afternoon gives golden light on the bridge; afterwards, walk out onto it (it reopened in May 2026 after repairs). Trains cross less often since the February 2026 service cut.
  - Hours: 8:00–23:00 (Sep 2026 listing)
  - Address: 16 Tập thể Ga Long Biên (Long Biên station housing, off Trần Nhật Duật)
  - Price: Drinks about 35–60k VND (2025 listing); food pricier
  - [Google Maps](https://www.google.com/maps/search/?api=1&query=Serein%20Cafe%20%26%20Lounge%2C%2016%20T%E1%BA%ADp%20th%E1%BB%83%20Ga%20Long%20Bi%C3%AAn%2C%20Ho%C3%A0n%20Ki%E1%BA%BFm%2C%20Hanoi) · [Source](https://wanderlog.com/place/details/1254304/serein-cafe--lounge)
- **Tamai** · Quán Sứ, French Quarter edge (Hoàn Kiếm) · $$ · *checked 2026*
  - This small, nostalgic café in an old French colonial house has turned its back on Instagram. Since February 2026 it has banned commercial shoots, pro cameras, outfit changes and table-hopping for photos, which makes it calmer than most of Hanoi's photogenic cafés.
  - Tip: Come to talk or read. Quick phone photos are fine, but don't film other guests. The room is small, so skip weekend afternoons.
  - Address: 84 Quán Sứ
  - Price: Drinks 40–80k VND (Apr 2026)
  - [Google Maps](https://www.google.com/maps/search/?api=1&query=Tamai%2C%2084%20Qu%C3%A1n%20S%E1%BB%A9%2C%20Ho%C3%A0n%20Ki%E1%BA%BFm%2C%20Hanoi) · [Source](https://znews.vn/chung-toi-khong-phai-quan-ca-phe-chup-anh-post1624907.html)
- **Miên Man Café** · St Joseph's Cathedral (Hoàn Kiếm) · $$ · *checked 2026*
  - Four floors, each with its own mood, sit right behind St Joseph's Cathedral, and the balcony looks straight at its neo-Gothic towers. Drinks lean creative, with house-made syrups and Hanoi twists such as green-rice (cốm) cream coffee.
  - Tip: Book a weekend balcony seat through the hotline (+84 834 22 6868) or Instagram (@mienman.official). Try the cà phê kem cốm or the salted-cream coffee. Moon Men, a small cocktail bar on the 5th floor, opens from 3:30pm.
  - Hours: 8:00–23:30 daily
  - Address: 3 Ấu Triệu (floors 1–4)
  - Price: Coffee 55–70k VND, signature drinks 60–75k (official menu, Sep 2026)
  - [Google Maps](https://www.google.com/maps/search/?api=1&query=Mien%20Man%20Cafe%2C%203%20%E1%BA%A4u%20Tri%E1%BB%87u%2C%20Ho%C3%A0n%20Ki%E1%BA%BFm%2C%20Hanoi) · [Source](https://mienmancafe.vn/)
- **Hỏa Xa Café (Long Biên Station)** (Hỏa Xa Cafe) · Long Biên Station, north edge of the Old Quarter (Hoàn Kiếm) · $ · *likely open*
  - In 2023 Vietnam Railways turned a former waiting room of the French-built Long Biên Station (1902) into a café. Four big arched doors open onto the tracks about 2 m away, behind a sturdy fence, so you can sip a coffee as trains pull in. It's Hanoi's coffee-and-trains moment done legally and safely, without Train Street's crush.
  - Tip: Come in the late afternoon, then walk out onto Long Biên Bridge's pedestrian lane. Trains have run less often since late February 2026, so ask staff when the next one is due. Keep the order simple: phin coffee, cold brew, or fresh orange, pineapple or guava juice. Serein (in the guide), up in the station housing nearby, has the better bridge view.
  - Hours: Daily 7:00–23:00 (mia.vn, Apr 2026)
  - Address: Long Biên Station (Ga Long Biên), Trần Nhật Duật, Hoàn Kiếm, Hanoi
  - Price: Drinks 35,000–60,000 VND (mia.vn, Apr 2026)
  - [Google Maps](https://www.google.com/maps/search/?api=1&query=H%E1%BB%8Fa%20Xa%20Cafe%2C%20Ga%20Long%20Bi%C3%AAn%2C%20Tr%E1%BA%A7n%20Nh%E1%BA%ADt%20Du%E1%BA%ADt%2C%20Ho%C3%A0n%20Ki%E1%BA%BFm%2C%20Hanoi) · [Source](https://mia.vn/cam-nang-du-lich/hoa-xa-cafe-ha-noi-20263)

#### After midnight

- **Puku Café & Sports Bar** · Tống Duy Tân food street, Old Quarter west edge (Hoàn Kiếm) · $$ · *likely open*
  - This long-running, round-the-clock café-bar is where Hanoi's night owls land once the bars have pulled their shutters down. It's big and comfortable, with quiet corners, and serves coffee, smoothies, beer and cocktails alongside burgers, salads, Vietnamese and vegetarian dishes at any hour, with live sport on the screens.
  - Tip: Use it for a 3am meal, a sober-up coffee before the Grab home, or an overnight match. Nê Cocktail Bar (in the guide) is on the same street, and Mytour also lists Xofa Café & Bistro (No. 14) and Aroi Dessert Café (No. 9) on Tống Duy Tân as open 24 hours.
  - Hours: 24/7 (Mytour 24-hour café list, updated 15 Sep 2026)
  - Address: 16–18 Tống Duy Tân, Hoàn Kiếm, Hanoi
  - Price: Menu about 50,000–220,000 VND, food included (Mytour, Sep 2026)
  - [Google Maps](https://www.google.com/maps/search/?api=1&query=Puku%20Cafe%20%26%20Sports%20Bar%2C%2016%20T%E1%BB%91ng%20Duy%20T%C3%A2n%2C%20Ho%C3%A0n%20Ki%E1%BA%BFm%2C%20Hanoi) · [Source](https://mytour.vn/vi/blog/bai-viet/kham-pha-8-quan-cafe-mo-xuyen-dem-tai-ha-noi-voi-digifood.html)

### Hanoi: Bars (Quán bar)

Hanoi drinks at two speeds. On the street, Hanoians sink same-day bia hơi at about 10,000–15,000 đồng a glass on plastic stools. Up the stairs and down hidden alleys, a cocktail scene built on Vietnamese ingredients now has two bars on Asia's 50 Best Bars 2026: Workshop14 at #34 and The Hudson Rooms at #46. Add colonial-era hotel bars with a century of stories, sunset terraces over West Lake and Hoàn Kiếm Lake, and a new wave of natural-wine bars, and every budget is covered.

#### Cocktail bars & speakeasies

- **★ Workshop14** · West Lake (Tây Hồ) · $$$ · *checked 2026*
  - Vietnam's top-ranked bar, opened in early 2025, is #34 on Asia's 50 Best Bars 2026 and won both the Best New Opening and Best Bar in Vietnam awards. In a calm, wood-clad former architect's studio by West Lake, Rich McDonough (ex-69 Colebrooke Row) uses vacuum distillation and clarification on Vietnamese produce.
  - Tip: Book ahead (+84 934 446 669 or Instagram), allow time, and ask for the veranda. Try Can't Stand the Heat, a margarita made with vacuum-redistilled Vietnamese chilli that keeps the flavour but drops the burn, or Terry's Chocolate Orange (cognac infused with Marou cacao nibs). Start with a golden-hour drink at the InterContinental's Sunset Bar, a short walk along Từ Hoa.
  - Hours: Daily, evenings (exact times not published)
  - Address: 6 Ngõ 5 Từ Hoa, Tây Hồ, Hanoi
  - Price: Average cocktail about US$12 (roughly 300k VND), per Asia's 50 Best
  - [Google Maps](https://www.google.com/maps/search/?api=1&query=Workshop14%2C%206%20Ng%C3%B5%205%20T%E1%BB%AB%20Hoa%2C%20T%C3%A2y%20H%E1%BB%93%2C%20Hanoi) · [Source](https://www.the50.com/bars/best-in-asia/awards/best-new-opening.html)
- **★ The Haflington** · Old Quarter (Hoàn Kiếm) · $$$ · *checked 2026*
  - The bar that put Hanoi on the cocktail map: in 2024 it became the first Vietnamese bar in Asia's 50 Best Bars top 50 (#47). A narrow Old Quarter corridor leads into a two-floor 'natural history museum' of fossils, old books and a dinosaur skeleton hanging over the bar.
  - Tip: Book weekend evenings well ahead, because tables are very hard to get; staff meet you on Hàng Mã and walk you in. Try the Rabbit's Habit (mezcal, Green Chartreuse, carrot, cinnamon) or the Coin Flipper (rye, Fernet-Branca, chocolate bitters). The crowd is now mostly tourists. It fell to #87 in 2025 and is not on the 2026 list.
  - Hours: Daily from late afternoon or evening until about 1am (listings differ on opening: 3pm or 6pm)
  - Address: 94 Hàng Mã, Hoàn Kiếm, Hanoi
  - Price: Cocktails 210–380k VND, VAT and service included (2026 local reviews)
  - [Google Maps](https://www.google.com/maps/search/?api=1&query=The%20Haflington%2C%2094%20H%C3%A0ng%20M%C3%A3%2C%20Ho%C3%A0n%20Ki%E1%BA%BFm%2C%20Hanoi) · [Source](https://amthuchiendai.vn/en/haflington-hanois-best-known-cocktail-bar/)
- **Nê Cocktail Bar** (Nê.Cocktailbar) · Old Quarter, west edge (Hoàn Kiếm) · $$$ · *checked 2026*
  - Home of the Phở Cocktail, which Phạm Tiến Tiệp, Vietnam's first World Class bartender champion (2012), created at the Metropole before opening this snug, speakeasy-style room in 2017. Gin and phở spices (cinnamon, star anise, cardamom) are set alight and poured through tiers of cups: the drink that launched Hanoi's Vietnamese-ingredient cocktail wave.
  - Tip: Order the Phở Cocktail first, then ask which street-food-inspired drinks are on. The sister bar Nê Boong-ke (12 Cửa Đông) pours the same signatures in a bigger room. The Tống Duy Tân food street outside serves grills and hotpot late.
  - Hours: Nightly about 7:30pm–2am (2026 listing)
  - Address: 3B Tống Duy Tân, Hoàn Kiếm, Hanoi
  - Price: Cocktails about 220–450k VND (Mar 2026 listing)
  - [Google Maps](https://www.google.com/maps/search/?api=1&query=N%C3%AA%20Cocktail%20Bar%2C%203B%20T%E1%BB%91ng%20Duy%20T%C3%A2n%2C%20Ho%C3%A0n%20Ki%E1%BA%BFm%2C%20Hanoi) · [Source](https://amthuchiendai.vn/en/hanois-best-cocktail-bars-recommendations-from-a-local/)
- **USEbar** · Hai Bà Trưng (just south of the French Quarter) · $$$ · *checked 2026*
  - Founded by Nguyễn Tuấn Anh, Diageo World Class Vietnam Bartender of the Year 2023, this low-waste bar turns local leftovers into precise, playful cocktails, served around a glowing bar in a minimalist room. It won Best New Bar and Sustainable Bar at the 2023 B.A.R. Awards.
  - Tip: Order the much-talked-about Ratatouille (made with mozzarella and parmesan), the Chocovardier (a Boulevardier with Vietnamese cacao) or the Tropical Old Fashioned with caramelised pineapple. Menu prices already include VAT and service.
  - Hours: Daily 6pm–12:30am
  - Address: 55 Ngõ Huế, Hai Bà Trưng, Hanoi
  - Price: Cocktails 280–380k VND incl. VAT and service
  - [Google Maps](https://www.google.com/maps/search/?api=1&query=USEbar%2C%2055%20Ng%C3%B5%20Hu%E1%BA%BF%2C%20Hai%20B%C3%A0%20Tr%C6%B0ng%2C%20Hanoi) · [Source](https://amthuchiendai.vn/en/usebar-modern-style-cocktails-in-hanoi/)
- **NỌT Cocktail Bar** (NỌT) · French Quarter, Hạ Hồi lanes (Hoàn Kiếm) · *checked 2025*
  - An insider favourite whose name drops the 'g' from ngọt (sweet), because the drinks are dry and savoury, built on ingredients from Vietnam's provinces such as mắc khén mountain pepper and fermented jackfruit. A friendly team serves them, with food matched to the drinks, in an intimate shophouse room.
  - Tip: Ask the bartenders to walk you through the regional ingredients and order whatever features mắc khén or jackfruit. Come early for the food, when the kitchen is at its busiest. The room is small, so arrive early or message ahead.
  - Hours: Daily 6pm–2am (Sep 2026 listing)
  - Address: 34 Ngõ Xóm Hạ Hồi, Hoàn Kiếm, Hanoi
  - [Google Maps](https://www.google.com/maps/search/?api=1&query=N%E1%BB%8CT%20cocktail%20bar%2C%2034%20Ng%C3%B5%20X%C3%B3m%20H%E1%BA%A1%20H%E1%BB%93i%2C%20Ho%C3%A0n%20Ki%E1%BA%BFm%2C%20Hanoi) · [Source](https://amthuchiendai.vn/en/hanois-best-cocktail-bars-recommendations-from-a-local/)
- **Polite & Co** · West side of Hoàn Kiếm Lake (Hoàn Kiếm) · $$$ · *checked 2025*
  - Billed as Hanoi's oldest cocktail bar (since 1995), this is a small 1920s London-style gentleman's lounge for whisky, cigars and well-made forgotten classics. It sits a minute from Hoàn Kiếm Lake, and the menu changes about twice a year.
  - Tip: Go at happy hour (5–8pm), when signature cocktails are 150k VND or buy one get one. It's an easy stop before or after dinner near the lake and St Joseph's Cathedral.
  - Hours: Daily about 4pm–midnight (2026 listing)
  - Address: 5B Ngõ Bảo Khánh, Hoàn Kiếm, Hanoi
  - Price: About 200–500k VND (Mar 2026 listing); signatures 150k or 2-for-1 at the 5–8pm happy hour
  - [Google Maps](https://www.google.com/maps/search/?api=1&query=Polite%20%26%20Co%2C%205B%20Ng%C3%B5%20B%E1%BA%A3o%20Kh%C3%A1nh%2C%20Ho%C3%A0n%20Ki%E1%BA%BFm%2C%20Hanoi) · [Source](https://amthuchiendai.vn/en/hanois-best-cocktail-bars-recommendations-from-a-local/)

#### Hotel bars & rooftops

- **★ The Hudson Rooms (Capella Hanoi)** · French Quarter (Hoàn Kiếm), by the Opera House · $$$$ · *checked 2026*
  - An Art Deco rooftop modelled on 1920s New York's Grand Central, ranked #46 on Asia's 50 Best Bars 2026 (up from #86 in 2025). It serves oysters and caviar by day, then at night an 'Express Line' cocktail menu laid out as four New York subway lines, led by World Class Vietnam 2025 winner Nguyễn Ngọc Khánh (Patrick).
  - Tip: Come for happy hour (5:30–7:30pm daily, buy one get one per person) and stay for the nightly 9:30pm red-carpet ritual. Order the Park Avenue (chestnut-infused gin, white port, house mandarin liqueur). Ask about Track 61, a tiny speakeasy inside the bar whose secret entrance you have to work out.
  - Music: Jazz-age theme. Its Asia's 50 Best profile mentions live jazz, but no nights are published. Red-carpet show nightly at 9:30pm.
  - Hours: Mon–Thu 5:30pm–midnight; Fri–Sun from about noon (oyster bar by day) to midnight (2026 listings)
  - Address: Top floor, Capella Hanoi, 11 Lê Phụng Hiểu, Hoàn Kiếm, Hanoi
  - Price: Average cocktail about US$15 (roughly 390k VND) per Asia's 50 Best; buy one get one 5:30–7:30pm
  - [Google Maps](https://www.google.com/maps/search/?api=1&query=The%20Hudson%20Rooms%2C%20Capella%20Hanoi%2C%2011%20L%C3%AA%20Ph%E1%BB%A5ng%20Hi%E1%BB%83u%2C%20Ho%C3%A0n%20Ki%E1%BA%BFm%2C%20Hanoi) · [Source](https://en.vietnamplus.vn/a-sun-group-hotels-bar-makes-asias-50-best-bars-post349145.vnp)
- **★ Bamboo Bar (Sofitel Legend Metropole)** · French Quarter (Hoàn Kiếm) · $$$$ · *checked 2026*
  - The thatched poolside bar of Hanoi's 1901 grand hotel sits directly above the Metropole's wartime bomb shelter, where guests including Joan Baez took cover during the December 1972 bombing. History is on the menu too: the Graham Greene cocktail was created by a Metropole bartender in 1951.
  - Tip: Order The Graham Greene (gin, dry vermouth, crème de mûre) or the Tonkin Express egg-coffee martini. For a cheaper round in the same hotel, angelina's 'angelina hour' (daily 4–7pm) brings a free second drink with every order, and Le Club next door has evening jazz. The bunker tours are for hotel guests only. 2026 is the hotel's 125th anniversary.
  - Hours: Daily 6am–11pm (food until 10:30pm)
  - Address: Sofitel Legend Metropole Hanoi, 15 Ngô Quyền, Hoàn Kiếm, Hanoi
  - Price: Metropole signature cocktails 350–380k VND plus 5% service and VAT (May 2026 menu); angelina cocktails 350k, second drink free 4–7pm (Sep 2026 menu)
  - [Google Maps](https://www.google.com/maps/search/?api=1&query=Bamboo%20Bar%2C%20Sofitel%20Legend%20Metropole%2C%2015%20Ng%C3%B4%20Quy%E1%BB%81n%2C%20Ho%C3%A0n%20Ki%E1%BA%BFm%2C%20Hanoi) · [Source](https://www.sofitel-legend-metropole-hanoi.com/dining/bamboo-bar/)
- **The Summit (Pan Pacific Hanoi)** · Trúc Bạch / West Lake (Ba Đình) · $$$ · *checked 2026*
  - A 20th-floor open-air terrace looking over both West Lake and Trúc Bạch Lake, with the Red River beyond. It is Hanoi's classic sunset drink, at prices well below the French Quarter's grand-hotel bars.
  - Tip: Arrive about 45 minutes before sunset (roughly 5:15–6pm from October to February) for a lake-facing seat, and order The Summit Lemongrass Martini. It opens at 2pm, so it also works for an afternoon drink. Smart casual.
  - Hours: Daily 2pm–midnight (last order 11:30pm)
  - Address: 20th floor, Pan Pacific Hanoi, 1 Thanh Niên, Ba Đình, Hanoi
  - Price: Signature cocktails 220k VND plus 5% service and 10% VAT (July 2026 menu)
  - [Google Maps](https://www.google.com/maps/search/?api=1&query=The%20Summit%2C%20Pan%20Pacific%20Hanoi%2C%201%20Thanh%20Ni%C3%AAn%2C%20Ba%20%C4%90%C3%ACnh%2C%20Hanoi) · [Source](https://www.panpacific.com/en/hotels-and-resorts/pp-hanoi/dining/the-summit.html)
- **Terraço Sky Bar** · Hoàn Kiếm Lake (Hoàn Kiếm) · $$$ · *checked 2026*
  - The best mid-priced view straight over Hoàn Kiếm Lake and Ngọc Sơn Temple. The signature cocktails are named after Hanoi's districts, and there is music on Friday and Saturday nights.
  - Tip: Happy hour (5–7pm, 30% off cocktails, beer and mixed drinks) lines up with sunset over the lake. Try the 'Ba Đình' (sweet-sour gin) or the richer, whisky-based 'Đống Đa'. Book a table on the website for weekend sunsets.
  - Music: DJ Shinz every Friday; live music every Saturday 8:30–10:30pm (weather permitting)
  - Hours: Daily 5pm–midnight
  - Address: 8th floor, La Sinfonía del Rey Hotel & Spa, 33–35 Hàng Dầu, Hoàn Kiếm, Hanoi
  - Price: 30% off cocktails, beer and mixed drinks 5–7pm
  - [Google Maps](https://www.google.com/maps/search/?api=1&query=Terra%C3%A7o%20Sky%20Bar%2C%20La%20Sinfon%C3%ADa%20del%20Rey%20Hotel%2C%2033%20H%C3%A0ng%20D%E1%BA%A7u%2C%20Ho%C3%A0n%20Ki%E1%BA%BFm%2C%20Hanoi) · [Source](https://lasinfoniavietnam.com/terracobar/)
- **Sunset Bar (InterContinental Hanoi Westlake)** · West Lake (Tây Hồ) · $$$$ · *checked 2026*
  - An overwater bar set among three pavilions out on West Lake and reached by a torchlit bridge, built for watching the sun go down over the water.
  - Tip: Golden hour (6–8pm daily) is buy one get one on the 'Làng' cocktail collection. Wine fans can book VinoVibes, a tasting on Friday 16 Oct 2026 from 7 to 9pm (980k VND, 900k early bird). Workshop14 is a short walk away for the next round.
  - Hours: Weekdays 4pm–midnight; Sat, Sun and public holidays 3pm–midnight
  - Address: InterContinental Hanoi Westlake, 5 Từ Hoa, Tây Hồ, Hanoi
  - Price: Cocktails from 290k VND++; 2-for-1 on 'Làng' cocktails 6–8pm
  - [Google Maps](https://www.google.com/maps/search/?api=1&query=Sunset%20Bar%2C%20InterContinental%20Hanoi%20Westlake%2C%205%20T%E1%BB%AB%20Hoa%2C%20T%C3%A2y%20H%E1%BB%93%2C%20Hanoi) · [Source](https://hanoi.intercontinental.com/dining/sunset-bar)

#### Beer & bia hơi

- **★ Tạ Hiện Beer Street & Bia Hơi Corner** (Phố Tạ Hiện / ngã tư bia hơi Tạ Hiện – Lương Ngọc Quyến) · Old Quarter (Hoàn Kiếm) · $ · *checked 2026*
  - Hanoi's beer street: plastic stools spill across the junction, same-day bia hơi costs about 10,000–15,000 VND a glass, and by 8pm it is a roaring street party that runs past midnight. It's loud and mostly tourists, but you should do it once.
  - Tip: Arrive around 6–7pm to get stools. Thursday brings a more local crowd, Friday and Saturday are a crush, and Sunday and Monday are quiet. Stick to bia hơi with snacks like nem chua rán (fried fermented pork) and dried squid, and ask prices before ordering seafood, as there have been bill scams. Police walk through around 11:45pm; bars pull their shutters halfway down and keep serving.
  - Hours: From late afternoon; packed about 8–11:30pm; police sweep around 11:45pm, then lock-ins
  - Address: Junction of Tạ Hiện and Lương Ngọc Quyến, Old Quarter, Hoàn Kiếm, Hanoi
  - Price: Bia hơi about 10,000–15,000 VND a glass; cash, pay when you leave
  - [Google Maps](https://www.google.com/maps/search/?api=1&query=T%E1%BA%A1%20Hi%E1%BB%87n%20Beer%20Street%2C%20L%C6%B0%C6%A1ng%20Ng%E1%BB%8Dc%20Quy%E1%BA%BFn%2C%20Ho%C3%A0n%20Ki%E1%BA%BFm%2C%20Hanoi) · [Source](https://www.vietnamtourism.com/en/hanoi-nightlife-ta-hien-bia-hoi-corners-and-the-midnight-closing-rules-explained)
- **Standing Bar** · Trúc Bạch (Ba Đình) · $$ · *checked 2026*
  - Hanoi's craft-beer free house pours 19 rotating taps from breweries across Vietnam, plus Vietnamese craft spirits, ciders and meads, with tapas, grilled skewers and sunset views over Trúc Bạch Lake. Its free Friday vinyl night has passed 250 editions.
  - Tip: Take a lakeside seat around sunset and ask for the newest Vietnamese releases. On Fridays, arrive around 8pm for a terrace spot at Strictly Vinyl. Coming up: an afrobeat 'FELAbration' with the Hanoi Vinyl Collective on 16 Oct 2026 and a 90s-vinyl night on 23 Oct. Check the bar's Facebook events page for monthly stand-up comedy.
  - Music: Strictly Vinyl every Friday, 8pm to about 11:45pm, free entry: vinyl-only DJs playing funk, soul, disco, afrobeat and hip-hop. Also occasional touring bands and a monthly international stand-up comedy night.
  - Hours: Mon–Fri 3pm–midnight; Sat–Sun noon–midnight
  - Address: 170 Trấn Vũ, Trúc Bạch, Ba Đình, Hanoi
  - Price: Vinyl nights are free entry
  - [Google Maps](https://www.google.com/maps/search/?api=1&query=Standing%20Bar%2C%20170%20Tr%E1%BA%A5n%20V%C5%A9%2C%20Tr%C3%BAc%20B%E1%BA%A1ch%2C%20Ba%20%C4%90%C3%ACnh%2C%20Hanoi) · [Source](https://standingbarhanoi.com/)
- **Bia Hơi Lan Chín** · Mỹ Đình (Nam Từ Liêm), west Hanoi · $ · *likely open*
  - A busy Old Quarter bia hơi restaurant with pavement seats, where locals and travellers drink fresh draught over roast duck, grilled pigeon and beef with pickled greens. It's a few minutes' walk from Tạ Hiện but far calmer.
  - Tip: Go during the after-work rush (about 4–7pm), when the beer is freshest. Share lạc rang (roasted peanuts), đậu rán (fried tofu) or vịt quay (roast duck).
  - Hours: Daily from about 8–9am to 11pm (2025–26 listings)
  - Address: 16 Lê Đức Thọ, Mỹ Đình 2, Nam Từ Liêm, Hanoi (now its only branch; the 22 Hàng Tre Old Quarter branch has closed)
  - Price: Draught beer from about 20k VND a glass; dishes about 55–250k VND (mia.vn, Jun 2026)
  - [Google Maps](https://www.google.com/maps/search/?api=1&query=Bia%20H%C6%A1i%20Lan%20Ch%C3%ADn%2C%2016%20L%C3%AA%20%C4%90%E1%BB%A9c%20Th%E1%BB%8D%2C%20Nam%20T%E1%BB%AB%20Li%C3%AAm%2C%20Hanoi) · [Source](https://mia.vn/cam-nang-du-lich/quan-bia-hoi-ha-noi-15373)

#### Wine bars

- **Vin Hơi** · Ba Đình (Hàng Bún, north of the Old Quarter) · $$$ · *checked 2026*
  - The name riffs on bia hơi, and so does the concept: mostly natural wine poured nhậu-style with shared plates, down to plastic Hanoi chairs and tables on the top balcony. It is run by Léa and Hải Trần, a French-Vietnamese chef couple who met in Paris; Hải was sous-chef at a Michelin-starred restaurant in France.
  - Tip: Grab a stool on the top balcony and let staff steer you through the natural list. The menu changes regularly; pâté en croûte, steak with aligot and baked Alaska stood out in January 2026. Closed Mondays.
  - Hours: Tue–Sun 6–11pm; closed Mon
  - Address: 38 Hàng Bún, Ba Đình, Hanoi
  - Price: Natural wine 140–250k VND a glass, bottles from about 1M VND; plates 60–250k VND
  - [Google Maps](https://www.google.com/maps/search/?api=1&query=Vin%20H%C6%A1i%2C%2038%20H%C3%A0ng%20B%C3%BAn%2C%20Ba%20%C4%90%C3%ACnh%2C%20Hanoi) · [Source](https://vietnamnews.vn/sunday/restaurant-review/1733017/a-love-letter-in-butter-and-wine.html)
- **THỞ Wine Space** · Ba Đình (Đội Cấn street, Ngọc Hà ward) · *checked 2026*
  - A wine shop and bar with a large balcony, outside the tourist zone on a street known for nem lụi and bánh xèo. Its by-the-glass list takes real risks (recently a Moldovan wine and a Western Australian natural wine), and the staff explain why each one is there.
  - Tip: Eat street food on Đội Cấn first, then ask what's open by the glass. The small plates go well beyond cheese boards and change with the seasons (the summer 2026 menu was called 'NỰC'). Monthly tastings are held here.
  - Hours: Bar Mon–Fri 6–11:30pm, Sat 6pm–midnight, closed Sun (shop Mon–Sat 9am–11:30pm)
  - Address: 207 Đội Cấn, Ngọc Hà, Hanoi
  - [Google Maps](https://www.google.com/maps/search/?api=1&query=TH%E1%BB%9E%20Wine%20Space%2C%20207%20%C4%90%E1%BB%99i%20C%E1%BA%A5n%2C%20Ba%20%C4%90%C3%ACnh%2C%20Hanoi) · [Source](https://thowinespace.com/)

#### After midnight

- **Moon Men Cocktail Bar** · St Joseph's Cathedral (Hoàn Kiếm) · $$$ · *checked 2026*
  - A small top-floor cocktail bar whose glass front and balcony look straight at the floodlit towers of St Joseph's Cathedral. It's one of the few serious cocktail rooms in central Hanoi that keeps pouring past midnight: until 1am Monday to Thursday and 2am Friday to Sunday. The list mixes classics with house-infused signatures under Vietnamese names such as Khói Lạc and Pia Mộng, plus a Ginseng Manhattan and mocktails.
  - Tip: Book a balcony table for weekend nights (hotline +84 834 22 6868, WhatsApp +84 967 941 880 or Instagram @moonmenbar.official), or come before 8pm, when drinks are 150k. It's in the same building as Miên Man Café (in the guide), so you can have coffee downstairs by day and cocktails upstairs at night. Cash, card and QR payments are accepted.
  - Hours: Mon–Thu 15:30–01:00; Fri–Sun 15:30–02:00; happy hour until 20:00 (official site, Oct 2026)
  - Address: 4th floor, 3 Ấu Triệu, Hoàn Kiếm, Hanoi (enter through the Miên+ doorway; the bar's site says to take the lift to floor 5)
  - Price: Signature cocktails 239k, mocktails 180k, happy-hour drinks 150k VND (official site); 8% service charge (Wikivoyage, Jun 2026)
  - [Google Maps](https://www.google.com/maps/search/?api=1&query=Moon%20Men%20Cocktail%20Bar%2C%203%20%E1%BA%A4u%20Tri%E1%BB%87u%2C%20Ho%C3%A0n%20Ki%E1%BA%BFm%2C%20Hanoi) · [Source](https://moonmenofficial.com/)

#### Local favourites

- **Bia Hơi Cường Hói** · West Lake, west shore (Trích Sài, Tây Hồ) · $ · *likely open*
  - A no-frills bia hơi right on the west shore of West Lake, where Hanoians drink cold, foamy draught in the lake breeze with views across the water. It's the cheap, local alternative to the lake's hotel terraces, and a world away from the expat bars of Xuân Diệu across the lake.
  - Tip: Come from late afternoon, when the after-work crowd arrives, and take a lake-facing table. Order nem Phùng (fermented pork from Phùng town) and chả cá rán (fried fish cake). Book at weekends and on big-match days (024 3728 2621). It closes at 10pm; for a coffee beforehand, Cộng Cà Phê's West Lake branch (in the guide) is along the same lakeside road at No. 75.
  - Hours: Daily 10:00–22:00 (mia.vn, Jun 2026; Mytour, Sep 2026)
  - Address: 1 Trích Sài, Tây Hồ, Hanoi
  - Price: No prices published; listings describe the draught as cheap
  - [Google Maps](https://www.google.com/maps/search/?api=1&query=Bia%20h%C6%A1i%20C%C6%B0%E1%BB%9Dng%20H%C3%B3i%2C%201%20Tr%C3%ADch%20S%C3%A0i%2C%20T%C3%A2y%20H%E1%BB%93%2C%20Hanoi) · [Source](https://mia.vn/cam-nang-du-lich/quan-bia-hoi-ha-noi-15373)
- **Bia Hơi Vân Hồ** (Quán bia hơi Hà Nội Vân Hồ) · Hai Bà Trưng (Vân Hồ, Lê Đại Hành area) · $ · *likely open*
  - A neighbourhood bia hơi that has poured Hanoi draught since June 1991, in a leafy space under climbing vines. Regulars come for cold, clean-tasting beer and a long list of drinking food, from salt-roasted chicken and duck to the house cá chìa vôi, a small river fish crisp-fried to the house recipe. It's everyday Hanoi drinking culture, far from the Old Quarter beer corners.
  - Tip: Go from late afternoon, bia hơi's rush hour, and order the cá chìa vôi with đậu mắm tôm (tofu with shrimp paste) and lạc rang (roasted peanuts). Search 'Bia hơi Vân Hồ, 2B Hoa Lư' rather than an old ward name. USEbar (in the guide) is a short Grab ride away for a cocktail afterwards.
  - Hours: About 8:00–22:30 (Mytour, Sep 2026); mia.vn (Jun 2026) gives 9:00–23:00
  - Address: 2B Hoa Lư, Hai Bà Trưng, Hanoi
  - Price: About 50,000–165,000 VND per person with snacks (Mytour, Sep 2026)
  - [Google Maps](https://www.google.com/maps/search/?api=1&query=Bia%20h%C6%A1i%20V%C3%A2n%20H%E1%BB%93%2C%202B%20Hoa%20L%C6%B0%2C%20Hai%20B%C3%A0%20Tr%C6%B0ng%2C%20Hanoi) · [Source](https://mia.vn/cam-nang-du-lich/quan-bia-hoi-ha-noi-15373)

### Hanoi: Music (Âm nhạc)

Hanoi is Vietnam's jazz capital and the heartland of northern folk music: within a short walk of Hoàn Kiếm Lake you can hear Quyền Văn Minh's saxophone in the country's first jazz club, young players in hidden listening rooms, and centuries-old ca trù and water-puppet orchestras. After dark, the underground has regrouped around a few clubs since Savage and Hanoi Rock City closed, while phòng trà and Trịnh Công Sơn cafés keep Vietnamese song alive. With the colonial Opera House shut for restoration, the big classical nights have moved to the new Hồ Gươm Opera.

#### Jazz

- **★ Binh Minh Jazz Club** · French Quarter (Hoàn Kiếm), behind the Opera House · $$ · *checked 2026*
  - Saxophonist Quyền Văn Minh, the godfather of Vietnamese jazz, opened Vietnam's first jazz club in 1997, and his intimate room behind the Opera House is still the city's jazz home: nightly bands play standards, Latin jazz, funk and Vietnamese songs rearranged as jazz. In September 2026 it hosted the Hanoi Jazztival's late-night jam sessions.
  - Tip: Book by phone (094 598 6336 / 024 3933 6555) or Facebook Messenger and arrive before 21:00 for a table near the small stage. Budget a cover of 100–150k VND per guest on top of drinks. Some older guides say 2 Tràng Tiền; the club is in the lane behind the Opera House. On Sundays, catch Minh's free afternoon set by the lake first.
  - Music: Live jazz every night 21:00–23:15 with local players and visiting international musicians: standards, Latin jazz, funk, blues and jazz arrangements of Vietnamese melodies.
  - Hours: Nightly; live music 21:00–23:15
  - Address: 1A Tràng Tiền (lane behind the Hanoi Opera House), Hoàn Kiếm
  - Price: Cover 100–150k VND per guest (club website); beer or spirits from about 110k VND (2024 review)
  - [Google Maps](https://www.google.com/maps/search/?api=1&query=Binh%20Minh%20Jazz%20Club%2C%201A%20Tr%C3%A0ng%20Ti%E1%BB%81n%2C%20Ho%C3%A0n%20Ki%E1%BA%BFm%2C%20H%C3%A0%20N%E1%BB%99i) · [Source](https://minhjazzvietnam.com/)
- **★ Long Waits Jazz** · Old Quarter (Hoàn Kiếm) · $$$ · *checked 2026*
  - A hidden second-floor listening room, opened in 2023, that has become the hub of Hanoi's young jazz scene. The house band plays classic-album tributes alongside Vietnam-rooted originals, and it was one of the two Night Jam clubs at Hanoi Jazztival 2026.
  - Tip: Go Friday or Saturday for the house band, or Thursday for the open stage where young players cut their teeth; Monday and Tuesday are free film nights. Book on +84 81 898 1156 or through the website. The entrance is a staircase off the alley at the same address as Tranquil Books & Coffee, easy to miss. Cocktails are pricey but good.
  - Music: Weekly programme posted on the club website (e.g. 5–11 Oct 2026): Mon–Tue free film screenings; Wed vocal jazz and tribute nights; Thu open stage for emerging musicians; Fri–Sat house band (album tributes, originals, crossover sets); Sun 'Listening Bar'. Sets roughly 21:00–23:30, some events from 20:00.
  - Hours: Evenings to about midnight; music roughly 21:00–23:30
  - Address: 2nd floor, 5 Nguyễn Quang Bích, Hoàn Kiếm (staircase from the alley)
  - Price: Cover was 250k VND in late 2024 (NYT 36 Hours); free entry on some nights
  - [Google Maps](https://www.google.com/maps/search/?api=1&query=Long%20Waits%20Jazz%2C%205%20Nguy%E1%BB%85n%20Quang%20B%C3%ADch%2C%20Ho%C3%A0n%20Ki%E1%BA%BFm%2C%20H%C3%A0%20N%E1%BB%99i) · [Source](https://www.longwaitsjazz.com/)
- **★ Sunday jazz at the Octagon House (Nhà Bát Giác)** (Âm nhạc cuối tuần – Nhà Bát Giác, vườn hoa tượng đài Lý Thái Tổ) · Hoàn Kiếm Lake (Hoàn Kiếm) · $ · *checked 2026*
  - Every Sunday afternoon since November 2025, the city has turned the octagonal pavilion by the Lý Thái Tổ statue into a free stage where Quyền Văn Minh, his saxophonist son Quyền Thiện Đắc and Binh Minh Jazz Club players perform for strolling locals. It's the most atmospheric way to hear top Vietnamese jazz, in the open air a few steps from the lake, and it costs nothing.
  - Tip: Arrive by 15:30 to get close to the pavilion. Some Sundays feature the city's other troupes or classical soloists instead of jazz. Make a day of it: the lake's walking street opens at 18:00 in winter (Fri–Sun), and Binh Minh's 21:00 set is a short walk away.
  - Music: The city's weekly 'Weekend Music' (Âm nhạc cuối tuần) programme, Sundays from 15:30 (about 15:30–17:00): jazz by Quyền Văn Minh, Quyền Thiện Đắc and Binh Minh Jazz Club musicians, plus guest artists and city arts troupes on some weeks. It was also a Hanoi Jazztival 2026 stage.
  - Hours: Sundays from 15:30 (about 90 minutes)
  - Address: Nhà Bát Giác (octagonal pavilion), Lý Thái Tổ statue garden, east shore of Hoàn Kiếm Lake
  - Price: Free, open-air in a public garden
  - [Google Maps](https://www.google.com/maps/search/?api=1&query=Nh%C3%A0%20B%C3%A1t%20Gi%C3%A1c%2C%20V%C6%B0%E1%BB%9Dn%20hoa%20L%C3%BD%20Th%C3%A1i%20T%E1%BB%95%2C%20Ho%C3%A0n%20Ki%E1%BA%BFm%2C%20H%C3%A0%20N%E1%BB%99i) · [Source](https://baovanhoa.vn/nghe-thuat/khi-jazz-go-cua-doi-song-van-hoa-ha-noi-266329.html)
- **Blake's House of Jazz** · Old Quarter (Hoàn Kiếm) · *checked 2026*
  - A moody, cocktail-led jazz room opened by a group of friends in 2022, with a fixed resident act each night so you know exactly what you'll hear. The Friday soul-tinged vocal night and the Saturday standards night are the hot tickets.
  - Tip: Reserve on Zalo or by phone (+84 36 441 7227) or through the website, especially for Friday and Saturday, which fill early. Every show carries an entrance fee that is added to your bill. Check the events page for specials such as Vietnamese-jazz shows and social-dance nights.
  - Music: Live jazz Thu–Sun 21:00–23:15. Thu: Syndicate of Four (modern jazz quartet). Fri: Soul After Dark ft. Bach Nguyen (vocal jazz, soul, blues). Sat: A Little Jazz, A Little Magic ft. Nguyet Ha (vocal standards). Sun: Soft Paws, Quick Turns ft. saxophonist Vladimir (jazz, blues).
  - Hours: Thu–Sun 19:00–00:30; occasional special events on other nights
  - Address: 64 Nguyễn Hữu Huân, Hoàn Kiếm
  - Price: Entrance fee for every show, settled with the bill (amount not published)
  - [Google Maps](https://www.google.com/maps/search/?api=1&query=Blake%27s%20House%20of%20Jazz%2C%2064%20Nguy%E1%BB%85n%20H%E1%BB%AFu%20Hu%C3%A2n%2C%20Ho%C3%A0n%20Ki%E1%BA%BFm%2C%20H%C3%A0%20N%E1%BB%99i) · [Source](https://blakebar.com)
- **Le Club Bar (Sofitel Legend Metropole)** · French Quarter (Hoàn Kiếm) · $$$$ · *checked 2026*
  - Live jazz most nights in the grand old hotel's bar, with a resident band and jazz singer, and drinks from the Metropole's Legendary Cocktail list, named for guests such as Graham Greene and Charlie Chaplin. It's the splurge, dress-up way to hear jazz in Hanoi.
  - Tip: Come for evening cocktails (17:00–19:00) and stay for the 20:00 set; 'The Last Telegram' is the newest Legendary Cocktail. There's no jazz on Mondays. High tea (15:00–16:30) shows off the room by daylight.
  - Music: Live jazz 20:00–22:45: Tue–Sat the band with a jazz diva, Sunday band only; none listed on Monday.
  - Hours: Daily 06:00–23:00; live jazz Tue–Sun 20:00–22:45
  - Address: Sofitel Legend Metropole Hanoi, 15 Ngô Quyền, Hoàn Kiếm
  - Price: Signature cocktails 350–380k VND++, wine by the glass from 280k++ (May 2026 menu), plus service charge and VAT
  - [Google Maps](https://www.google.com/maps/search/?api=1&query=Le%20Club%20Bar%2C%20Sofitel%20Legend%20Metropole%2C%2015%20Ng%C3%B4%20Quy%E1%BB%81n%2C%20Ho%C3%A0n%20Ki%E1%BA%BFm%2C%20H%C3%A0%20N%E1%BB%99i) · [Source](https://www.sofitel-legend-metropole-hanoi.com/dining/le-club-bar/)

#### Clubs & DJs

- **★ Unmute** · Phúc Tân, by the Red River at the edge of the Old Quarter (Hoàn Kiếm) · $$ · *checked 2026*
  - Since Savage closed, this 300-capacity club on the top floors of a 24-hour fruit-and-vegetable market building has been the heart of Hanoi's underground, with a spring-loaded dancefloor, a custom four-way sound system and a roof-terrace bar. Founded by DJ Lam Dao, it books local residents and collectives such as CUỐN and METRØ alongside names like Jeff Mills and DJ Nobu.
  - Tip: Buy on Resident Advisor in advance: about 226k VND online against 300–400k at the door, with cheaper under-24 tickets. The way in is an unmarked parking-garage door, so keep quiet in the lane. Carry ID and stay clean, because police raid and drug-test at Hanoi clubs. Head up to the terrace for ambient and sunrise sets.
  - Music: Techno, house, electro, disco and ambient club nights, mostly Fri–Sat about 22:00–05:00. Recent and upcoming: The Observatory takeover (Fri 25 Sep 2026); levolant/Tanohi/CB/Vitro (Fri 9 Oct 2026); Tangela/awuwa/Ai Lien/Canicule (Sat 10 Oct 2026).
  - Hours: Club nights mainly Fri–Sat, about 22:00–05:00 (some until 06:00)
  - Address: Floors 4–6, 169 Phúc Tân, Hoàn Kiếm
  - Price: About 226k VND online (RA), 300–400k at the door, 150–200k under-24 tickets (2026 events)
  - [Google Maps](https://www.google.com/maps/search/?api=1&query=Unmute%2C%20169%20Ph%C3%BAc%20T%C3%A2n%2C%20Ho%C3%A0n%20Ki%E1%BA%BFm%2C%20H%C3%A0%20N%E1%BB%99i) · [Source](https://www.huckmag.com/article/hanoi-nightlife-clubs-raids-police-unmute-outlandish)
- **Factory 47** · Old Quarter (Hoàn Kiếm) · $ · *checked 2026*
  - An industrial-style Old Quarter bar, with gears and chains on the walls and pool tables upstairs, that becomes a home for the underground at night: the Ca Đêm parties run here, and so do the Savage crew's takeovers since that club closed.
  - Tip: Check Resident Advisor and Instagram for Ca Đêm nights (e.g. a Savage takeover with Ouissam, Di Linh, TaoFu, Zwi and CONG, Fri 9 Oct 2026). Le Maquis Club, another RA-listed DJ spot, is a few doors down at 41 Hàng Buồm, so you can do both in one night.
  - Music: DJ nights from disco, house and techno to progressive, psychedelic, downtempo and trip-hop; Ca Đêm runs about 21:00–03:00.
  - Hours: Daily from about noon to 02:00 (Sun from 10:00); DJ nights to about 03:00
  - Address: 47 Hàng Buồm, Hoàn Kiếm
  - Price: Ca Đêm DJ nights have been free entry (Jun 2026)
  - [Google Maps](https://www.google.com/maps/search/?api=1&query=Factory%2047%2C%2047%20H%C3%A0ng%20Bu%E1%BB%93m%2C%20Ho%C3%A0n%20Ki%E1%BA%BFm%2C%20H%C3%A0%20N%E1%BB%99i) · [Source](https://mixmag.asia/read/hanoi-ca-dem-at-factory47-events)
- **Le Maquis Club** · Old Quarter (Hoàn Kiếm) · $$ · *checked 2026*
  - A 100-capacity speakeasy-style club bar in the middle of the Old Quarter where Hanoi's DJ collectives (Thêm, Strawberry Cult, DiscHogs, Flavacode, Tổ Công Tác) throw genuinely late parties, often running to 04:00–05:00 in a city that mostly winds down at midnight. Entry is cheap and the crowd comes for the music.
  - Tip: Check Resident Advisor for the week's line-up and get in before midnight. Next up: DiscHogs' vinyl-only night with Hocking & K-Sang (Thêm), Sat 10 Oct 2026, 22:00–05:00, 200k VND. It's a few doors from Factory 47 (47 Hàng Buồm), so you can hop between the two. RA nights are 18+, so carry ID.
  - Music: Collective-run DJ nights: house and electro (Thêm), bass and UK garage (Flavacode, Tổ Công Tác), vinyl-only sets (DiscHogs), Strawberry Cult parties, R&B/hip-hop nights and queer parties (Dập Dìu – Ái Tiệc). Recent: Strawberry Cult Vol. III (12 Sep 2026) and Tổ Công Tác x Flavacode Bass Edition (19 Sep 2026).
  - Hours: Club nights mostly Saturdays (some Fridays), about 21:00/22:00 to 04:00–05:00; next: Sat 10 Oct 2026, 22:00–05:00
  - Address: 41 Hàng Buồm, Hoàn Kiếm
  - Price: Cover 100–200k VND on most 2026 RA-listed nights, some free; drink prices not published
  - [Google Maps](https://www.google.com/maps/search/?api=1&query=Le%20Maquis%20Club%2C%2041%20H%C3%A0ng%20Bu%E1%BB%93m%2C%20Ho%C3%A0n%20Ki%E1%BA%BFm%2C%20H%C3%A0%20N%E1%BB%99i) · [Source](https://ra.co/clubs/287276)
- **Mirage** · West Lake – Xuân Diệu (Tây Hồ) · $$ · *unverified*
  - A small, boxy upstairs room that has been a pillar of the scene since 2021 and came back after a 2024 closure for road widening, with adventurous bookings: its POUND TOWN series brought Chicago ghetto-house pioneer Traxman here for his first Vietnam show in November 2025.
  - Tip: Check Instagram and Resident Advisor before going: the latest listings found were for June 2026, and Huck reported a police raid in mid-2026 with reopening unclear. If it's on, expect a late night, often to sunrise; carry ID and leave calmly if the music is cut.
  - Music: House, techno, electro, ghetto house/juke and left-field club music, mainly weekend nights. 2026 listings included Globular feat. Karnak On Acid (31 Jan), Numero 6 (28 Feb), Paradise Characters (25 Apr) and HUM and Friends (27 Jun).
  - Hours: Event nights (mostly Saturdays, some Fridays) about 22:00 to 06:00–07:00 (RA listings, 2025–26)
  - Address: 99 Xuân Diệu, Quảng An, Tây Hồ
  - Price: About 200k VND entry (RA: 31 Jan and 27 Jun 2026); bigger guest nights 200k online / 300k at the door (Nov 2025)
  - [Google Maps](https://www.google.com/maps/search/?api=1&query=Mirage%2C%2099%20Xu%C3%A2n%20Di%E1%BB%87u%2C%20T%C3%A2y%20H%E1%BB%93%2C%20H%C3%A0%20N%E1%BB%99i) · [Source](https://mixmag.asia/read/hanoi-pound-town-x-hyper-hill-present-traxman-events)
- **Comma Bar** · Just west of the Old Quarter · *checked 2026*
  - A small bar built around DJ culture rather than bottle service, with two steady weekly residencies. Friday's 'Groove Theory' plays jazz house, soulful house, breaks and future beats, and Saturday's 'After Hour Club' plays deep, hypnotic house for the late hours, with local selectors such as Savage resident Zwi. It's the grown-up alternative to the Tạ Hiện bars.
  - Tip: Come after 22:00 on a Friday or Saturday; RA lists each week's guest DJ. Entry was free on every RA-listed night in September and early October 2026 but cost 300k VND on summer nights, so check the current listing.
  - Music: Fri 'Groove Theory' and Sat 'After Hour Club', both 22:00–late, with resident and guest DJs (e.g. Koleco on 2 Oct and Zwi on 3 Oct 2026); occasional special editions.
  - Hours: DJ nights Fri and Sat from 22:00 till late; other bar hours not published
  - Address: 69 Tôn Thất Thiệp
  - Price: Free entry on RA-listed nights Sep–early Oct 2026; 300k VND on Jun–Aug 2026 DJ nights; drink prices not published
  - [Google Maps](https://www.google.com/maps/search/?api=1&query=Comma%20Bar%2C%2069%20T%C3%B4n%20Th%E1%BA%A5t%20Thi%E1%BB%87p%2C%20H%C3%A0%20N%E1%BB%99i) · [Source](https://ra.co/events/2551262)

#### Traditional & classical

- **★ Thang Long Water Puppet Theatre** (Nhà hát Múa rối Thăng Long) · Hoàn Kiếm Lake, edge of the Old Quarter (Hoàn Kiếm) · $$ · *checked 2026*
  - Hanoi's classic water-puppet house: in about 50 minutes, 15–17 short scenes of village life and legend splash across a pool while a live traditional orchestra and chèo-style singers perform beside the stage. It's the easiest first taste of northern Vietnamese folk music.
  - Tip: Buy at the box office or call 024 3824 9494; the old thanglongwaterpuppet.org domain is now a spam site. From November to March book the 20:00 show 3–7 days ahead, because it sells out first; earlier shows are easier. If it's full, the national Vietnam Puppetry Theatre (361 Trường Chinh) runs its own shows.
  - Music: Live traditional orchestra and chèo-style singers throughout, with Vietnamese narration; no interval.
  - Hours: Several shows daily from mid-afternoon (e.g. 16:10, 17:20, 18:30, 20:00), extra morning shows on Sundays; holiday schedules vary
  - Address: 57B Đinh Tiên Hoàng, Hoàn Kiếm
  - Price: Tickets 100k / 150k / 200k VND; camera fee 20k, video 50k
  - [Google Maps](https://www.google.com/maps/search/?api=1&query=Thang%20Long%20Water%20Puppet%20Theatre%2C%2057B%20%C4%90inh%20Ti%C3%AAn%20Ho%C3%A0ng%2C%20Ho%C3%A0n%20Ki%E1%BA%BFm%2C%20H%C3%A0%20N%E1%BB%99i) · [Source](https://www.vietnamtourism.com/en/thang-long-water-puppet-theatre-in-hanoi-showtimes-tickets-and-how-long-it-lasts)
- **★ Bích Câu Ca Trù Theatre (Bích Câu Đạo Quán)** (Nhà hát Ca trù Bích Câu – Bích Câu Đạo Quán) · Đống Đa (Cát Linh, near the Temple of Literature) · $ · *checked 2026*
  - Hear UNESCO-listed ca trù, the sung poetry of old Hanoi's literati, inside a historic Taoist temple, where artisans Nguyễn Thị Vân Mai and Trần Văn Trúc have taught it free since 2008. Their club has staged free performances every Saturday evening since 2017, and international visitors are welcome.
  - Tip: Go on a Saturday: sit in on the free afternoon class, then stay for the evening show. The start time isn't published, so arrive in the early evening, and listen quietly, since ca trù is chamber music. It's near the Temple of Literature, so pair the two.
  - Music: Ca trù: a female singer keeping time on phách bamboo clappers, answered by the long-necked đàn đáy lute and the praise drum (trống chầu). Free shows every Saturday evening; free classes on Saturday afternoons.
  - Hours: Saturday afternoons (free class) and Saturday evenings (free show; start time not published)
  - Address: 14 Cát Linh, Đống Đa
  - Price: Free
  - [Google Maps](https://www.google.com/maps/search/?api=1&query=B%C3%ADch%20C%C3%A2u%20%C4%90%E1%BA%A1o%20Qu%C3%A1n%2C%2014%20C%C3%A1t%20Linh%2C%20%C4%90%E1%BB%91ng%20%C4%90a%2C%20H%C3%A0%20N%E1%BB%99i) · [Source](https://vietnamnews.vn/life-style/1796149/free-ca-tru-classes-held-to-preserve-traditional-singing-art.html)
- **CADAO Collective** · West Lake (Tây Hồ) · *checked 2026*
  - Opened in July 2026 as the home of the new Tây Hồ Puppet Guild, this leafy heritage space puts a water-puppet pavilion alongside intimate xẩm and ca trù sets, some reworked with contemporary arrangements, plus shadow puppetry with projections.
  - Tip: Aim for a weekend, when the shows run. Times and prices aren't published, so check the venue's channels or drop in to ask (it's open daily 07:00–22:00). The water-puppet programme changes monthly, and the upstairs dining area makes it a full evening.
  - Music: Ca trù and xẩm, both traditional and remixed, alongside water and shadow puppetry set to folk melodies and new compositions.
  - Hours: Daily 07:00–22:00; performances mainly at weekends (times not published)
  - Address: 66 Tô Ngọc Vân, Tây Hồ
  - Price: Ticket prices not published
  - [Google Maps](https://www.google.com/maps/search/?api=1&query=CADAO%20Collective%2C%2066%20T%C3%B4%20Ng%E1%BB%8Dc%20V%C3%A2n%2C%20T%C3%A2y%20H%E1%BB%93%2C%20H%C3%A0%20N%E1%BB%99i) · [Source](https://vietnamnews.vn/life-style/1785245/new-cultural-space-expands-ha-noi-s-stage-for-traditional-heritage.html)
- **Hồ Gươm Opera** (Nhà hát Hồ Gươm) · French Quarter, south of Hoàn Kiếm Lake (Hoàn Kiếm) · *checked 2026*
  - With the colonial Opera House closed for restoration since January 2026, this 800-seat house near the lake is where Hanoi's big classical nights happen: the London Symphony Orchestra under Sir Antonio Pappano played here on 2–3 Oct 2026. It also hosts the season's New Year and Lunar New Year symphony concerts.
  - Tip: Book on hoguomopera.com or call 0835 661 999 (international programmes: 082 558 3888). Watch for the late-December New Year concert (28 Dec last season) and the post-Tết concert (24 Feb 2026 last season); the 2026–27 dates weren't announced by early October.
  - Music: Visiting orchestras, opera and Vietnamese gala concerts, usually at 20:00.
  - Hours: Performance nights only
  - Address: 40 Hàng Bài, Hoàn Kiếm
  - Price: Varies by programme
  - [Google Maps](https://www.google.com/maps/search/?api=1&query=Nh%C3%A0%20h%C3%A1t%20H%E1%BB%93%20G%C6%B0%C6%A1m%2C%2040%20H%C3%A0ng%20B%C3%A0i%2C%20Ho%C3%A0n%20Ki%E1%BA%BFm%2C%20H%C3%A0%20N%E1%BB%99i) · [Source](https://hoguomopera.com/)
- **Đông Kinh Cổ Nhạc – 'Old Quarter Music Stories'** (Đông Kinh Cổ Nhạc – Chuyện nhạc phố cổ) · Old Quarter (Hoàn Kiếm) · $ · *checked 2026*
  - Since 2015 this ensemble of master traditional artists has staged a free monthly hour of Thăng Long–Hanoi music (ca trù, chèo, tuồng, xẩm, quan họ, chầu văn) in a plain third-floor room set up like a village communal house. With no stage effects or microphones, you hear silk strings and voices as they were meant to sound, a few streets from the Old Quarter's bars.
  - Tip: Entry is free. Buy a 50k or 100k VND bamboo token (thẻ tre) and toss it into the bronze bowl after a piece you love, the old way of rewarding singers. Dates change month to month, so check the ensemble's Facebook page or ask at the centre, and arrive early because seats are limited.
  - Music: Acoustic northern traditions performed by master artists: ca trù, chèo, tuồng, xẩm, quan họ and chầu văn; about one hour.
  - Hours: Once a month (date varies); about 1 hour
  - Address: 3rd floor, Old Quarter Cultural Exchange Centre (Trung tâm Giao lưu Văn hóa Phố cổ), 50 Đào Duy Từ, Hoàn Kiếm
  - Price: Free; optional 50k/100k VND bamboo tokens as tips
  - [Google Maps](https://www.google.com/maps/search/?api=1&query=Trung%20t%C3%A2m%20Giao%20l%C6%B0u%20V%C4%83n%20h%C3%B3a%20Ph%E1%BB%91%20c%E1%BB%95%2C%2050%20%C4%90%C3%A0o%20Duy%20T%E1%BB%AB%2C%20Ho%C3%A0n%20Ki%E1%BA%BFm%2C%20H%C3%A0%20N%E1%BB%99i) · [Source](https://tienphong.vn/epaper/2026/TP-90/8/)
- **Hanoi Chèo Theatre – 'Nét duyên Chèo Hà Thành'** (Nhà hát Chèo Hà Nội – Rạp Đại Nam) · Phố Huế (Hai Bà Trưng) · *checked 2026*
  - Chèo is the sung folk theatre of the Red River Delta, and the city's chèo company now stages 'Nét duyên Chèo Hà Thành' (The Charm of Hanoi's Chèo), a continuous one-hour showcase of song, dance and live traditional music that debuted in July 2026.
  - Tip: Saturday shows start at 20:00 (October 2026: 17, 24 and 31 Oct, per the theatre's schedule). Book on 091 891 8894, 090 416 3977 or 091 511 8064, or via nhahatcheohanoi.vn, which posts each month's schedule as an image. Full-length classics such as 'Quan Âm Thị Kính' play on some other nights.
  - Music: Chèo singing and dance with a live traditional ensemble; about 60 minutes from 20:00.
  - Hours: Saturdays 20:00 (Oct 2026: 17, 24, 31 Oct); occasional other nights
  - Address: Rạp Đại Nam, 89 Phố Huế, Hai Bà Trưng
  - Price: Prices not published online; ask when booking
  - [Google Maps](https://www.google.com/maps/search/?api=1&query=R%E1%BA%A1p%20%C4%90%E1%BA%A1i%20Nam%2C%2089%20Ph%E1%BB%91%20Hu%E1%BA%BF%2C%20Hai%20B%C3%A0%20Tr%C6%B0ng%2C%20H%C3%A0%20N%E1%BB%99i) · [Source](https://nhahatcheohanoi.vn/chuong-trinh-moi-nhat/lich-dien-thang-10)
- **'Stories of Hanoi – A Very Hanoi Day' (Friday show)** (Chuyện Hà Nội – Một ngày rất Hà Nội (Nhà hát Ca Múa Nhạc Thăng Long)) · Old Quarter, near Hoàn Kiếm Lake (Hoàn Kiếm) · *checked 2026*
  - A new city-backed show for visitors that follows a Hanoi day from quiet dawn to the busy Old Quarter night in 45 minutes. Under People's Artist Tấn Minh, đàn bầu, đàn tranh, bamboo flute and xẩm meet beatbox and hip hop, with dance and modern lighting, and the audience gets bamboo clappers to keep time with the musicians.
  - Tip: It's the Friday counterpart to the Chèo Theatre's Saturday 'Nét duyên Chèo Hà Thành' (in this guide). Prices haven't been published, so ask at the box office or through your hotel. Afterwards, walk to the lake for the Friday-night walking street.
  - Music: Traditional instruments (đàn bầu, đàn tranh, sáo trúc) and xẩm with beatbox, hip hop and dance; music direction by Trí Minh.
  - Hours: Fridays 20:00 (weekly since August 2026); about 45 minutes
  - Address: Thăng Long Song & Dance Theatre (Nhà hát Ca Múa Nhạc Thăng Long), 31–33 Lương Văn Can, Hoàn Kiếm
  - Price: Ticket prices not published
  - [Google Maps](https://www.google.com/maps/search/?api=1&query=Nh%C3%A0%20h%C3%A1t%20Ca%20M%C3%BAa%20Nh%E1%BA%A1c%20Th%C4%83ng%20Long%2C%2031%20L%C6%B0%C6%A1ng%20V%C4%83n%20Can%2C%20Ho%C3%A0n%20Ki%E1%BA%BFm%2C%20H%C3%A0%20N%E1%BB%99i) · [Source](https://baovanhoa.vn/nghe-thuat/ha-noi-ra-mat-chuong-trinh-nghe-thuat-chat-luong-cao-huong-toi-phuc-vu-du-lich-252342.html)

#### Rock & live bands

- **Outpost Hanoi** · Tứ Liên, by the Red River dyke (Tây Hồ) · $$ · *checked 2026*
  - With Hanoi Rock City gone, this community-minded venue near the Red River has the most regular band nights in town: rock nights, a tribute-band festival, charity gigs and big themed all-nighters with DJs and sound systems.
  - Tip: Halloween is its biggest night: 'Back From The Dead III: The Freak Show' on Sat 31 Oct 2026 runs from sunset to sunrise across 3 stages and 2 venues, with early-bird tickets on Megatix. It's a Grab ride from the Old Quarter.
  - Music: Local and expat bands (rock, tribute and cover acts), reggae/dub sound systems and DJ after-parties. E.g. 'Sound Off for Blue Dragon' charity gig with Red River Revival, Puddles and Not Too Shabby, Sat 10 Oct 2026 from 19:00.
  - Hours: Event nights, usually from about 19:00
  - Address: Alley 154 Đê Quai, Tứ Liên, Tây Hồ
  - Price: Gig entry about 150k VND at the door (Oct 2026 charity show); festival tickets on Megatix
  - [Google Maps](https://www.google.com/maps/search/?api=1&query=Outpost%20Hanoi%2C%20Ng%C3%B5%20154%20%C4%90%C3%AA%20Quai%2C%20T%E1%BB%A9%20Li%C3%AAn%2C%20T%C3%A2y%20H%E1%BB%93%2C%20H%C3%A0%20N%E1%BB%99i) · [Source](https://allevents.in/org/outpost-hanoi/21142706)
- **Purple Blues Bar** · Old Quarter (Hoàn Kiếm) · $$ · *likely open*
  - The Old Quarter's dedicated live-rock room since June 2022. A rotation of local bands, including Purple Blues, the band local press calls the 'eldest brother' of Hanoi rock, plays everything from US/UK classic rock and pop hits to hard rock and metal, often in sets of up to three hours.
  - Tip: Arrive before 22:00, because it fills fast, or book a table on 090 343 34 96. Mondays usually have no band. Reviewers say drinks aren't cheap, but no cover charge is mentioned.
  - Music: Nightly local bands (Purple Blues Band, Sor Band, Skull Of Rock, Nevermind, Makesense): classic and hard rock, heavy metal, US/UK rock and pop covers; usually no band on Mondays.
  - Hours: Tue–Sun 15:00–02:00 per its own website (Google listing says daily, to about 03:00 Fri–Sat); bands usually about 21:45–midnight
  - Address: 7 Hàng Muối, Hoàn Kiếm
  - Price: No cover mentioned; reviewers note drinks aren't cheap
  - [Google Maps](https://www.google.com/maps/search/?api=1&query=Purple%20Blues%20Bar%2C%207%20H%C3%A0ng%20Mu%E1%BB%91i%2C%20Ho%C3%A0n%20Ki%E1%BA%BFm%2C%20H%C3%A0%20N%E1%BB%99i) · [Source](http://www.purpleblues.id.vn/home)

#### Records & vinyl

- **LP Club & Montauk** · Kim Mã (Ba Đình) · *checked 2026*
  - Hanoi's best record-shop-and-bar combo. LP Club, upstairs, has sold pre-owned and new vinyl since 2018 (the jazz and classic-rock crates are the strongest) and presses CDs and vinyl for local indie acts; Montauk, downstairs, is an 'Eternal Sunshine'-themed café-cocktail bar lined with sleeves, cassettes and band merch.
  - Tip: Dig upstairs in the late afternoon, then take a sofa downstairs for a highball. Used classics start around 350k VND and new LPs run about 850k–1.25M. Ask about Vietnamese pressings and album-release parties for local indie bands. Phone 0904 546 883.
  - Music: Curated playlists (mostly Western, some Vietnamese) and occasional album-release shows by local indie artists.
  - Hours: LP Club daily 10:00–20:00; Montauk café-bar 07:30–23:30
  - Address: 174 Kim Mã, Ba Đình
  - Price: Records about 350k–1.25M VND (web shop); drink prices not published
  - [Google Maps](https://www.google.com/maps/search/?api=1&query=LP%20Club%20Montauk%2C%20174%20Kim%20M%C3%A3%2C%20Ba%20%C4%90%C3%ACnh%2C%20H%C3%A0%20N%E1%BB%99i) · [Source](https://lpclub.vn)
- **Dia Than Vintage** · Yên Phụ, by West Lake (Tây Hồ) · *unverified*
  - A crate-digger's shop founded by local DJ 2T, stocking rare pre-owned pressings across pop, rock, jazz, funk, soul, folk/world and electronic, plus a dedicated corner of classic breaks and rare material for DJs and beatmakers.
  - Tip: It's closed on Mondays. Pair a dig with a lakeside coffee on the Yên Phụ strip.
  - Hours: Tue–Sun 09:30–19:00; closed Mon
  - Address: 147 Yên Phụ, Tây Hồ
  - [Google Maps](https://www.google.com/maps/search/?api=1&query=%C4%90%C4%A9a%20Than%20Vintage%2C%20147%20Y%C3%AAn%20Ph%E1%BB%A5%2C%20T%C3%A2y%20H%E1%BB%93%2C%20H%C3%A0%20N%E1%BB%99i) · [Source](https://www.trvlogue.com/en/hanoi-vintage-shops-west-lake)

#### Upcoming gigs & festivals

- **Sun Symphony Orchestra: 'LONGING' with Boris Giltburg** (Hòa nhạc Masterworks: LONGING – Thiên Cổ Tình (Dàn nhạc Giao hưởng Mặt Trời)) · French Quarter, south of Hoàn Kiếm Lake (Hoàn Kiếm) · *checked 2026*
  - On the eve of Hanoi's Liberation Day (10 Oct), the Sun Symphony Orchestra brings pianist Boris Giltburg, winner of the 2013 Queen Elisabeth Competition, to play Rachmaninoff's Second Piano Concerto. The programme adds excerpts from Prokofiev's Romeo and Juliet and new arrangements of two beloved Hanoi songs: Hoàng Dương's 'Hướng về Hà Nội' for cello and orchestra (soloist Phúc Phan) and Trịnh Công Sơn's 'Nhớ mùa thu Hà Nội'.
  - Tip: Book through the 'Mua vé' (buy tickets) Google Form on the concert page at sunsymphony.vn. Prices still read 'to be updated' on 6 Oct, so book as soon as sales open. Hồ Gươm Opera is a short walk south of the lake, and Binh Minh Jazz Club, behind the old Opera House, plays until 23:15 if you want a nightcap.
  - Music: Sun Symphony Orchestra; Boris Giltburg (piano), Phúc Phan (cello). Rachmaninoff Piano Concerto No. 2, Prokofiev Romeo and Juliet (excerpts), and 'Hướng về Hà Nội' and 'Nhớ mùa thu Hà Nội' in new orchestral arrangements.
  - Hours: Fri 9 Oct 2026, 20:00 – Hồ Gươm Opera, 40 Hàng Bài
  - Address: Hồ Gươm Opera (Nhà hát Hồ Gươm), 40 Hàng Bài, Hoàn Kiếm
  - Price: Prices not yet published (the ticket function read 'to be updated' on sunsymphony.vn on 6 Oct 2026); booking via Google Form
  - [Google Maps](https://www.google.com/maps/search/?api=1&query=Nh%C3%A0%20h%C3%A1t%20H%E1%BB%93%20G%C6%B0%C6%A1m%2C%2040%20H%C3%A0ng%20B%C3%A0i%2C%20Ho%C3%A0n%20Ki%E1%BA%BFm%2C%20H%C3%A0%20N%E1%BB%99i) · [Source](https://sunsymphony.vn/event/hoa-nhac-masterworks-longing-thien-co-tinh/)
- **Noise Hanoi: The Resurrection (Halloween all-dayer)** · Tây Hồ (per researcher notes; exact address not published) · $$ · *checked 2026*
  - A Halloween party of live bands and DJs around a garden pool, from lunchtime to sunrise. The Gratoners reunite for a one-off set, joined by the visiting La Belle Et La Bete, the dub-and-electronics act Jungle Lily and Hanoi's own Sally chơi nhạc. Reggae and pool DJs play by day, and Strawberry Cult's Colette and E.motep take over after the bands.
  - Tip: Presales are 200k VND and costumes are expected. It clashes with Outpost's 'Back From The Dead III' (in this guide), so pick one. Get the garden's exact location from the RA event page or the promoter before you set off, and carry ID (18+).
  - Music: Live: The Gratoners, La Belle Et La Bete, Jungle Lily, Sally chơi nhạc. DJs: Pickled.VN, Defused Moods and One Nayton (daytime); Colette, E.motep, Mari-J and more (night).
  - Hours: Sat 31 Oct 2026, from 12:00–13:00 until 06:00 Sun 1 Nov – Sala Nature Garden
  - Address: Sala Nature Garden (street address not given on the RA listing)
  - Price: 200k VND presale, covering the pool DJs and all the live music (RA)
  - [Google Maps](https://www.google.com/maps/search/?api=1&query=Sala%20Nature%20Garden%2C%20T%C3%A2y%20H%E1%BB%93%2C%20H%C3%A0%20N%E1%BB%99i) · [Source](https://ra.co/events/2546586)
- **Ồn Ào Vol. 3: Skin On Skin at Mirage** · West Lake – Xuân Diệu (Tây Hồ) · *checked 2026*
  - One of the biggest club bookings of Hanoi's autumn: Skin On Skin, the producer behind the club hit 'Burn Dem Bridges', headlines the third Ồn Ào party in Mirage's small upstairs room, with Vietnamese DJs dMb, CON.R, LINHTONIK and PH00000NG in support.
  - Tip: Ticket details still read 'to be updated' on RA in early October. Watch RA and buy online as soon as sales open, because Mirage is small. It's 18+, so carry ID.
  - Music: Skin On Skin with dMb (VN), CON.R, LINHTONIK and PH00000NG; club music into the early hours.
  - Hours: Fri 13 Nov 2026, 22:00–05:00 – Mirage, 99 Xuân Diệu
  - Address: Mirage, 99 Xuân Diệu, Quảng An, Tây Hồ
  - Price: Not yet announced; other 2026 Mirage nights charged 150–270k VND (RA)
  - [Google Maps](https://www.google.com/maps/search/?api=1&query=Mirage%2C%2099%20Xu%C3%A2n%20Di%E1%BB%87u%2C%20T%C3%A2y%20H%E1%BB%93%2C%20H%C3%A0%20N%E1%BB%99i) · [Source](https://ra.co/events/2542196)

### Hanoi: Games (Trò chơi)

Hanoi's video-game scene is excellent and cheap. Big 'cyber' chains led by Vikings and OEG run hundreds of 240–500 Hz PCs per branch, most seats cost 10,000–25,000 VND an hour, and a five-star hotel now has a proper console lounge. Real-world shooting is thin and tightly regulated: there is no tourist live-fire range, no paintball field we could verify and no laser-tag arena we could confirm for 2026, so archery and the traditional crossbow are the legal outlets, alongside an English-language escape-room brand, late-night mall bowling and student board-game cafés.

#### Esports & PC gaming

- **★ OEG Stadium (Chợ Mơ)** (OEG Stadium – TTTM Chợ Mơ) · Bạch Mai – Chợ Mơ (Hai Bà Trưng) · $ · *checked 2026*
  - Hanoi's flagship esports complex has 250 PCs over two floors of the Chợ Mơ mall, plus a stage that hosted the NSOC 2025 national student final. Its Stadium Zone (RTX 4090, 360 Hz) and Infinity Capsule (RTX 4090, 500 Hz ASUS ROG Swift) are the most extreme CS2 and Valorant seats in the city.
  - Tip: For shooters, the FPS Zone (RTX 3060, 240 Hz ASUS ROG Strix, 18,700 VND/h) is the value pick. To splurge, take the Stadium Zone (i9-13900F, RTX 4090, 64 GB, Zowie XL2566K 360 Hz, 49,500/h) or the Infinity Capsule (99,000/h). Sign-up isn't explained online, so ask at the counter to open a member account and top up; the hotline is 0363 459 459. OEG's NSOC 2026 student league (Valorant and Liên Quân) runs until 29 Nov, so watch OEG's pages for the finale.
  - Hours: OEG lists it as open all day ('hoạt động cả ngày'), but see the tip on the legal 22:00–08:00 limit
  - Address: TTTM Chợ Mơ, 459 Bạch Mai, Trương Định, Hai Bà Trưng, Hà Nội
  - Price: 16,500 VND/h in the Duo, VIP and MOBA zones (RTX 3060, 240 Hz); FPS Zone 18,700; Private (RTX 4060) 22,000; Premium (380 Hz) 27,500; Stadium Zone (RTX 4090) 49,500; Infinity Capsule 99,000 (OEG site, Oct 2026)
  - [Google Maps](https://www.google.com/maps/search/?api=1&query=OEG%20Stadium%20Ch%E1%BB%A3%20M%C6%A1%2C%20459%20B%E1%BA%A1ch%20Mai%2C%20H%C3%A0%20N%E1%BB%99i) · [Source](https://cyber.oeg.vn/tttm-cho-mo)
