- Bring your own reading: Sa Pa has no bookshop or book café. Hostelworld listings show book exchanges at some homestays, such as Mega View Homestay (24 Cầu Mây) and The Zen House Sapa beside the Ancient Stone Field, so ask when you book.
- The one book for the ride up: Nguyễn Thành Long's short story 'Lặng lẽ Sa Pa' ('Quiet Sa Pa'), written in 1970 after a trip to Lào Cai and still taught in Vietnamese schools. Its young weather-station worker lives alone on a 2,600 m peak and meets passengers on the Lào Cai–Sa Pa bus. There's no real station or memorial to visit, so read it on the way.
- Stories, not storytelling shows: we found no regular folk-tale or storytelling event for visitors in 2025–26. Hmong and Red Dao trekking guides and homestay hosts are the best tellers of local legends, including those of the stone field, so visit the museum for background first and then ask on the trail.
- Tết folk games: at Fansipan's 2026 spring fair at Bản Mây (days 3–5 of Tết), visitors joined Hmong games such as đánh yến (feather shuttlecock), ném pao (ball toss), blindfold duck-catching and stilt-walking. Tết 2027 falls on Saturday 6 February, so expect the fair around 8–10 February 2027, and check sunworld.vn for the programme.
- After midnight, the friendliest options are Color Bar (to about 01:30) and Relax Bar on Thạch Sơn St (to 01:00). Most other picks close by midnight, and the Cầu Mây DJ lounges that run later draw overcharging complaints.
- For private-room karaoke, Sun Paradise Land's bar list (27 Nov 2025) names Infinity Lounge & Karaoke Sa Pa (6A, Group 6, 'Treo Garden Alley'; 16:00–01:00). We could not verify it, and the same list still includes the closed H'Mong Sisters, so check Google Maps first. Relax Bar's karaoke nights are the casual alternative.
- Two places share the Coóng name: the treehouse café-homestay on the Fansipan road toward Cát Cát, which Google marks closed, and Coóng Coffee & fastfood at 6 Thạch Sơn (daily 7:00–23:00), an ordinary town café. An open 'Coóng' on Google Maps is not the treehouse.
- Mid-November art shows: in 2025 the Winter Festival staged 'Thiêng' (Red Dao rituals such as the cấp sắc coming-of-age ceremony, with 3D mapping, about 65 minutes) on 7 and 14 Nov and 'Vũ điệu dưới trăng' (Dao, Mông, Tày, Giáy and Xá Phó music and dance) on 8 and 15 Nov, both on Sân Quần square. 2026 dates were not out by 6 Oct; watch baolaocai.vn in late October.

### Sa Pa: closed, moved or doubtful

- **The Coóng – Coóng Sol music nights**: Google marks The Coong Cafe and Homestay (Cát Cát) permanently closed (via Wanderlog, Oct 2026). The last Coóng Sol evidence is a Nov 2025 listicle and older press.
- **Bamboo Bar / Bamboo Lounge weekend folk show (Bamboo Sapa Hotel)**: No 2026 confirmation of the show: the hotel's own site and its Jul 2026 happy-hour post mention only the lounge, and listicles recycle older show listings. The lounge's daily 16:00–20:00 buy-one-get-one happy hour is in the tips.
- **The H'mong Sisters**: Google marks it permanently closed (Wanderlog's best-pubs list, updated 17 Jul 2026), although Local Vietnam's June 2025 nightlife guide still recommends its pool table.
- **Coóng Coffee & Stay**: Doubtful. Round 1 found Google marks the treehouse café-homestay (The Coong Cafe and Homestay, Fansipan road toward Cát Cát) permanently closed. Its only 2026 mention is a passing line in VnExpress's evergreen Sa Pa guide (updated 16 Apr 2026). The open 'Coóng Coffee & fastfood' listing at 6 Thạch Sơn (Google 4.4 from 433) is a separate in-town café without the treehouse setting.
- **Skyline Bar**: Doubtful. The live-band claim rests only on identical listicle copy (Mytour, updated 15 Sep 2026, and Toplist; Mytour also still lists closed bars). No Google listing, schedule or review mentioning the band turned up. The Hotpot Center restaurant in the same building (71 Xuân Viên) gets mixed reviews.

### Sa Pa: considered but not picked

- **Sa Pa Night Market (weekend folk stage)**: Sources conflict on location and hours (Sun Group: N1 St, Fri–Sun 16:00–22:00; Google: Lương Định Của St, daily 18:30–23:30), so it appears only as a hedged add-on in the Love Market tip.
- **The Mong Pub & Coffee**: Not selected: a pool table by a wood fire plus cards, chess and checkers (4.9 on Google from 141 reviews, listing open Oct 2026), but it's next door to Ninety Pub and overlaps with it, so it appears in that pick's tip and in tips.
- **Dance Under the Moon & Thiêng (ethnic music-and-dance spectaculars)**: Not selected: the existing Winter Festival pick already covers these mid-November art shows. No 2026 dates yet: Báo Lào Cai's Autumn Festival 2026 report (23 Aug 2026) lists events only up to 25 Sep, and no Winter Festival 2026 programme had appeared by 6 Oct 2026. Show names and 2025 dates are in the tips.
- **La Do Homestay & Coffee**: Not selected: it is next door to Viettrekking Coffee (31 vs 33 Hoàng Liên) with the same Mường Hoa view, and there is no price or 2026 evidence, so it adds nothing distinct.
- **Sapa Sky View Restaurant & Bar**: Not selected: a hotel-priced 7th-floor view space (Châu Long Hotel, 24 Đồng Lợi; Mytour, updated 15 Sep 2026, lists 07:00–22:30). Absinthe already covers evening view drinks and four view cafés cover mornings, and reviewers say you pay for the view rather than the drinks.

## Phú Quốc

*Phú Quốc · island in the Gulf of Thailand, far south*

Phú Quốc's west coast faces the sunset, so the island's day pivots on golden hour: salt coffee or cold brew on Sao Mai Hill, a bespoke cocktail or a 19th-floor Kraken-themed rooftop at dusk, then fire dancers on the sand of Long Beach. After dark the music splits between the Trần Hưng Đạo strip's cover bands and beach DJs and the resort-scale spectacles of Sunset Town and Grand World, where fireworks go off every night. It isn't an all-night clubbing island and specialty coffee is scarce, but sea-view cafés, a few genuinely inventive cocktail bars and the island's own sim-berry wine make up for it.

### Phú Quốc: Cafés (Cà phê)

#### Sunset & sea-view cafés

- **★ Chuồn Chuồn Bistro & Sky Bar** · Dương Đông – Sao Mai Hill (above Long Beach) · $$ · *checked 2026*
  - Terraces wrapped around a big shade tree on Sao Mai Hill look over Dương Đông's rooftops, Long Beach and the Gulf of Thailand, making this the island's signature view café from morning coffee to sunset cocktails. The coffee is more serious than at most view spots, with an Arabica-blend espresso, house cold brews and all the Vietnamese classics.
  - Tip: Arrive around 4:30pm for a west-facing table on the top deck; weekdays are calmer. Order the Chuồn Chuồn cold brew (tangerine and sage honey, 95k) or the house salt coffee (85k), then switch to a cocktail as the sun drops. The access road is steep, so take a Grab car rather than a motorbike at night or in rain. Zyzu Kafé on the same hill has the same sweep of sea for 40–80k a drink.
  - Hours: Daily 7:30–23:00
  - Address: 69 Trần Hưng Đạo (Sao Mai Hill), Dương Đông, Phú Quốc
  - Price: Phin coffee 70–80k, salt coffee 85k, cold brew 95k, coconut coffee 110k, cocktails from about 180k VND; service and VAT included
  - [Google Maps](https://www.google.com/maps/search/?api=1&query=Chu%E1%BB%93n%20Chu%E1%BB%93n%20Bistro%20%26%20Sky%20Bar%2C%2069%20Tr%E1%BA%A7n%20H%C6%B0ng%20%C4%90%E1%BA%A1o%2C%20D%C6%B0%C6%A1ng%20%C4%90%C3%B4ng%2C%20Ph%C3%BA%20Qu%E1%BB%91c) · [Source](https://chuonchuonbistrobar.com/)
- **Buddy Ice Cream & Cafe** · Dương Đông – Bạch Đằng waterfront (by Dinh Cậu and the night market) · $$ · *checked 2026*
  - A long-running, Australian-owned meeting point on the harbour front that serves homemade ice cream, coconut coffee and toasted Vegemite-and-cheese sandwiches, with free wifi and staff who double as an informal tourist desk.
  - Tip: Mornings are best, with the sea breeze and less sun; hot food stops at 6pm, but ice cream and drinks run until 10pm. It's open-air, so expect some smoke from nearby tables. For a cheaper local coffee facing the Dinh Cậu shrine, Coffee Phố Biển is a few doors along at 2 Bạch Đằng.
  - Hours: Daily 6:30–22:00
  - Address: 06 Bạch Đằng, Khu phố 2, Dương Đông, Phú Quốc
  - Price: Menu 20k–145k VND
  - [Google Maps](https://www.google.com/maps/search/?api=1&query=Buddy%20Ice%20Cream%20%26%20Cafe%2C%206%20B%E1%BA%A1ch%20%C4%90%E1%BA%B1ng%2C%20D%C6%B0%C6%A1ng%20%C4%90%C3%B4ng%2C%20Ph%C3%BA%20Qu%E1%BB%91c) · [Source](https://www.wanderlog.com/place/details/4990410/buddy-ice-cream--cafe)
- **Cà Phê Bàng** · Dương Đông – Trần Phú seafront (north of the river mouth) · $ · *checked 2026*
  - A breezy, white-painted local café with a sailing-boat mural on the seafront road north of town. It has a clear sunset view and none of the Long Beach strip's bustle, and it's friendly and cheap.
  - Tip: Order the salt coffee (cà phê muối), an avocado smoothie or the kumquat yogurt. It opens at 6:30am, so it works for a quiet early coffee as well as for sunset.
  - Hours: Daily 6:30–22:30 (Sun to 23:00)
  - Address: 225 Trần Phú, Dương Đông, Phú Quốc
  - Price: Local prices
  - [Google Maps](https://www.google.com/maps/search/?api=1&query=C%C3%A0%20Ph%C3%AA%20B%C3%A0ng%2C%20225%20Tr%E1%BA%A7n%20Ph%C3%BA%2C%20D%C6%B0%C6%A1ng%20%C4%90%C3%B4ng%2C%20Ph%C3%BA%20Qu%E1%BB%91c) · [Source](https://wanderlog.com/place/details/6332786)

#### Coffee & local hangouts

- **★ Tarapaca Cafe** · Dương Đông – Long Beach strip (Trần Hưng Đạo) · *checked 2026*
  - The best all-rounder for coffee lovers on the Long Beach strip: a calm, softly lit room built around Vietnamese styles, from phin filter and bạc xỉu to salt coffee, a coconut range and the house Paca's Egg Coffee. Freelancers praise its tables with power outlets, and it holds 4.7★ from about 740 Google reviews.
  - Tip: Order Paca's Egg Coffee or a salt coffee with a tuna sandwich or a waffle, and come in the morning for a laptop session before the beach. Its website says 8am–10pm but Google lists 9am–6pm, so check before a late visit.
  - Hours: 8:00–22:00 per its website (Google lists 9:00–18:00)
  - Address: 106A Trần Hưng Đạo, Dương Đông, Phú Quốc
  - Price: No prices published online
  - [Google Maps](https://www.google.com/maps/search/?api=1&query=Tarapaca%20Cafe%2C%20106A%20Tr%E1%BA%A7n%20H%C6%B0ng%20%C4%90%E1%BA%A1o%2C%20D%C6%B0%C6%A1ng%20%C4%90%C3%B4ng%2C%20Ph%C3%BA%20Qu%E1%BB%91c) · [Source](https://tarapacacafe.vn/)
- **Mix Bakery Coffee & Tea** · Dương Đông – town centre (Đường 30/4) · $ · *checked 2026*
  - A three-storey garden café of greenery and mini fountains where young locals meet over cupcakes and cheap drinks. On weeknight evenings it hosts music sessions, from requests and themed nights to bolero and Vietnamese evergreens.
  - Tip: Climb to the hidden garden floor for a coffee and a cupcake, and ask the staff which evenings have music, since no schedule is published.
  - Music: Regular weeknight music evenings: requests, themed nights, bolero and Vietnamese evergreen songs (Sun Group, June 2026); nights and start times not published
  - Hours: Daily 7:00–23:00
  - Address: 156 Đường 30/4, Khu phố 1, Dương Đông, Phú Quốc
  - Price: Drinks 30k–65k VND
  - [Google Maps](https://www.google.com/maps/search/?api=1&query=Mix%20Bakery%20Coffee%20%26%20Tea%2C%20156%20%C4%90%C6%B0%E1%BB%9Dng%2030%20Th%C3%A1ng%204%2C%20D%C6%B0%C6%A1ng%20%C4%90%C3%B4ng%2C%20Ph%C3%BA%20Qu%E1%BB%91c) · [Source](https://www.wanderlog.com/place/details/2509938/mix-bakery-coffee--tea)

#### Bakeries & breakfast

- **Eric Kayser Phu Quoc (Maison Kayser)** · Sunset Town (An Thới, south island) · *checked 2026*
  - The Paris baker's first Vietnamese branch, opened with Sun Group in November 2025, makes long-fermented, naturally leavened bread and proper butter croissants and pain au chocolat. Its outdoor tables face the sea and the Kiss Bridge.
  - Tip: Come between 7:30 and 9am, when the bread is just out of the oven, for a croissant or pain au chocolat with a cappuccino, or a baguette with egg and cheese. Prices are above local cafés. For a sea-facing seat before the evening fireworks, RuNam's café in the Hexagon building (100–250k a drink) is the other polished option.
  - Hours: 7:30–23:00 (Sun Group, March 2026)
  - Address: Sunset Bazaar street, near Pearl Square, Sunset Town, An Thới, Phú Quốc
  - Price: Above local café prices; no menu published online
  - [Google Maps](https://www.google.com/maps/search/?api=1&query=Eric%20Kayser%2C%20Sunset%20Town%2C%20An%20Th%E1%BB%9Bi%2C%20Ph%C3%BA%20Qu%E1%BB%91c) · [Source](https://visitphuquoc.com.vn/en/eric-kayser-phu-quoc-authentic-french-bakery-worth-trying-9740)
- **Hardy's German Bakery & Cafe** · Dương Đông – Long Beach strip (Trần Hưng Đạo) · $$ · *checked 2026*
  - Hardy, Gai and their family bake the strip's real bread, with crusty rolls, homemade baguettes, European pastries and proper coffee: a welcome break from sweet condensed-milk coffee.
  - Tip: Come for breakfast and order a bread-roll sandwich or a chicken bánh mì on the house baguette. There's no air-con, so go early.
  - Hours: Daily 7:30–22:00
  - Address: 141 Trần Hưng Đạo, Khu phố 7, Dương Đông, Phú Quốc (small entrance near King Kong Mart)
  - Price: Sandwiches about 50k–80k VND
  - [Google Maps](https://www.google.com/maps/search/?api=1&query=Hardy%27s%20German%20Bakery%20%26%20Cafe%2C%20141%20Tr%E1%BA%A7n%20H%C6%B0ng%20%C4%90%E1%BA%A1o%2C%20D%C6%B0%C6%A1ng%20%C4%90%C3%B4ng%2C%20Ph%C3%BA%20Qu%E1%BB%91c) · [Source](https://wanderlog.com/place/details/2677365)

### Phú Quốc: Bars (Quán bar)

#### Cocktail bars & speakeasies

- **★ Bittersweet & Co (Bittersweet Hidden Cocktail Bar)** · Cửa Lấp – Long Beach strip (south of Dương Đông) · *checked 2026*
  - The island's first hidden cocktail bar, opened in 2016, is still its best serious one, according to a May 2026 guide. You enter through a chocolate shop, there's no menu, and the bartenders build each drink around your taste with Vietnamese spirits and island ingredients such as pepper, tamarind and even fish sauce.
  - Tip: Name a spirit and a flavour direction (sour, spicy, herbal) and let the bartender riff. Book online for slots between 7pm and 11:30pm. It also runs bartending classes and a Vietnamese-spirits workshop that tastes 8 traditional and modern liquors. Food is only nuts and popcorn, so eat first, and ignore the old 16 Lê Lợi address in some listicles.
  - Music: No live music; quiet and conversational
  - Hours: Daily 18:00–01:00 (online booking slots 19:00–23:30)
  - Address: 185 Trần Hưng Đạo, Cửa Lấp, Phú Quốc (by Salinda Resort)
  - Price: No printed menu or online price list; ask prices when you order. Guides call it good value for the quality.
  - [Google Maps](https://www.google.com/maps/search/?api=1&query=Bittersweet%20Hidden%20Cocktail%20Bar%2C%20185%20Tr%E1%BA%A7n%20H%C6%B0ng%20%C4%90%E1%BA%A1o%2C%20C%E1%BB%ADa%20L%E1%BA%A5p%2C%20Ph%C3%BA%20Qu%E1%BB%91c) · [Source](https://bittersweet.bar/bittersweet-hidden-cocktail-bar/)
- **Origin 1982 Cocktail Bar** · Dương Đông – Long Beach strip (Trần Hưng Đạo) · $$$ · *checked 2026*
  - Billed as Phú Quốc's first Vietnamese-inspired speakeasy, it fills a 1982 wooden house up an unlit drive off the main road with dark wood, red lanterns and a patio. Reviewers rate it 4.9★ from about 650 Google reviews and praise mixologist Ruby's creations, from anchovy-infused cocktails that nod to the island's fish-sauce trade to gins from different Vietnamese regions.
  - Tip: Look for the dark driveway off Trần Hưng Đạo. Happy hour runs 6:30–8:30pm. Ask for the anchovy cocktail or an off-menu creation, and pair it with Bittersweet further down the same road.
  - Hours: Daily 7:30am–midnight per Google (coffee by day, cocktails in the evening); VinWonders (Dec 2025) gives 18:00–01:00
  - Address: 149 Trần Hưng Đạo, Khu phố 7, Dương Đông, Phú Quốc (in the Cội Nguồn museum grounds)
  - Price: Reviewers cite about US$10–11 per cocktail (≈260–290k VND)
  - [Google Maps](https://www.google.com/maps/search/?api=1&query=Origin%201982%2C%20149%20Tr%E1%BA%A7n%20H%C6%B0ng%20%C4%90%E1%BA%A1o%2C%20D%C6%B0%C6%A1ng%20%C4%90%C3%B4ng%2C%20Ph%C3%BA%20Qu%E1%BB%91c) · [Source](https://wanderlog.com/place/details/7495332/origin-1982)

#### Rooftops & resort bars

- **★ INK 360 (InterContinental Phu Quoc)** · Bãi Trường (south Long Beach) – Phu Quoc Marina · $$$$ · *checked 2026*
  - The island's highest rooftop bar is Ashley Sutton's fantasy of Kraken tentacles curling around the bar on the 19th floor, with a 360-degree view straight into the Long Beach sunset. Signature cocktails use local ingredients, champagne is poured by the glass, and a May 2026 guide rates it the best rooftop on Phú Quốc.
  - Tip: Book a sunset table on TableCheck or call +84 297 397 8888, and arrive at the 5pm opening because the rail tables fill first. Ask when booking about any sunset-hours entry charge for non-guests. Hours can change with hotel occupancy or private events. Sailing Club next door has its circus at 8pm and fire show at 9pm.
  - Music: Live DJs and themed evenings per the hotel; a May 2026 guide calls the DJ nights occasional, so check ahead.
  - Hours: Daily 17:00–23:00 (may vary with hotel occupancy)
  - Address: 19th floor, InterContinental Phu Quoc Long Beach Resort, Bãi Trường, Dương Tơ, Phú Quốc
  - Price: Cocktails from about US$15 (≈390k VND) per a 2026 guide; luxury-hotel prices
  - [Google Maps](https://www.google.com/maps/search/?api=1&query=INK%20360%2C%20InterContinental%20Phu%20Quoc%20Long%20Beach%20Resort%2C%20B%C3%A3i%20Tr%C6%B0%E1%BB%9Dng%2C%20Ph%C3%BA%20Qu%E1%BB%91c) · [Source](https://phuquoc.intercontinental.com/dining/ink-360/)
- **Department of Chemistry Bar (JW Marriott Phu Quoc Emerald Bay)** · Bãi Khem (far south, near An Thới) · *checked 2026*
  - Bill Bensley's whimsical, university-themed resort hides a bar styled as a fully equipped chemistry lab, with lab glassware, a periodic table on the ceiling, peanuts in test tubes and 'chemists' mixing potions in front of you. Day beds look out over Emerald Bay.
  - Tip: Non-guests should book on SevenRooms or call +84 29 7377 9999. Come for the late-afternoon light and stay for the evening music set; staff will adjust a cocktail's strength and sweetness if you ask. It's in the same far-south corner as Sunset Town, so it pairs well with the 9pm show.
  - Music: Live music in the evening, about 18:30–21:00 (review summary)
  - Hours: Daily 16:00–24:00 (official site; Google lists 12:00–23:00)
  - Address: JW Marriott Phu Quoc Emerald Bay Resort & Spa, Bãi Khem, An Thới, Phú Quốc
  - Price: Five-star resort pricing; menu prices not published online
  - [Google Maps](https://www.google.com/maps/search/?api=1&query=Department%20of%20Chemistry%20Bar%2C%20JW%20Marriott%20Phu%20Quoc%20Emerald%20Bay%2C%20B%C3%A3i%20Khem%2C%20Ph%C3%BA%20Qu%E1%BB%91c) · [Source](https://www.marriott.com/en-us/hotels/pqcjw-jw-marriott-phu-quoc-emerald-bay-resort-and-spa/dining/)
- **Le Bar (La Veranda Resort Phu Quoc – MGallery)** · Dương Đông – Long Beach (Trần Hưng Đạo) · $$$ · *checked 2026*
  - A 1920s Indochine-style bar of vintage armchairs and sunset sea views, set to soulful jazz and blues, and the easiest place to taste Phú Quốc's rượu sim (rose-myrtle berry) in a cocktail: the house steeps its own sim rhum arrangé.
  - Tip: Order the Signature Phú Quốc Sim Berry Martini (275k), the Peppercorn Martini made with Phú Quốc pepper (275k) or the alcohol-free Sim Haven with sim honey and hibiscus (180k). Come for sunset; the resort's number is +84 297 3982 988.
  - Music: Soulful jazz and blues soundtrack and 'live entertainment' per the resort; no schedule published
  - Hours: Daily 11:00–22:00
  - Address: La Veranda Resort, Trần Hưng Đạo, Khu phố 7, Dương Đông, Phú Quốc
  - Price: Signature cocktails 265–275k VND, mocktails ~180k, Saigon beer 90k, Kronenbourg draught 160k; plus 5% service charge and VAT (Dec 2025 menu)
  - [Google Maps](https://www.google.com/maps/search/?api=1&query=Le%20Bar%2C%20La%20Veranda%20Resort%2C%20Tr%E1%BA%A7n%20H%C6%B0ng%20%C4%90%E1%BA%A1o%2C%20D%C6%B0%C6%A1ng%20%C4%90%C3%B4ng%2C%20Ph%C3%BA%20Qu%E1%BB%91c) · [Source](https://laverandaresorts.com/restaurants-bars/le-bar/)
- **Francesca Rooftop & Luna Folk (La Festa Phu Quoc, Curio Collection by Hilton)** · Sunset Town (An Thới, south island) · $$$$ · *checked 2026*
  - Two of four venues added in 2026 to the hotel's new art wing. Francesca is a two-level pool bar and sunset rooftop deck, billed as the highest in Sunset Town, looking over Kiss Bridge, the bay and the nightly fireworks; Luna Folk is an intimate speakeasy and jazz bar.
  - Tip: Francesca has a VND 700,000 per-adult minimum spend, so treat it as your sunset-to-fireworks seat and book on TableCheck. Finish with a nightcap at Luna Folk.
  - Music: Luna Folk is a jazz bar; the hotel's launch announcement mentions a live saxophonist. No schedule published.
  - Hours: Francesca daily 11:00–22:00
  - Address: New art wing, La Festa Phu Quoc, Curio Collection by Hilton, Sunset Town, An Thới, Phú Quốc
  - Price: Francesca: minimum spend VND 700,000 per adult
  - [Google Maps](https://www.google.com/maps/search/?api=1&query=La%20Festa%20Phu%20Quoc%2C%20Curio%20Collection%20by%20Hilton%2C%20Sunset%20Town%2C%20An%20Th%E1%BB%9Bi%2C%20Ph%C3%BA%20Qu%E1%BB%91c) · [Source](https://www.ttgasia.com/2026/09/11/la-festa-phu-quoc-unveils-more-dining-charms/)

#### Beach clubs & sunset bars

- **★ Sailing Club Phu Quoc** · Bãi Trường (south Long Beach) – Phu Quoc Marina, next to the InterContinental · $$$ · *checked 2026*
  - Long Beach's stylish all-day beach club has a rim-less infinity pool with a swim-up bar, bamboo architecture and a crowd that gathers on the sand at sunset. After dark comes the nightly line-up: a circus act at 8pm, fire dancing at 9pm, then a DJ.
  - Tip: Come around 4:30pm for a pool-edge lounger and stay for the shows. Friday is Ladies' Night (8–10pm, free selected cocktails), and the club runs monthly specials, such as Oktoberfest and a Taco Day night in early October 2026. It now closes at 10pm (old guides say 2am), so start here and finish on the Trần Hưng Đạo strip, 15–20 minutes north by car.
  - Music: Nightly circus 20:00, fire show 21:00 and DJ session from 21:30 (mainly tropical house); Ladies' Night every Friday 20:00–22:00
  - Hours: Daily 06:30–22:00
  - Address: Lot B7, Bãi Trường Complex, Dương Tơ, Phú Quốc
  - Price: Vietnamese-rum cocktails from 150k++ VND (++ means service charge and VAT are added)
  - [Google Maps](https://www.google.com/maps/search/?api=1&query=Sailing%20Club%20Phu%20Quoc%2C%20Lot%20B7%20B%C3%A3i%20Tr%C6%B0%E1%BB%9Dng%2C%20D%C6%B0%C6%A1ng%20T%C6%A1%2C%20Ph%C3%BA%20Qu%E1%BB%91c) · [Source](https://sailingclubphuquoc.com/)
- **On The Rock at Mango Bay** · Ông Lang beach (Cửa Dương), north of Dương Đông · $$$ · *checked 2026*
  - Mango Bay eco-resort's open-air restaurant-bar sits on the rocks at the ocean's edge, facing west over open sea. It's the island's most romantic, unhurried sunset, with no party music and few crowds.
  - Tip: Book a front table for a sunset cocktail and stay for dinner; call the resort on +84 96 968 1820 about walk-ins. From November to April the seasonal Beach Bar adds barefoot drinks and table barbecue on the sand. Ông Lang has no bar strip, so plan to stay put for the evening.
  - Hours: Daily from 11:00 to about 22:00 (one 2026 guide says 23:00)
  - Address: Mango Bay Resort, Alley 8 Lê Thúc Nha, Ông Lang beach, Cửa Dương, Phú Quốc
  - Price: Dishes about 190–520k VND, excluding VAT and service (Aug 2026 menu); drinks list not published online
  - [Google Maps](https://www.google.com/maps/search/?api=1&query=On%20The%20Rock%2C%20Mango%20Bay%20Resort%2C%20%C3%94ng%20Lang%2C%20Ph%C3%BA%20Qu%E1%BB%91c) · [Source](https://mangobayphuquoc.com/en/dining/)

#### Craft beer

- **East West Brewing Taproom Phu Quoc** · Long Beach (Bãi Trường), about 10 minutes from the airport · *checked 2026*
  - Saigon's award-winning East West Brewing opened this taproom on the sand in January 2026, the best place on the island for proper craft pints with a sunset. It launched its easy-drinking Good Times Lager here, brewed for the tropical heat.
  - Tip: Book a table on the brewery's website and come for sunset; try the Good Times Lager (4.2%) alongside the core range. The brewery plans to add an on-site brewhouse here.
  - Hours: Daily 15:00–23:00
  - Address: Beachfront of Long Beach Resort Phu Quoc (WorldHotels), Bãi Trường, Phú Quốc
  - [Google Maps](https://www.google.com/maps/search/?api=1&query=East%20West%20Brewing%20Taproom%2C%20Long%20Beach%20Resort%20Phu%20Quoc%2C%20Ph%C3%BA%20Qu%E1%BB%91c) · [Source](https://eastwestbrewing.vn/taproom/phu-quoc)
- **Sun Bavaria GastroPub & Phu Quoc Brew House** · Sunset Town (An Thới, south island) · $$ · *checked 2026*
  - A Bavarian-style beer hall of about 5,000 seats that Sun Group says Guinness lists as the world's largest beachfront beer restaurant. It pours Sun KraftBeer from the group's pink three-storey brewhouse nearby, run by world-champion brewer Oliver Wesseloh, and its sea terrace faces the Kiss Bridge and the nightly shows.
  - Tip: Grab an open-air seafront table by about 5pm for sunset, then stay for the shows and fireworks. Try the Kiss Bridge Helles, Sunset Town Dry Hop Pale Ale or Hòn Thơm Paradise Wheat. Book 2–5 days ahead for weekends; dinner-show combos are better value than à la carte. It's big and touristy, so fit it around the shows rather than making a special trip.
  - Music: Per the operator, a European band and dancers perform nightly, plus DJs and dinner shows; direct view of Symphony of the Sea (from November) and the fireworks
  - Address: Seafront facing Kiss Bridge, Sunset Town (Thị trấn Hoàng Hôn), An Thới, Phú Quốc
  - Price: Craft beer about 80–120k VND per 330 ml, 250–350k per 1-litre pitcher; dinner shows with free-flow beer from about 640k (Sun Group figures)
  - [Google Maps](https://www.google.com/maps/search/?api=1&query=Sun%20Bavaria%20GastroPub%2C%20Sunset%20Town%2C%20An%20Th%E1%BB%9Bi%2C%20Ph%C3%BA%20Qu%E1%BB%91c) · [Source](https://visitphuquoc.com.vn/en/sun-bavaria-gastropub-a-must-visit-culinary-and-entertainment-destination-in-phu-quoc)

#### Beyond Long Beach

- **40six Beach Haus** · Gành Dầu (north-west tip of the island) · $$ · *checked 2026*
  - The far north's standout sunset bar sits on the bay at Gành Dầu, the island's north-west tip, looking across the water towards Cambodia's islands. It grew out of Mun's Cafe & Rum Bar, the rum shack Vietnam Coracle loved (Mun's owner stayed on as a partner), and now pairs signature cocktails and a wide range of speciality beers with Thai-Italian food such as wood-fired pizza and pasta.
  - Tip: Arrive around 4:30pm to swim first (Sasa Eco Diving runs dives from the venue) and claim a sea-facing table for sunset; reviewers recommend the loaded fries, miso coco pasta and triple-tomato pizza. It's closed on Mondays. Grand World is nearby in the north, so you can go on to its 8:15pm Tinh Hoa show; otherwise arrange your ride back to Dương Đông before dark. Older guides still call it Mun's.
  - Music: DJ shows and sunset sessions during its October–April event season (own website; no dates posted)
  - Hours: Tue–Sun 10:00–22:00; closed Monday
  - Address: Tổ 01, Ấp Gành Dầu, xã Gành Dầu, Phú Quốc (beachfront off ĐT45)
  - Price: No menu prices published as text online; one Aug 2026 listing gives 50–200k VND per item. Prices exclude a 5% service charge (own website), and reviewers find them slightly high but worth it for the view
  - [Google Maps](https://www.google.com/maps/search/?api=1&query=40six%20Beach%20Haus%2C%20G%C3%A0nh%20D%E1%BA%A7u%2C%20Ph%C3%BA%20Qu%E1%BB%91c) · [Source](https://40sixbeachhaus.com/)
- **PH Rooftop Bar & Lounge (Phụng Hưng Boutique Hotel)** (PH Rooftop Bar & Lounge – Khách sạn Phụng Hưng Boutique) · An Thới town (harbour), south island · $$ · *checked 2026*
  - A hotel rooftop above An Thới, the island's busy southern port, with views over the harbour, the southern islands and the Hòn Thơm cable car. Vietnam Coracle called it an unexpected find, and a May 2026 guide rates it a solid choice in a corner of the island with few rooftops. It's a calm, mid-priced alternative to Sunset Town's resort bars.
  - Tip: Thursday is music night (7:30–9:30pm, 20k VND cover, free for children under 130 cm). It opens at 6:30am for coffee, handy before the Hòn Thơm cable car or an island-hopping boat from An Thới, and makes an easy sunset drink on the way back.
  - Music: Weekly music night every Thursday 19:30–21:30 (20k VND per person); style not stated
  - Hours: Daily 06:30–23:00
  - Address: 5th floor, Phụng Hưng Boutique Hotel, 60–62 Nguyễn Văn Cừ, An Thới, Phú Quốc
  - Price: Food and drinks 39–210k VND per item (mia.vn, June 2026); Thursday music night adds 20k per person
  - [Google Maps](https://www.google.com/maps/search/?api=1&query=PH%20Rooftop%20Bar%20%26%20Lounge%2C%2060%20Nguy%E1%BB%85n%20V%C4%83n%20C%E1%BB%AB%2C%20An%20Th%E1%BB%9Bi%2C%20Ph%C3%BA%20Qu%E1%BB%91c) · [Source](https://mia.vn/cam-nang-du-lich/ph-rooftop-bar-lounge-dang-cap-nha-hang-dao-ngoc-sang-trong-84)
- **Istanbul Beach Club (Bãi Khem)** (Istanbul Bãi Kem) · Bãi Khem (far south, near An Thới) · *checked 2026*
  - A Turkish-themed, independent beach club on the white sand of Bãi Khem, the resort-lined bay in the far south, with loungers on the beach and an indoor bar behind. It serves kebabs, mezze and pizza with juices and cocktails from breakfast until nearly midnight, and is rated 4.8★ from about 3,900 Google reviews.
  - Tip: Use it as a base for an afternoon swim (reviewers like the pizzas, chicken skewers and mango juice), and ask whether loungers are free with an order. Bãi Khem faces south-east, so there's no sea sunset here: ride on to Sunset Town, a short trip away, for golden hour and the 9pm Kiss of the Sea.
  - Hours: Daily 08:00–23:45 (Google); Sun Group's June 2026 list says until 23:30
  - Address: Lô 06, Bãi Khem, An Thới, Phú Quốc
  - Price: No menu prices published online
  - [Google Maps](https://www.google.com/maps/search/?api=1&query=Istanbul%20Bai%20Kem%2C%20B%C3%A3i%20Khem%2C%20An%20Th%E1%BB%9Bi%2C%20Ph%C3%BA%20Qu%E1%BB%91c) · [Source](https://sunparadiseland.com/SunParadiseLandPhuQuoc/tin-tuc/goi-y-top-10-quan-bar-phu-quoc-chill-cho-du-khach-20367)

### Phú Quốc: Music (Âm nhạc)

#### Big shows & fireworks

- **★ Kiss of the Sea** (Nụ Hôn Của Biển Cả) · Sunset Town (An Thới, south island) · $$$$ · *checked 2026*
  - Phú Quốc's signature night out: about 30 minutes of projections on walls of water, lasers, fire and 60+ international performers in a roughly 5,000-seat theatre built over the sea, ending in a fireworks finale. Reviewers find the story hard to follow but rate the fireworks as the highlight.
  - Tip: Skip Tuesdays, when only a shorter lights-and-fireworks version runs. Seats within a ticket tier are first-come, so arrive 30–60 minutes early and aim for central, raised rows. Read the storyline first (a local boy meets a girl from another world), and book online at booking.sunworld.vn or via OTAs, which usually beats the gate price. Walk the Kiss Bridge at golden hour beforehand (50–100k VND, often included in show tickets).
  - Music: Music-driven spectacle synced with water projections, lasers and fire, with 60+ dancers and acrobats and a ~5-minute fireworks finale around 21:30; Sunset Town fires fireworks twice nightly
  - Hours: Nightly 21:00 (about 30 minutes plus fireworks); no full show on Tuesdays
  - Address: Open-air sea theatre at the end of Kiss Bridge (Cầu Hôn), Sunset Town, An Thới, Phú Quốc
  - Price: About 750,000–1,000,000 VND by seat zone (reference price 950,000); children under 1 m free; combos with Symphony of the Sea from about 1,200,000 VND
  - [Google Maps](https://www.google.com/maps/search/?api=1&query=Kiss%20of%20the%20Sea%2C%20Sunset%20Town%2C%20An%20Th%E1%BB%9Bi%2C%20Ph%C3%BA%20Qu%E1%BB%91c) · [Source](https://localvietnam.com/phu-quoc/kiss-of-the-sea/)
- **Symphony of the Sea** (Bản Giao Hưởng Đại Dương) · Sunset Town (An Thới, south island) · $$$ · *checked 2026*
  - A fast, story-free stunt show on the bay: jet-skis and flyboarders tear around to music while lasers flash and fireworks go off from the water itself. Season 3, due in November 2026, gets a 200 m stage, 14 jet-skis, 8 flyboarders and three times the fireworks, designed with the 2025 and 2026 champions of the Da Nang fireworks festival.
  - Tip: It's seasonal: Season 3 is expected to open in November 2026, but no exact date had been announced by 5 October, so check before an October or early-November trip. Cheaper tickets mean standing on Kiss Bridge; for a seat, take the beach-club 'Chill Show' package or a dinner show at Sun Bavaria. Wind and rain affect it more than Kiss of the Sea. Arrive 30 minutes early, since Sunset Town's entrances are confusing, then walk on to Kiss of the Sea at 9pm.
  - Music: Jet-ski and flyboard stunts set to international music, with lasers and about 1,000 fireworks including water fireworks; 20–30 minutes from about 19:45 in season
  - Hours: In season nightly around 19:45; Season 3 expected from November 2026 (exact date not announced)
  - Address: The bay in front of Kiss Bridge (Cầu Hôn), Sunset Town, An Thới; watched from Kiss Bridge, Sunset Beach or Sun Bavaria GastroPub
  - Price: 2025–26 season: standing tickets about 550–660k VND, beach-club Chill Show about 650k, Sun Bavaria dinner show about 850–950k; under 1 m free; Season 3 prices not yet published
  - [Google Maps](https://www.google.com/maps/search/?api=1&query=Kiss%20Bridge%20%28C%E1%BA%A7u%20H%C3%B4n%29%2C%20Sunset%20Town%2C%20An%20Th%E1%BB%9Bi%2C%20Ph%C3%BA%20Qu%E1%BB%91c) · [Source](https://en.vietnamplus.vn/phu-quocs-hottest-selling-show-unveils-a-new-version-with-three-times-more-fireworks-post351771.vnp)
- **Tinh Hoa Việt Nam & Charm of Venice (Grand World)** (Tinh Hoa Việt Nam / Sắc Màu Venice) · Grand World, Bãi Dài / Gành Dầu (north island) · $$ · *checked 2026*
  - The north island's big night: Tinh Hoa Việt Nam (Quintessence of Vietnam) puts about 300 performers on an 11,200 m² outdoor stage with 3D projection mapping, telling Vietnamese history and folklore from fishing-village dances to the Nghinh Ông whale-worship festival. Afterwards, the free Charm of Venice show fills Grand World's central lake with dancers, lasers and water effects.
  - Tip: Come early: the Tinh Hoa zone is free 9am–4pm and 6–7pm, with short traditional-music ensemble sets (hòa tấu cổ nhạc) and a royal palanquin procession at 5:15pm. The evening runs Mộc Huyền pre-show 7:35pm, main show 8:15–9pm, lantern release 9pm, then Charm of Venice by the lake at 9:30pm (get a spot by 9:15). Free Vinbus shuttles run from Dương Đông, and Sunset Town is too far away to combine on the same night.
  - Music: Dance and music theatre to a recorded score; free daytime traditional-instrument ensemble sets; Charm of Venice water-music and laser show 21:30–22:00, with a laser show at 22:45
  - Hours: Tinh Hoa Việt Nam nightly 20:15–21:00; Charm of Venice 21:30–22:00; Grand World open 24/7 (schedules may change for weather)
  - Address: Grand World Phú Quốc, Bãi Dài, Gành Dầu, Phú Quốc
  - Price: Tinh Hoa Việt Nam 300,000 VND (≥140 cm), 230,000 VND for 1–1.4 m and over-60s; Charm of Venice free
  - [Google Maps](https://www.google.com/maps/search/?api=1&query=Tinh%20Hoa%20Vi%E1%BB%87t%20Nam%2C%20Grand%20World%20Ph%C3%BA%20Qu%E1%BB%91c%2C%20B%C3%A3i%20D%C3%A0i%2C%20Ph%C3%BA%20Qu%E1%BB%91c) · [Source](https://vinwonders.com/vi/tinh-hoa-viet-nam/)

#### Live bands & pubs

- **★ The Cheeky Traveller English Pub & Bar** · Dương Đông – Long Beach bar strip (Hẻm 118) · *checked 2026*
  - The island's most consistent live-band bar is a friendly, wood-panelled English pub run by Kim and Ken, where the house band plays crowd-pleasing covers every night and guests get pulled up to sing. It holds 4.8★ from about 880 Google reviews, and an April 2026 reviewer called the live music incredible.
  - Tip: Come around 8pm for the full set and use the free pool table between songs; happy hour runs 5–9pm. One guide says it doesn't open every day, so check its Facebook first. Then walk on to Blue Monkey for a later set, catching OCSEN's 10pm fire show on the beach in between.
  - Music: Live bands nightly, about 19:30–23:00: covers from rock to hip hop, with sing-alongs; sports on TV
  - Hours: Daily from about 17:00 (Google says noon) to 02:00; live music about 19:30–23:00
  - Address: Hẻm 118 Trần Hưng Đạo, Khu phố 7, Phú Quốc
  - Price: No current menu online; a 2022 listing quoted budget pub prices (beer about US$1, cocktails about US$2)
  - [Google Maps](https://www.google.com/maps/search/?api=1&query=The%20Cheeky%20Traveller%20English%20Pub%20%26%20Bar%2C%20H%E1%BA%BBm%20118%20Tr%E1%BA%A7n%20H%C6%B0ng%20%C4%90%E1%BA%A1o%2C%20Ph%C3%BA%20Qu%E1%BB%91c) · [Source](http://thecheekytraveller.com/)
- **Blue Monkey Bar** · Dương Đông – Long Beach bar strip (Trần Hưng Đạo) · *checked 2026*
  - One of the strip's most established nightlife spots, with Vietnamese singers and bands most nights, a famous house coconut vodka and a crowd that keeps going later than almost anywhere else nearby. It rates 4.6★ on Google (about 440 reviews) and 4.8 on TripAdvisor, and has a sister bar in Saigon's Thảo Điền.
  - Tip: Arrive after 9pm, when the music starts, and order the coconut vodka or a craft beer on tap; shisha and bar games are there for a late night. Google pins it at no. 116, so navigate by name.
  - Music: Live Vietnamese singers and bands most nights from about 21:00, with guests singing and dancing along; party crowd until 02:00
  - Hours: Daily 18:00–02:00
  - Address: 116 Trần Hưng Đạo, Khu phố 7, Phú Quốc (Google Maps and the bar's 2026 menu page; its site footer still says 114)
  - [Google Maps](https://www.google.com/maps/search/?api=1&query=Blue%20Monkey%20Bar%2C%20114%20Tr%E1%BA%A7n%20H%C6%B0ng%20%C4%90%E1%BA%A1o%2C%20Ph%C3%BA%20Qu%E1%BB%91c) · [Source](https://bluemonkey.vn)

#### Beach parties & DJs

- **OCSEN Beach Bar & Club** (Ốc Sên Beach Bar) · Long Beach – Hẻm 124 Trần Hưng Đạo (south edge of Dương Đông) · $$$ · *checked 2026*
  - Long Beach's best-known sand party has rows of orange beanbags facing the sunset, DJs playing tropical and deep house, and fire dancers on the beach after dark. A May 2026 guide calls it touristy, with mixed reviews on prices and service, but it's still the easiest sunset-to-midnight beach scene on the island.
  - Tip: Arrive 4:30–6pm for a front-row beanbag and book ahead for weekends. The fire show is around 10pm (times vary, so ask at the bar) and goes ahead in light rain. A ticketed NYE party is already on sale for 31 Dec 2026.
  - Music: DJ sets (tropical and deep house) from sunset, louder after dark; nightly fire show around 22:00; occasional acoustic bands
  - Hours: Daily 16:00–01:00
  - Address: Hẻm 124 Trần Hưng Đạo, Dương Tơ, Phú Quốc
  - Price: 2026 menu: cocktails 200–270k VND, beers 65–100k, local craft beer 120k, shisha 440k; plus 8% service
  - [Google Maps](https://www.google.com/maps/search/?api=1&query=OCSEN%20Beach%20Bar%20%26%20Club%2C%20H%E1%BA%BBm%20124%20Tr%E1%BA%A7n%20H%C6%B0ng%20%C4%90%E1%BA%A1o%2C%20Ph%C3%BA%20Qu%E1%BB%91c) · [Source](https://www.ocsenbeachbar.com/shows-events)

#### After midnight

- **House No. 1** · Dương Đông – Bạch Đằng riverfront (night market area) · $$ · *checked 2026*
  - Dương Đông's late-night local bar, a few minutes from the night market, where the evening moves from a live band to DJ sets to karaoke over cocktails and whisky. A May 2026 guide calls it one of the island's most social bars and one of the few places that still feels busy after midnight.
  - Tip: Make it the last stop: eat at the night market, work through the Long Beach strip, then come here once the beach bars close. Navigate to 58 Bạch Đằng, because some listings still show an old address on Lê Lợi.
  - Music: Live band, DJ sets and karaoke on regular nights, building from mellow to rowdy (Znews, Dec 2025)
  - Hours: From 17:00 to about 02:00 (Znews, Dec 2025); other listings give closing times between 01:00 and 03:00
  - Address: 58 Bạch Đằng, Dương Đông, Phú Quốc
  - Price: Drinks from 120k VND (Znews, Dec 2025)
  - [Google Maps](https://www.google.com/maps/search/?api=1&query=House%20No.%201%2C%2058%20B%E1%BA%A1ch%20%C4%90%E1%BA%B1ng%2C%20D%C6%B0%C6%A1ng%20%C4%90%C3%B4ng%2C%20Ph%C3%BA%20Qu%E1%BB%91c) · [Source](https://znews.vn/len-do-goi-cocktail-tai-5-local-bar-o-phu-quoc-post1612131.html)

#### Night markets & festivals

- **VUI-Fest Bazaar (Vui Phết Night Market) & Giòn Bar** (Chợ đêm Vui Phết) · Sunset Town (An Thới, south island) · $$ · *checked 2026*
  - Sunset Town's seaside night market has 100+ food, craft-beer and souvenir stalls, with free roving acts (fire dancing, circus, magic, traditional instruments) and the nightly fireworks overhead. Giòn Bar, one of its bars, pours craft beer and cocktails on live acoustic and DJ nights.
  - Tip: Use it as the base for a south-island night: graze the stalls from about 6pm, watch the free fireworks from the waterfront, and confirm per-kg prices before ordering seafood. Check the boards on arrival for the pots-and-pans 'Loảng Xoảng' percussion show, which ran in past seasons.
  - Music: Free street performances nightly (fire dance, circus, magic, traditional instruments); acoustic and DJ sets at Giòn Bar
  - Hours: Daily 16:00–24:00
  - Address: Seafront promenade, Sunset Town, An Thới, Phú Quốc
  - Price: Free entry; street snacks to premium seafood
  - [Google Maps](https://www.google.com/maps/search/?api=1&query=VUI-Fest%20Bazaar%2C%20Sunset%20Town%2C%20An%20Th%E1%BB%9Bi%2C%20Ph%C3%BA%20Qu%E1%BB%91c) · [Source](https://visitphuquoc.com.vn/en/vui-fest-bazaar)
- **Countdown 2027 & Tết 2027 fireworks (expected)** (Đón năm mới 2027 & Tết Đinh Mùi) · Sunset Town (south); Dương Đông; Grand World & Corona Square (north) · *likely open*
  - The island's biggest party season. Last New Year's Eve brought DJ, fire-show and fireworks parties on the beach in Dương Đông, a public countdown stage at Corona Square, a New Year version of Kiss of the Sea and V-pop stars such as Phương Vy, Hiền Hồ and Suni Hạ Linh; a similar programme is expected for 31 Dec 2026 and Tết, but none has been announced.
  - Tip: As of late September 2026 Sun Group had not published a 31 Dec 2026 programme, so check official channels before booking, and book rooms and flights early: late December and Tết are peak season. Tết falls on 6 Feb 2027 (eve 5 Feb); in past years Sunset Town added extra fireworks over the holiday. OCSEN is already selling tickets for its NYE 2026 party.
  - Music: Expected, not announced. Last season (25 Dec 2025–4 Jan 2026) Sunset Town ran a Beach Music & Fireworks Week with 50+ shows and about 200 international artists, plus DJ beach parties in Dương Đông and V-pop concerts.
  - Hours: Expected: 31 Dec 2026 from the evening to after midnight; Tết eve 5 Feb 2027. No programme announced as of 5 Oct 2026
  - Address: Sunset Town, An Thới (main venue); countdown stages also expected in Dương Đông and on the north island
  - Price: Sunset Town entry is free and the fireworks can be watched from the waterfront; shows and parties are ticketed. Last year's Corona Square countdown (19:00–01:00) was open to the public.
  - [Google Maps](https://www.google.com/maps/search/?api=1&query=Sunset%20Town%2C%20An%20Th%E1%BB%9Bi%2C%20Ph%C3%BA%20Qu%E1%BB%91c) · [Source](https://sunparadiseland.com/SunParadiseLandPhuQuoc/tin-tuc/don-nam-moi-2027-o-phu-quoc-o-dau-dep-goi-y-diem-vui-choi-xem-phao-hoa-29830)

### Phú Quốc: Games (Trò chơi)

Phú Quốc has no gaming centre: we found no esports lounge, VR arcade, laser tag, paintball, escape room or legal shooting range with any 2025–26 presence, only anonymous neighbourhood net cafés, and Timezone has no branch here. Your shooter fix is VinWonders' interactive dark ride and Wild West shooting games; after dark it's pool and darts on the Long Beach strip, or the 24-hour Corona Casino if table games appeal.

#### Theme-park shooting games

- **★ VinWonders Phu Quoc** · Bãi Dài / Gành Dầu (north island), Phú Quốc United Center · $$$$ · *checked 2026*
  - With no esports lounge or laser tag on the island, this is Phú Quốc's real shooter fix: The Dragon's Spell in the Mystical Castle is billed as Vietnam's first interactive dark ride, in which you play a knight battling a fire dragon to rescue the king. Over in Fantasy World, the Western Frontier zone turns you into a sharpshooter in interactive shooting games.
  - Tip: Hit the shooting games and The Dragon's Spell when the themed zones open at 10:00, before queues build; a Game Fastpass (about US$12) skips the line on the top four rides. Height minimums are 110 cm for The Dragon's Spell and 100 cm for The Sheriff Academy and Hustling Games Village. Stay for the Once multimedia show (18:45–19:05) on European Avenue, then cross to Grand World or the Corona Casino next door.
  - Hours: Daily 09:00–19:30; most themed zones 10:00–17:30/18:00; Once show 18:45–19:05
  - Address: Bãi Dài, Gành Dầu, Phú Quốc, An Giang
  - Price: Adult (over 140 cm) 950,000 VND; children 1–1.4 m and over-60s 710,000 VND (official guide, Feb 2026; another official page quotes up to 1,050,000 VND for adults, so check when booking). Game Fastpass for the top four rides about US$12.
  - [Google Maps](https://www.google.com/maps/search/?api=1&query=VinWonders%20Ph%C3%BA%20Qu%E1%BB%91c%2C%20B%C3%A3i%20D%C3%A0i%2C%20G%C3%A0nh%20D%E1%BA%A7u%2C%20Ph%C3%BA%20Qu%E1%BB%91c) · [Source](https://vinwonders.com/en/wonderpedia/news/complete-guide-to-the-vinwonders-phu-quoc-itinerary/)

#### Pool, darts & bar games

- **The Rabbit Hole Irish Sports Bar** · Dương Đông – Long Beach bar strip (Hẻm 118 Trần Hưng Đạo) · *checked 2026*
  - Phú Quốc's best bet for bar games: two professional pool tables, darts, foosball, board games and a two-player classic arcade cabinet, with the big matches on screen and Irish stout and Magners cider on draught alongside craft beers and cocktails. Live bands play several nights a week, so it covers games, drinks and music in one stop.
  - Tip: Come for the 15:00–19:00 happy hour (buy two, get one free on draught beer, cocktails and wine by the glass), claim a pool table early, then stay for the band from 19:30. If both tables are taken, the Cheeky Traveller in the same alley (see Music) has a free pool table between sets.
  - Music: Live music Thursday–Saturday in low season and nightly in high season (December–April), per the bar's site; its October 2026 listings show bands Wednesday–Friday from 19:30.
  - Hours: Daily 10:00–02:00
  - Address: 2/1/118 Trần Hưng Đạo, Khu phố 7, Phú Quốc
  - Price: No published prices for pool, darts or drinks; happy hour 15:00–19:00 is buy two, get one free on draught beer, cocktails and wine by the glass.
  - [Google Maps](https://www.google.com/maps/search/?api=1&query=The%20Rabbit%20Hole%20Irish%20Bar%2C%202/1/118%20Tr%E1%BA%A7n%20H%C6%B0ng%20%C4%90%E1%BA%A1o%2C%20Ph%C3%BA%20Qu%E1%BB%91c) · [Source](https://www.therabbitholeirishbar.com/phu-quoc/)

#### Casino

- **Corona Casino Phu Quoc** · Bãi Dài / Gành Dầu (north island), Phú Quốc United Center · *checked 2026*
  - Gambling rather than gaming, but it's the island's only game room that never closes: a licensed 24-hour casino beside VinWonders with baccarat, roulette, blackjack, Texas Hold'em and three-card poker tables, slots and electronic gaming stations.
  - Tip: Foreigners must be 18+, register with a valid passport and pay no entry fee; Vietnamese citizens must be 21+, buy an hourly or monthly entry ticket and prove an income above 10 million VND a month, under a pilot scheme whose future is under review. Dress smart-casual (no swimwear, sleepwear or tank tops), set a budget, and treat it as a late-night add-on once VinWonders closes at 19:30.
  - Hours: 24/7
  - Address: Corona Resort & Casino, Phú Quốc United Center, Bãi Dài, Gành Dầu, Phú Quốc
  - Price: Free entry for foreigners; Vietnamese buy an hourly or monthly entry ticket. Table minimums aren't published.
  - [Google Maps](https://www.google.com/maps/search/?api=1&query=Corona%20Casino%2C%20Ph%C3%BA%20Qu%E1%BB%91c%20United%20Center%2C%20B%C3%A3i%20D%C3%A0i%2C%20G%C3%A0nh%20D%E1%BA%A7u%2C%20Ph%C3%BA%20Qu%E1%BB%91c) · [Source](https://vinwonders.com/vi/wonderpedia/news/casino-phu-quoc-diem-giai-tri-dang-cap-xuyen-ngay-dem-co-gi/)

### Phú Quốc: Books & literary (Sách & văn chương)

We found no independent or English-language bookshop or book café on the island, so pack your own reading. Phú Quốc's literary weight is in its history: the Coconut Tree Prison, chronicled by war novelist Chu Lai; the shrine to Nguyễn Trung Trực, whose last words are among the most quoted in Vietnamese history; and a founder-run museum with a small multilingual archive.

#### Literary landmarks

- **★ Phu Quoc Prison (Coconut Tree Prison) Historical Site** (Di tích Nhà tù Phú Quốc (Nhà lao Cây Dừa)) · An Thới (south island) · $ · *checked 2026*
  - Once the South's largest POW camp, holding more than 32,000 prisoners at its peak, this is the island's one site with real literary weight: war novelist Chu Lai, author of 'Mưa đỏ' (Red Rain), chronicled it in his ký sự 'Nhà lao Cây Dừa'. You walk through the rebuilt B2 compound with its barbed-wire 'tiger cages' and graphic tableaux of more than 40 tortures, a hall of 43 original artefacts and 100-plus photos, and the prisoners' cemetery on Hill 100.
  - Tip: Arrive at the 07:30 opening to beat the heat, and hire an on-site guide (optional) for the prisoners' stories, including the 1969 escape when men in Zone B2 tunnelled out with spoons and tins. Read Chu Lai's 'Nhà lao Cây Dừa' (NXB Văn học, 2004; Vietnamese only) before you go. The torture scenes are graphic, so think twice with young children; dress modestly and bring water and a hat.
  - Hours: Daily 07:30–11:00 and 13:30–17:00 (some guides say the morning session runs to 11:30)
  - Address: 350 Nguyễn Văn Cừ, An Thới, Phú Quốc
  - Price: Free entry; optional on-site guide about 100,000–200,000 VND per group
  - [Google Maps](https://www.google.com/maps/search/?api=1&query=Nh%C3%A0%20t%C3%B9%20Ph%C3%BA%20Qu%E1%BB%91c%2C%20350%20Nguy%E1%BB%85n%20V%C4%83n%20C%E1%BB%AB%2C%20An%20Th%E1%BB%9Bi%2C%20Ph%C3%BA%20Qu%E1%BB%91c) · [Source](https://junglebosstours.com/vi/kham-pha/blog-du-lich/nha-tu-phu-quoc)

#### Historic shrines & museums

- **Nguyen Trung Truc Temple (Gành Dầu)** (Đền thờ Nguyễn Trung Trực Phú Quốc) · Gành Dầu (north-west tip, near Bãi Dài) · $ · *checked 2026*
  - A shrine near Gành Dầu cape to Nguyễn Trung Trực, who sank the French warship L'Espérance in 1861 and was captured on Phú Quốc in 1868; before his execution he spoke one of the most quoted lines in Vietnamese history: 'Only when the French have pulled up every blade of grass in this land will the Vietnamese stop fighting them.' Built in 1993 and renovated in 2016, it centres on his statue, with swords, drums, gongs and the remains of a boat that carried food to his fighters.
  - Tip: The yearly festival, held here since 1996, falls on the 27th of the 8th lunar month (7 October in 2026), with incense rites and a re-enactment of the L'Espérance attack. It's on the same north-island run as VinWonders and Grand World; dress modestly, and since no visiting hours are published, go in daylight.
  - Address: Mũi Gành Dầu, Phú Quốc
  - Price: No entry fee mentioned in our sources
  - [Google Maps](https://www.google.com/maps/search/?api=1&query=%C4%90%E1%BB%81n%20th%E1%BB%9D%20Nguy%E1%BB%85n%20Trung%20Tr%E1%BB%B1c%2C%20G%C3%A0nh%20D%E1%BA%A7u%2C%20Ph%C3%BA%20Qu%E1%BB%91c) · [Source](https://vietnamnews.vn/sunday/features/942036/more-to-phu-quoc-than-beaches-and-sea.html)
- **Coi Nguon Museum** (Bảo tàng Cội Nguồn) · Dương Đông – Long Beach (Trần Hưng Đạo) · $ · *checked 2025*
  - Phú Quốc's only private museum, opened in 2009 by local collector Huỳnh Phước Huệ, crams some 3,000 objects (ancient ceramics, coral and shells, fish-sauce and pearl-farming gear, stilt-house life) into a multi-storey building, plus a library of 300-plus documents on the island in Vietnamese, English and French.
  - Tip: Treat it as an hour's primer on island history before the prison trip. Reviewers call it a fun mish-mash with patchy English labels and some neglected displays (3.8/5 on Google), but praise the cheap ticket and the views from the upper floors; ask whether the document library is open to browse.
  - Hours: Daily from 07:00; Google lists 07:00–16:00, Sun Paradise Land (Oct 2025) 07:00–11:00 and 13:00–17:00
  - Address: 149 Trần Hưng Đạo, Khu phố 7, Phú Quốc
  - Price: 50,000 VND
  - [Google Maps](https://www.google.com/maps/search/?api=1&query=B%E1%BA%A3o%20t%C3%A0ng%20C%E1%BB%99i%20Ngu%E1%BB%93n%2C%20149%20Tr%E1%BA%A7n%20H%C6%B0ng%20%C4%90%E1%BA%A1o%2C%20Ph%C3%BA%20Qu%E1%BB%91c) · [Source](https://sunparadiseland.com/SunParadiseLandPhuQuoc/tin-tuc/kinh-nghiem-kham-pha-bao-tang-coi-nguon-phu-quoc-chi-tiet-12347)

### Phú Quốc: local tips

- Sunset sets the schedule: the west coast (Ông Lang, Dương Đông, Long Beach, Sunset Town) faces it, golden hour runs roughly 4:30–6:30pm and the sun sets around 5:15–6:15pm, so arrive 30–45 minutes early for a front table. Skies are clearest November–April; in the May–October monsoon expect rain-outs and rough seas, and some places open only in the dry season (Mango Bay's Beach Bar runs November–April).
- Plan by hub. The Dương Đông–Long Beach strip on Trần Hưng Đạo (Chuồn Chuồn, Tarapaca, Origin 1982, Bittersweet, Cheeky Traveller, Blue Monkey, OCSEN) is the walkable late-night zone; the Bãi Trường resorts (INK 360, Sailing Club) are 15–20 minutes south by car; Sunset Town and Bãi Khem are 30–40 minutes from Dương Đông; and Grand World is in the far north, an hour or more from Sunset Town, so don't attempt both show hubs in one evening. Use Grab or a taxi rather than a motorbike after drinking: distances are long and the roads are dark.
- A live-music crawl on the strip: happy hour at The Rabbit Hole Irish Bar (Hẻm 118, 3–7pm, buy two get one free), Cheeky Traveller's house band from about 7:30pm in the same alley, OCSEN's beach fire show around 10pm, then Blue Monkey's Vietnamese singers until 2am. After that, a May 2026 guide names House No.1 (58 Bạch Đằng, by the night market) as the after-midnight bar. Sunset Town's Teatro Club (EDM, house and hip-hop until about 2am) is the only proper nightclub, but its reviews are polarised.
- Sunset Town fires fireworks twice a night, and you can watch them free from the waterfront, VUI-Fest Bazaar or the seafront bars; you only need a ticket for the full 9pm Kiss of the Sea (no full show on Tuesdays). Book at booking.sunworld.vn or through OTAs, re-check times on the day (Sun Group's own pages are marketing and schedules shift), and allow extra time: after 7pm private cars can't use Sunset Town's main streets and electric buggies do the last stretch.
- Budget for '++': resort and hotel menus add service charge and VAT on top, roughly 13–16% (Le Bar adds 5% service plus VAT, OCSEN 8% service), while Chuồn Chuồn's prices include both. Benchmarks: local cafés 20–85k VND a drink, craft beer 80–120k, beach-bar cocktails 200–270k, rooftop cocktails from about US$15 at INK 360.
- Rượu sim, made from the rose-myrtle (sim) berry, is the island's sweet-tart wine. Taste it at a producer such as Bảy Gáo in Suối Mây (free or about 30–50k VND entry with a tasting; bottles roughly 150–500k) rather than at the night market, or try it in Le Bar's Sim Berry Martini. Sim nectar and syrup are non-alcoholic, so check the label.
- Specialty coffee is thin on the island, and researchers found no well-known local roaster. For espresso go to Chuồn Chuồn (Arabica blend) or Eric Kayser; otherwise order Vietnamese styles (cà phê muối or salt coffee, coconut coffee, bạc xỉu, egg coffee), best at Tarapaca, which also has power outlets for laptop days.
- Since July 2025 Phú Quốc has been a special zone (đặc khu) of An Giang province, so listings mix 'Kiên Giang', 'Dương Đông' and new ward names, and many 2026 SEO and resort-blog listicles still send readers to closed or moved venues (Rory's Beach Bar is now on the east coast; Aroi Dessert Cafe, WoodHouse Coffee and Lá CafeDeli are marked permanently closed). Navigate by venue name plus the Trần Hưng Đạo number or alley (Hẻm 118, Hẻm 124), and check recent Google reviews before a long ride.
- Gamers' reality check: we found no esports or PC-gaming lounge, VR arcade, laser tag, paintball, escape room or bowling alley on Phú Quốc with any 2025–26 presence, and Timezone, Vietnam's main arcade chain, has no branch here. Dương Đông's neighbourhood net cafés (quán net) publish no specs, so save CS2 and Valorant for Saigon.
- Treat any 'airsoft' or real-gun shooting offered by touts or tour desks on the island as illegal: Vietnam restricts guns tightly, and we found no licensed range on Phú Quốc.
- More pool: the Cheeky Traveller, in the same alley as The Rabbit Hole (Hẻm 118 Trần Hưng Đạo; see Music), has a free pool table between live sets, so you can play at one and catch the band at the other.
- Plan by geography: VinWonders, Corona Casino, Grand World and the Nguyễn Trung Trực temple all sit in the north around Bãi Dài and Gành Dầu, while the prison is in An Thới, 25–29 km (35–45 minutes) south of Dương Đông, so pair it with Sunset Town or Bãi Sao.
- The Nguyễn Trung Trực festival at Gành Dầu falls on the 27th day of the 8th lunar month: 7 October in 2026, and late September or October in other years.
- Bring your own reading: we found no independent, second-hand or English-language bookshop, book café or visitor reading room on Phú Quốc, and no book fair or poetry night announced through March 2027, so load your e-reader or buy books in Saigon before you fly.
- Prison reading: Chu Lai's ký sự 'Nhà lao Cây Dừa' (NXB Văn học, 2004; first printed in Kiên Giang in 1992) is the classic account. Former prisoners have also published memoir collections, all in Vietnamese; we found no English translations.
- Literary day trip: Hà Tiên, about 1 hr 20 min by fast boat from Bãi Vòng port, was home to Mạc Thiên Tích's 18th-century Chiêu Anh Các poetry society and its 'Hà Tiên thập cảnh' (Ten Views of Hà Tiên) poems.
- Out of town, the sunset spots are still on the west coast: Gành Dầu in the far north (40six Beach Haus) and An Thới in the south (PH Rooftop). The east and south-east coast (Hàm Ninh, Cây Sao, Bãi Sao, Bãi Khem) faces sunrise, so make those daytime beach trips and be back on the west side for golden hour.
- Hàm Ninh fishing village has no real café scene beyond a few morning cà phê sữa đá stalls: go early for sunrise on the pier, then eat Hàm Ninh crab at the floating restaurants there.
- Rạch Vẹm, the starfish beach in the north (20–25 km from Dương Đông), has floating raft restaurants rather than cafés; some, such as Nhà bè Sóng Xanh, throw in free karaoke and fishing if you eat there. Starfish season is October–March.
- Several out-of-town bars that 2026 listicles still recommend are shut or doubtful: Rory's Beach Bar at Cây Sao showed as temporarily closed on Google in October 2026, and Vietnam Coracle marks Rock Island Club (Nam Nghi, Cửa Cạn), Paradiso Beach Club (Bãi Sao) and The Pier (Cây Sao) as temporarily closed; researchers found no 2025–26 evidence that any has reopened. Check recent reviews or Facebook before crossing the island.

### Phú Quốc: closed, moved or doubtful

- **Rory's Beach Bar**: Dropped: it has moved from Long Beach to Cây Sao beach, Hàm Ninh, on the east coast (VinWonders Dec 2025; localvietnam May 2026), Google showed it as temporarily closed in July 2026, and many listicles still give the old 118/10 Trần Hưng Đạo address.
- **Rory's Beach Bar mini-golf & pétanque (Cây Sao, Hàm Ninh)**: Researchers found its Google listing marked 'temporarily closed' (via Wanderlog, Oct 2026) and its website last updated in 2024; too doubtful to send you across the island to play.
- **The Dog Bar (88 Trần Hưng Đạo)**: Wikivoyage still sends readers here for pool and darts, but its only link is an old MySpace page and researchers found no 2025–26 evidence it still exists; The Rabbit Hole covers pool and darts.
- **Rory's Beach Bar (Cây Sao)**: Doubtful: its Google listing at Group 3, Cây Sao shows 'Temporarily Closed' (Wanderlog, checked Oct 2026), matching round 1's July 2026 finding, although May–June 2026 listicles (localvietnam, Sun Group) still recommend it. Re-check its Facebook page before adding.

### Phú Quốc: considered but not picked

- **Aloha Beach Bar (Aloha Seafood & Bar)**: Not selected: a casual beanbag beach bar with live music and fire shows on many evenings (localvietnam, May 2026), but OCSEN and Sailing Club cover the Long Beach sunset slot and its status is only 'likely open'.
- **Ann Cafe & Brunch Phu Quoc**: Not selected: Google still shows it as operational (4.9★), but Sun Group's June 2026 café guide lists the café at the same address, 7 Lê Thị Hồng Gấm, as 'Godfather Garden Cafe', so it may have been rebranded; it also closes at 5pm.
- **Bay Gao Sim Garden (Vườn sim Bảy Gáo)**: Not selected as a pick: a daytime rượu sim tasting garden rather than a bar (newest evidence Dec 2025); covered in tips.
- **Bar Jade (Regent Phu Quoc)**: Not selected: no dated evidence, and non-guest access isn't stated; Regent's rooftop Fu Bar may be for hotel guests only.
- **Coffee Pho Bien (Coffee Phố Biển)**: Not selected: a simple local café opposite Dinh Cậu (Sun Group guide, Aug 2026), but it has only ~40 Google reviews and no published hours, some guides name a different café at the address, and Buddy a few doors along covers the waterfront; mentioned in Buddy's tip.
- **The Garden Coffee, Food & Spa**: Not selected: well rated (4.8★ from ~760 Google reviews) but out on the ĐT45 road towards Ông Lang, so useful only if you're staying there; reviewers complain about hygiene in the spa rooms.
- **Kiss Bridge (Sunset Town)**: Merged into Kiss of the Sea and Symphony of the Sea: the bridge is the sunset walk and viewing point for both shows.
- **Once show at VinWonders Phu Quoc**: Not selected: only worth seeing as part of a full-day VinWonders ticket (950,000 VND); the Grand World pick covers the north-island evening.
- **The Rabbit Hole Irish Bar**: Not selected: a sports pub with live bands only on some nights and newest dated evidence from October 2025; Cheeky Traveller and Blue Monkey cover live music on the same strip. Mentioned in tips for its happy hour.
- **RuNam Phu Quoc**: Not selected: a polished chain café with a Kiss Bridge view (Sun Group guide, June 2026), but Eric Kayser covers coffee in Sunset Town; mentioned in that tip.
- **Santo Beach Club (Hòn Thơm)**: Not selected: described only on Sun Group's own promotional pages, reachable only with a cable-car ticket, and no hours or drink prices are published.
- **Sunset Sanato Beach Club**: Not selected: an art-installation beach with an entry fee (70–100k VND) and overpriced food, more a photo stop than a bar, though handy near the airport (Google 4.0★ from ~2,700 reviews).
- **Teatro Club**: Not selected: Sunset Town's only real nightclub, but reviews are polarised (3.8★ from ~144 Google ratings, a quarter of them 1-star) and menu prices exclude VAT and service; mentioned in tips.
- **Zyzu Kafé**: Not selected as a separate pick: Chuồn Chuồn's cheaper neighbour on Sao Mai Hill (Google reviews May 2026), mentioned in its tip.
- **Apollo Cafe (Sunset Town)**: Not selected: a Bill Bensley design café with an 'upside-down town' ceiling and a balcony over the Kiss Bridge, but it is described only on Sun Group's own promotional pages, with no hours or prices published. Eric Kayser already covers coffee in Sunset Town.
- **Cafe Cao (Cafe Cào)**: Not selected: a cheap local café (10–30k VND) at 54 Đường 30/4 serving Phú Quốc's bún kèn and bún quậy breakfasts, but its only source is Sun Group's Dương Đông café list (15 Jun 2026), with no hours or independent reviews found. Mix Bakery covers the same street.
- **Goc Ca Phe – Phu Quoc Riverside Coffee, Tea & Books (Góc Cà Phê)**: Not selected: a riverside café with books and cơm gà (30–50k VND) at 267 TL47, listed in Sun Group's café list (15 Jun 2026), but listings disagree on where it is and no hours are published.
- **Zyzu Kafe (Zyzu Kafé)**: Not selected: already mentioned in Chuồn Chuồn's tip (same 69 Trần Hưng Đạo hill address, drinks 40–80k per Sun Group, Jun 2026), and it is on the Long Beach strip, not beyond it.

## Saigon

*Hồ Chí Minh City (Sài Gòn) · the south*

Saigon is bigger, louder and later than Hanoi. Sock-filter coffee stalls that date back to 1938 sit a few streets from fine-robusta roasters, and cocktail bars hide up the stairwells of 1960s apartment blocks. Nights run from plastic-stool nhậu and rooftop bands to phòng trà ballads, jazz and techno. Gamers get high-refresh PC arenas for well under US$1 an hour and a real-gun range at the Củ Chi Tunnels; readers get the Book Street beside the post office and Graham Greene's Hotel Continental.

### Saigon: Cafés (Cà phê)

Saigon runs on robusta: brewed slowly through a phin or strained through cloth socks, poured over ice with condensed milk and drunk on plastic stools, park lawns and the balconies of 1960s apartment blocks. Sock-coffee stalls dating back to 1938 still work alongside a serious specialty scene that champions Vietnamese fine robusta and Đà Lạt arabica, while hidden cafés fill old apartment blocks, alleys and riverside villas.

#### Sock coffee & heritage cafés

- **★ Cheo Leo Café** (Cà phê vợt Cheo Leo) · District 3 (Bàn Cờ ward) · $ · *checked 2025*
  - Saigon's oldest café has brewed cà phê vợt since 1938 and is still run by the founder's family down a District 3 alley. The coffee is strained through cloth socks and kept hot in clay pots over charcoal, much as it was when students and intellectuals made it their hangout before 1975.
  - Tip: Come in the morning, when neighbourhood regulars fill the low stools, and order a hot cà phê sữa; the house version is finished with condensed milk and a little butter. Enter via alley 109, because some sites print just '36 Nguyễn Thiện Thuật'.
  - Hours: Daily about 6am–6pm
  - Address: 109/36 Nguyễn Thiện Thuật (inside alley 109), District 3
  - Price: About 25k VND a coffee (2026 guide)
  - [Google Maps](https://www.google.com/maps/search/?api=1&query=Cheo%20Leo%20Cafe%20109/36%20Nguy%E1%BB%85n%20Thi%E1%BB%87n%20Thu%E1%BA%ADt%20District%203%20Ho%20Chi%20Minh%20City) · [Source](https://thanhnien.vn/nhung-quan-ca-phe-vot-lung-danh-lau-doi-nhat-tphcm-ban-da-ghe-chua-185250616162555189.htm)
- **★ Ba Lù Café** (Cà phê vợt Ba Lù) · Chợ Lớn (District 5) · $ · *checked 2026*
  - Chợ Lớn's sock-coffee stall has worked inside Phùng Hưng market since 1953 and is now run by the founder's children. They still roast the beans by hand over a wood fire with butter and salt, the Chinese-Vietnamese way, and brew them in cloth socks and clay pots.
  - Tip: Go in the morning, try it black first to taste the roast, and buy a bag of the house-roasted beans to take home. In 2026 the founder's daughter opened a second shop, Cà phê Ba Lù 1953, at Nguyễn Trãi and Châu Văn Liêm with the same prices, so you can compare the two.
  - Hours: Early morning to late afternoon (listings vary)
  - Address: Inside Phùng Hưng market (listed as 199B Phùng Hưng), Chợ Lớn; second shop 'Cà phê Ba Lù 1953' at the corner of Nguyễn Trãi and Châu Văn Liêm
  - Price: Black 25k, milk coffee 30k, egg coffee 35k VND (Thanh Niên, Mar 2026)
  - [Google Maps](https://www.google.com/maps/search/?api=1&query=C%C3%A0%20ph%C3%AA%20Ba%20L%C3%B9%20ch%E1%BB%A3%20Ph%C3%B9ng%20H%C6%B0ng%20Ch%E1%BB%A3%20L%E1%BB%9Bn%20Ho%20Chi%20Minh%20City) · [Source](https://thanhnien.vn/ca-phe-vot-ba-lu-hon-70-nam-o-cho-lon-vi-sao-bong-co-them-tiem-moi-185260314134241594.htm)
- **Phan Đình Phùng Sock Coffee** (Cà phê vợt Phan Đình Phùng) · Phú Nhuận · $ · *checked 2026*
  - A third-generation family stall that has brewed sock coffee in a Phú Nhuận alley since 1954, over a fire that reportedly hasn't gone out in decades. It now runs around the clock: retirees at dawn, office workers by day and crowds of young Saigonese after 9pm.
  - Tip: This is the sock coffee to have after dark. Expect plastic stools, no Wi-Fi or air-con and no card or QR payment, so bring small notes.
  - Hours: 24/7
  - Address: 330/2 Phan Đình Phùng (in the alley), Phú Nhuận
  - Price: 10–15k VND a cup (Tuổi Trẻ, Jun 2026); cash only
  - [Google Maps](https://www.google.com/maps/search/?api=1&query=C%C3%A0%20ph%C3%AA%20v%E1%BB%A3t%20330/2%20Phan%20%C4%90%C3%ACnh%20Ph%C3%B9ng%20Ph%C3%BA%20Nhu%E1%BA%ADn%20Ho%20Chi%20Minh%20City) · [Source](https://tuoitre.vn/thuong-hieu-toi-yeu-sai-gon-tho-khe-cung-ngon-lua-liu-riu-cua-ca-phe-vot-100260914123105665.htm)
- **Cà Phê Đỗ Phủ** (Cà phê Đỗ Phủ – Cơm tấm Đại Hàn) · Tân Định (District 1) · $ · *checked 2025*
  - Before 1975 this two-storey café and broken-rice shop was cover for the Biệt động Sài Gòn commandos: Trần Văn Lai hid weapons and messages here, including in a dresser with a false bottom. The house keeps its original mid-century furniture and artefacts and still serves coffee and cơm tấm.
  - Tip: Come at lunch for cơm tấm with the house kimchi, a wartime holdover, then take a French butter coffee up to the second-floor balcony. There's no air-con.
  - Hours: 7am–10pm
  - Address: 113A Đặng Dung, Tân Định ward (District 1)
  - Price: Cơm tấm 35–55k VND, special 70k (mia.vn, 2025)
  - [Google Maps](https://www.google.com/maps/search/?api=1&query=C%C3%A0%20ph%C3%AA%20%C4%90%E1%BB%97%20Ph%E1%BB%A7%20C%C6%A1m%20t%E1%BA%A5m%20%C4%90%E1%BA%A1i%20H%C3%A0n%20113A%20%C4%90%E1%BA%B7ng%20Dung%20Ho%20Chi%20Minh%20City) · [Source](https://saigoneer.com/saigon-street-food-restaurants/26146-hẻm-gems-cà-phê-đỗ-phủ-cơm-tấm-đại-hàn)

#### Café apartments

- **★ The Café Apartment (42 Nguyễn Huệ)** (Chung cư 42 Nguyễn Huệ) · District 1 (Nguyễn Huệ walking street) · $$ · *checked 2026*
  - A peeling nine-storey 1960 apartment block facing Nguyễn Huệ walking street, where dozens of flats have become cafés, tea rooms and shops, each balcony hung with its own sign. Long-time residents still live among them, so it feels like old Saigon stacked vertically rather than a mall.
  - Tip: Take the lift and pick a balcony: Sài Gòn Ơi (5th floor) for cà phê sữa đá, The Letter (6th) or Buihaus (7th); % Arabica is on the 4th. Come mid-afternoon or for sunset, because lift queues build towards the 8pm peak.
  - Hours: Most cafés about 8am–10pm (Sài Gòn Ơi 8:30am–9pm; Buihaus to 10:30pm; % Arabica 7am–11pm)
  - Address: 42 Nguyễn Huệ, Sài Gòn ward (District 1)
  - Price: Drinks mostly 30–100k VND (% Arabica 100k+); lift 5k VND per person, refunded when you order
  - [Google Maps](https://www.google.com/maps/search/?api=1&query=Chung%20c%C6%B0%2042%20Nguy%E1%BB%85n%20Hu%E1%BB%87%20cafe%20apartment%20Ho%20Chi%20Minh%20City) · [Source](https://mia.vn/cam-nang-du-lich/chung-cu-42-nguyen-hue-12503)
- **Mơ Đi Hội** · District 1 (Phạm Ngũ Lão / Bến Thành) · $$ · *checked 2025*
  - Florists run this plant- and flower-filled hideaway on the 4th floor of a maze-like old apartment block, reached through a plain door behind the bánh mì stalls. Window sofas and a ladder-up loft with floor seating look out on Huyện Sỹ Church.
  - Tip: Follow the colourful signs up the stairs and order the signature trà hoa nở (blooming tea). The same building holds Cà Zone (2nd floor) and Em có café (6th floor) if you want to café-hop.
  - Hours: 8am–10pm
  - Address: 4th floor, 145 Nguyễn Trãi, District 1
  - Price: Coffee 55–60k VND; signature teas and mocktails 75–85k (Saigoneer, Aug 2024)
  - [Google Maps](https://www.google.com/maps/search/?api=1&query=M%C6%A1%20%C4%90i%20H%E1%BB%99i%20cafe%20145%20Nguy%E1%BB%85n%20Tr%C3%A3i%20District%201%20Ho%20Chi%20Minh%20City) · [Source](https://www.saigoneer.com/saigon-street-food-restaurants/27214-hẻm-gems-mơ-đi-hội,-when-florists-dream-of-opening-a-cafe)

#### Specialty & third-wave

- **★ Every Half Coffee Roasters** · District 1, District 3 and Thảo Điền · $$ · *checked 2026*
  - The roaster leading Vietnam's fine-robusta push: its Cư M'gar (Đắk Lắk) fine robusta was named Gold in Filter Coffee (Robusta) at the Global Coffee Awards 2025, and it also roasts liberica and highland arabica. Its Thảo Điền roastery runs partly on solar power.
  - Tip: Order the fine robusta black as a pour-over, ideally next to a Vietnamese arabica, and take home a 200g bag of Fine Robusta Đắk Lắk Honey (150k VND). There are 35 branches, so for the brew-bar experience go to the roastery or the District 3 cafés; the Ngô Đức Kế store is on the same street as The Workshop.
  - Hours: Daily 7am–9:30pm (official store list)
  - Address: Đồng Khởi store: 8–24 Ngô Đức Kế, District 1; pour-over cafés at 232/23 Võ Thị Sáu and 6E Tú Xương, District 3; Roastery Thảo Điền: 01 Đường Số 10, An Khánh ward
  - Price: Fine Robusta Đắk Lắk Honey beans 150k VND per 200g
  - [Google Maps](https://www.google.com/maps/search/?api=1&query=Every%20Half%20Coffee%20Roasters%208-24%20Ng%C3%B4%20%C4%90%E1%BB%A9c%20K%E1%BA%BF%20District%201%20Ho%20Chi%20Minh%20City) · [Source](https://everyhalf.vn/location)
- **★ The Workshop Coffee** · District 1 (off Nguyễn Huệ) · $$ · *checked 2026*
  - Saigon's original specialty roaster-café sits up the stairs of a century-old colonial building: a loft with cathedral ceilings and exposed brick around a central island where siphons, pour-overs and a La Marzocco run in full view. The Dot (Mar 2026) calls it the downtown original still pouring.
  - Tip: Sit at the brew island and order a V60 of Đà Lạt arabica, then buy beans. There's no background music, just grinders and conversation. A few locals call it overrated, so go for the room and the brewing ritual; Every Half's store at 8–24 Ngô Đức Kế is on the same street for a fine-robusta comparison.
  - Hours: About 8am–8:30pm (2025–26 listings)
  - Address: 2nd floor, 27 Ngô Đức Kế, Sài Gòn ward (District 1)
  - Price: Drinks about 70–120k VND (Vietnam Airlines guide)
  - [Google Maps](https://www.google.com/maps/search/?api=1&query=The%20Workshop%20Coffee%2027%20Ng%C3%B4%20%C4%90%E1%BB%A9c%20K%E1%BA%BF%20District%201%20Ho%20Chi%20Minh%20City) · [Source](https://thedotmagazine.com/the-best-coffeeshops-in-ho-chi-minh-city/)
- **GRANDMUM Cafe** · Tân Định (District 1), near the Pink Church · *checked 2026*
  - A home-like alley house with a piano, a turntable and a small pond, run by Lê Nhã Thi, Vietnam's first AeroPress champion. She brews delicate filter coffees that set East African arabicas beside Vietnamese robusta, and hosts a five-drink Vietnam Coffee Omakase.
  - Tip: Book the omakase (five Vietnamese coffee drinks, up to four guests, 18+) through Airbnb Experiences or by messaging @grandmumcafe well ahead, because sessions sell out. Ignore old listings at 46 Ter Võ Văn Tần.
  - Hours: Mon–Fri 10am–5pm; Sat–Sun 8:30am–5pm (Google listing via Wanderlog)
  - Address: 86B1 alley 82 Võ Thị Sáu, District 1
  - [Google Maps](https://www.google.com/maps/search/?api=1&query=GRANDMUM%20Cafe%20h%E1%BA%BBm%2082%20V%C3%B5%20Th%E1%BB%8B%20S%C3%A1u%20T%C3%A2n%20%C4%90%E1%BB%8Bnh%20Ho%20Chi%20Minh%20City) · [Source](https://www.airbnb.com/experiences/732332)
- **MANKI Specialty Coffee Bar** · District 1 (Bến Thành ward, off Bùi Thị Xuân) · *checked 2026*
  - One counter, seven seats and no tables: you face the barista while rotating lots from its own roaster, Pinkbeans, are brewed in front of you, lately a washed La Esperanza from Colombia and Gedeb Chelchele from Ethiopia. It's Saigon's purist coffee bar.
  - Tip: Book a slot at mankicoffee.com or on +84 898 68 28 17 (also WhatsApp), especially at weekends, and note the 12:00–13:30 break. Beans come in 200g bags; the Pinkbeans roastery now fills MANKI's original space at 42 Lý Tự Trọng.
  - Hours: Daily 8:30am–12pm and 1:30–5pm
  - Address: 134/12/3 Bùi Thị Xuân, Bến Thành ward (District 1)
  - Price: Drink prices not published
  - [Google Maps](https://www.google.com/maps/search/?api=1&query=MANKI%20Specialty%20Coffee%20Bar%20134/12/3%20B%C3%B9i%20Th%E1%BB%8B%20Xu%C3%A2n%20Ho%20Chi%20Minh%20City) · [Source](https://mankicoffee.com/)
- **Lacàph Coffee Experiences Space** (Lacàph) · District 1 (Nguyễn Thái Bình, near Bến Thành) · *checked 2026*
  - A roaster devoted entirely to Vietnamese coffee (its Phin Blend won two Great Taste stars), whose Coffee Guides run tastings and hands-on workshops. It's the easiest place for a visitor to learn robusta versus arabica and phin versus filter, and to try experimental lots such as a beer-fermented coffee.
  - Tip: Book a workshop: Fun & Easy Vietnamese Coffee (1.5 hours, 714k VND, three brews plus bánh mì), egg coffee (630k), or the 2.5-hour hands-on discovery or coffee-cocktail class (860k each). It also arranges farm trips to the highlands.
  - Hours: Daily 7:30am–7:30pm (Đồng Khởi bar 9am–6:30pm)
  - Address: 220A Nguyễn Công Trứ, District 1; espresso bar upstairs at 151 Đồng Khởi
  - Price: Workshops 630k–860k VND per person; drink prices not published
  - [Google Maps](https://www.google.com/maps/search/?api=1&query=Lac%C3%A0ph%20Coffee%20Experiences%20Space%20220A%20Nguy%E1%BB%85n%20C%C3%B4ng%20Tr%E1%BB%A9%20Ho%20Chi%20Minh%20City) · [Source](https://lacaph.com/vietnamese-coffee-experiences)
- **Okkio Caffe** · District 1 (Lê Lợi) and Thảo Điền · $$ · *checked 2026*
  - A polished local mini-chain that takes Vietnamese robusta seriously: Barista Magazine singled out its 100% Đắk Lắk robusta for notes of cocoa, orange and dark chocolate. Its Thảo Điền branch, designed by Red5studio as a homage to Wong Kar-wai's 2046, is one of the city's most striking café interiors.
  - Tip: Order the Đắk Lắk robusta as a phin or pour-over, or an espresso tonic. The Lê Lợi branch has a narrow staircase and fills fast, so go early; make the trip to Thảo Điền for the architecture.
  - Hours: Lê Lợi 7:30am–8pm (Feb 2026 listing)
  - Address: 120–122 Lê Lợi, District 1; 110 Xuân Thủy, Thảo Điền; also 151 Đồng Khởi (Opera House) and 97A Phó Đức Chính (Fine Arts Museum)
  - Price: Drinks 60–100k VND at Lê Lợi (Feb 2026)
  - [Google Maps](https://www.google.com/maps/search/?api=1&query=Okkio%20Caffe%20120%20L%C3%AA%20L%E1%BB%A3i%20District%201%20Ho%20Chi%20Minh%20City) · [Source](https://www.saigoneer.com/vietnam-architecture/20614-photos-d2-s-okkio-cafe-is-an-architectural-homage-to-wong-kar-wai)

#### Street classics: bệt, coconut & salt

- **Cà phê bệt at 30/4 Park** (Cà phê bệt Công viên 30/4) · District 1 (Independence Palace / Notre-Dame) · $ · *checked 2026*
  - Saigon's sit-on-the-ground coffee culture: young locals spread mats on the shady lawns between the Independence Palace and Notre-Dame and talk for hours over iced coffee. Local papers found the park packed on cool mornings in January 2026 and over the 30 April holiday.
  - Tip: Bring a mat plus your own coffee and breakfast, as many locals do, or buy from a vendor and agree the price first. Skip the tarp-covered benches by Hồ Con Rùa and the Central Post Office, where vendors expect you to buy before you sit.
  - Hours: Best early morning and after dusk
  - Address: Lawns of Công viên 30/4, between the Independence Palace and Notre-Dame Cathedral, District 1
  - Price: Free if you bring your own; vendor coffee is cheap (agree the price first)
  - [Google Maps](https://www.google.com/maps/search/?api=1&query=C%C3%B4ng%20vi%C3%AAn%2030/4%20Ho%20Chi%20Minh%20City) · [Source](https://thanhnien.vn/nghi-le-304-nhieu-nguoi-chon-ca-phe-bet-o-cong-vien-30-thang-4-de-chill-185260430105750493.htm)
- **Cộng Cà Phê** · District 1 (Lý Tự Trọng) and Hồ Con Rùa (District 3) · $$ · *checked 2026*
