  - The Hanoi-born chain that made coconut coffee (cà phê cốt dừa) famous, blending strong coffee with icy coconut cream, served in retro subsidy-era rooms of wooden furniture, cassette players and folk-art paintings. It's the most reliable place in Saigon to try the drink.
  - Tip: Order the cốt dừa cà phê and ask for 'ít ngọt' (less sweet) if you prefer. The Hồ Con Rùa branch sits upstairs on the Turtle Lake roundabout, so walk round the lake afterwards; 26 Lý Tự Trọng is the central District 1 option.
  - Hours: 26 Lý Tự Trọng about 7:30am–10:30pm (Dec 2025 listing)
  - Address: Ground floor, 26 Lý Tự Trọng, District 1; Hồ Con Rùa branch upstairs at 8 Bis Công Trường Quốc Tế, District 3; more branches citywide
  - Price: Drinks about 45–75k VND (2026 guide)
  - [Google Maps](https://www.google.com/maps/search/?api=1&query=C%E1%BB%99ng%20C%C3%A0%20Ph%C3%AA%2026%20L%C3%BD%20T%E1%BB%B1%20Tr%E1%BB%8Dng%20District%201%20Ho%20Chi%20Minh%20City) · [Source](https://congcaphe.com/stores)
- **Chú Long Salt Coffee** (Cà phê muối Chú Long) · Citywide (District 1, District 3, Phú Nhuận and more) · $ · *checked 2026*
  - The cheap street chain behind Saigon's salt-coffee (cà phê muối) craze: strong coffee under a salty, creamy topping. It opened more than a dozen branches in two months in 2024 and was still adding new ones in 2026.
  - Tip: Order it iced and stir the salty cream through. After a 2026 rebrand the signs show the brand name and a cup of salt coffee instead of Uncle Long's face, so look for the name. Coconut coffee is on the menu too.
  - Hours: Varies by branch
  - Address: Many branches; find the nearest on Maps (a 2024 list included 68 Nguyễn Huệ, District 1, and 301 Nguyễn Đình Chiểu, District 3)
  - Price: About 15–20k VND a cup (2024); prices unchanged after the 2026 rebrand (Tuổi Trẻ)
  - [Google Maps](https://www.google.com/maps/search/?api=1&query=C%C3%A0%20ph%C3%AA%20mu%E1%BB%91i%20Ch%C3%BA%20Long%20Ho%20Chi%20Minh%20City) · [Source](https://tuoitre.vn/ca-phe-muoi-chu-long-am-tham-thay-doi-dien-mao-20260616162622292.htm)

#### Local favourites

- **Railway-alley cafés (Hẻm 115 Lê Văn Sỹ)** (Nhỏ Café & Phường Cà Phê – hẻm 115 Lê Văn Sỹ) · Phú Nhuận (on the District 3 border) · $ · *checked 2025*
  - Hẻm 115 runs beside the North–South railway through Phú Nhuận, where the track is raised and fenced off and small local cafés have opened along the lanes. Nhỏ Café doubles as its owner's antique museum, crammed with colourful old TVs, radios, cameras, rotary phones, old coins and banknotes, with a menu designed like an old envelope; further in, Phường Cà Phê has resident cats and the blare of passing locomotives.
  - Tip: Start with a cà phê sữa đá among Nhỏ's collection, then walk on to Phường Cà Phê for a second cup with the cats and wait for a train. You hear the horns from your seat rather than standing by the rails as on Hanoi's train street, so it's calm enough for a long afternoon.
  - Hours: Nhỏ Café 9am–10:30pm (mia.vn, Sep 2025); Phường Cà Phê hours not published
  - Address: Nhỏ Café at 115/30 Lê Văn Sỹ; Phường Cà Phê at 115/102 Lê Văn Sỹ, Phú Nhuận
  - Price: Nhỏ Café drinks 38–55k VND (mia.vn, Sep 2025)
  - [Google Maps](https://www.google.com/maps/search/?api=1&query=Nh%E1%BB%8F%20Cafe%20115/30%20L%C3%AA%20V%C4%83n%20S%E1%BB%B9%20Ph%C3%BA%20Nhu%E1%BA%ADn%20Ho%20Chi%20Minh%20City) · [Source](https://mia.vn/cam-nang-du-lich/tiem-cafe-sai-gon-xua-19371)

#### River & sunset cafés

- **Satsuma House** · Thảo Điền (former District 2) · $$ · *unverified*
  - A renovated 1970s villa with a leafy garden on the Saigon River, looking across to the skyline and Landmark 81, with a chic Saint-Tropez-meets-Saigon feel. Weekend mornings bring coffee in the garden, and sunset brings cocktails such as a lychee gin or a citrusy spritz.
  - Tip: Time it for sunset (around 5:40pm in October). It opens at 3pm on weekdays and 8am at weekends, and is closed on Mondays. Expect above-average prices.
  - Hours: Tue–Fri 3pm–11:30pm; Sat–Sun 8am–11:30pm; closed Monday
  - Address: 28b Nguyễn Văn Hưởng, Thảo Điền
  - Price: Coffee above the city average; cocktails cost more
  - [Google Maps](https://www.google.com/maps/search/?api=1&query=Satsuma%20House%2028b%20Nguy%E1%BB%85n%20V%C4%83n%20H%C6%B0%E1%BB%9Fng%20Th%E1%BA%A3o%20%C4%90i%E1%BB%81n%20Ho%20Chi%20Minh%20City) · [Source](https://vietcetera.com/en/a-day-in-thao-dien-saigons-cool-neighborhood)
- **Tiệm Cà Phê Dưới Tán Cây** · Bình Quới peninsula (Bình Thạnh) · $ · *checked 2025*
  - A simple local café on the Saigon River bank in leafy Bình Quới, where you sit under the trees in the river breeze and watch the sky and water turn gold at sunset for very little money.
  - Tip: Arrive before sunset, and earlier still at weekends when it fills up. Order a Vietnamese coffee, or stay on for the weekend acoustic sets, when guests sing along with the band.
  - Music: Acoustic sets Saturday and Sunday, 7:30–9:30pm
  - Hours: Daily 8am–10:30pm; acoustic sets 7:30–9:30pm on Saturdays, Sundays and holidays (café's TikTok bio)
  - Address: 353/7A Bình Quới, Bình Thạnh
  - Price: Drinks from about 33–35k VND by day; acoustic nights a flat 70k per person (reviewsaigon.vn, May 2026); motorbike parking 3k (Dec 2025)
  - [Google Maps](https://www.google.com/maps/search/?api=1&query=Ti%E1%BB%87m%20c%C3%A0%20ph%C3%AA%20d%C6%B0%E1%BB%9Bi%20t%C3%A1n%20c%C3%A2y%20353/7A%20B%C3%ACnh%20Qu%E1%BB%9Bi%20B%C3%ACnh%20Th%E1%BA%A1nh%20Ho%20Chi%20Minh%20City) · [Source](https://mia.vn/cam-nang-du-lich/tiem-ca-phe-duoi-tan-cay-17121)

#### After midnight

- **3 Hào Cà Phê** · District 7 (Tân Quy Đông residential area, Tân Hưng ward) · $ · *checked 2025*
  - A retro-industrial café that looks more like a 1990s Vietnamese factory than a coffee shop: grey walls, old steel tables and chairs, vintage desk lamps, typewriters and Vietnamese books from the 1980s and 90s. It stays open until 2:30am for night owls with a deadline or a book.
  - Tip: Order the best-selling salted-cream matcha latte or a salt coffee. It's a long Grab ride from District 1, so it suits anyone staying in District 7; payment is cash or bank transfer.
  - Hours: 7:30am–2:30am
  - Address: 46 Đường số 53, KDC Tân Quy Đông, Tân Hưng ward (District 7)
  - Price: Under 100k VND per person (Saigoneer, Aug 2025)
  - [Google Maps](https://www.google.com/maps/search/?api=1&query=3%20H%C3%A0o%20C%C3%A0%20Ph%C3%AA%2046%20%C4%90%C6%B0%E1%BB%9Dng%20s%E1%BB%91%2053%20T%C3%A2n%20Quy%20%C4%90%C3%B4ng%20District%207%20Ho%20Chi%20Minh%20City) · [Source](https://saigoneer.com/saigon-street-food-restaurants/28336-hẻm-gems-3-hào,-a-retro-industrial-cafe-in-d7-for-night-owls-on-deadlines)

### Saigon: Bars (Quán bar)

Saigon drinks at every altitude and price: plastic-stool nhậu strips toasting 'một, hai, ba, dzô!' over iced beer and grilled snails, brewers who put jasmine and passion fruit into Vietnamese craft beer, and a cocktail circuit hidden up the stairwells of old apartment blocks and colonial mansions, with two bars on the extended Asia's 50 Best Bars 2026 list. Add the hotel rooftops where war correspondents once drank above the Opera House, and one night can run from 15,000-VND draught to a 350,000-VND rotavap cocktail.

#### Cocktail bars & speakeasies

- **★ The Enigma Mansion** · District 1 (Bến Nghé, near the river) · $$$$ · *checked 2026*
  - Saigon's highest-ranked bar (No.55 on Asia's 50 Best Bars 2026, 51–100) fills a century-old colonial mansion of exposed brick and mirrors, with a phoenix hanging from the ceiling. Co-founder Vu Ngoc's team of World Class competition bartenders distils and ferments Vietnamese ingredients into precise, technical cocktails.
  - Tip: Order the Culantro (bourbon, culantro distillate, coffee, amaro, Bến Tre chocolate) or the Cluster of Citrus (gin, Vietnamese basil–kumquat oleo, orange tonic). Book Friday and Saturday tables, ask for an upstairs booth, and check its Instagram for guest-bartender nights.
  - Hours: Daily 4pm–2am
  - Address: 5H Tôn Đức Thắng, Bến Nghé, District 1
  - Price: Cocktails from about 350k VND (Vietcetera); 50 Best Discovery puts the average at ~US$12.
  - [Google Maps](https://www.google.com/maps/search/?api=1&query=The%20Enigma%20Mansion%2C%205H%20T%C3%B4n%20%C4%90%E1%BB%A9c%20Th%E1%BA%AFng%2C%20District%201%2C%20Ho%20Chi%20Minh%20City) · [Source](https://www.the50.com/discovery/Establishments/Vietnam/Ho-Chi-Minh-City/The-Enigma-Mansion.html)
- **★ Stir – Modern Classic Cocktail** · District 1 (Bến Thành, by Bến Thành Market) · $$$$ · *checked 2026*
  - Tatler's Vietnam Bar of the Year 2026 and No.84 on Asia's 50 Best Bars 2026 (51–100), a list it has made every year since 2024. Hidden up a stairwell beside Bến Thành Market, it turns regional ingredients and the street food outside into cocktails on a Vietnamese-language menu.
  - Tip: Order the Xôi Mít (jackfruit, jasmine, sticky rice, peated Scotch) or the Nước Mía Highball (fresh-pressed sugarcane, rum, calamansi, pandan); the Truffle Martini is the cult drink. Happy hour is 6–8pm, and groups of 4–6 can ask about the bookable lab room.
  - Hours: Daily 4:30pm–1:30am
  - Address: 1st floor, alley 136 Lê Thánh Tôn, Bến Thành, District 1
  - Price: Cocktails from about 350k VND (Vietcetera); 50 Best Discovery puts the average at ~US$12.
  - [Google Maps](https://www.google.com/maps/search/?api=1&query=Stir%20Modern%20Classic%20Cocktail%2C%20136%20L%C3%AA%20Th%C3%A1nh%20T%C3%B4n%2C%20District%201%2C%20Ho%20Chi%20Minh%20City) · [Source](https://www.tatlerasia.com/dining/stir-modern-classic-cocktail-bar-vn-en)
- **★ Nhau Nhau** · District 1 (Tôn Thất Đạm wet market) · $$ · *checked 2026*
  - Chef Peter Cuong Franklin's jade-tiled tribute to 1960s Saigon rock and soul, and to nhậu, Vietnam's after-work drinking culture, sits in a wet-market tube house above his Michelin-starred Anan. It has the most playfully Vietnamese menu in town, from a phở cocktail to rice-wine shots with fried crickets.
  - Tip: Order the Phở Cocktail (with a smoking cinnamon stick) or the Tamarind Whisky Sour. The brave take a shot of Quy Nhơn's Bàu Đá rice wine with a fried cricket, or the blow-torched coconut-worm shot, and bánh xèo tacos soak it up. Closed Mondays.
  - Hours: Tue–Sun from 5pm; closed Mon
  - Address: 2nd floor, 89 Tôn Thất Đạm, Bến Nghé, District 1 (above Anan Saigon)
  - Price: 50 Best Discovery puts the average cocktail at ~US$5.
  - [Google Maps](https://www.google.com/maps/search/?api=1&query=Nhau%20Nhau%2C%2089%20T%C3%B4n%20Th%E1%BA%A5t%20%C4%90%E1%BA%A1m%2C%20District%201%2C%20Ho%20Chi%20Minh%20City) · [Source](https://www.the50.com/discovery/Establishments/Vietnam/Ho-Chi-Minh-City/Nhau-Nhau.html)
- **Summer Experiment** · District 1 (Bến Thành, Lý Tự Trọng) · $$$ · *checked 2026*
  - Up a hidden staircase near Bến Thành Market, Jay Moir's garden bar wraps living walls and home-grown herbs around some 30 playful, sustainability-minded cocktails made from local produce. It ranked No.77 on Asia's 50 Best Bars 2021 and is on Tatler's Best 20 Bars of Vietnam 2026.
  - Tip: Share the 365 Days of Summer, poured from a watering can, or try the Milk Batch Baby, a nitro-frozen boozy ice cream with gin and yuzu. Come at happy hour, then head downstairs to the COI jazz bar. No smoking indoors.
  - Hours: Mon–Thu & Sun 4pm–1am; Fri–Sat 4pm–2am
  - Address: 2nd floor, 77–79 Lý Tự Trọng, Bến Thành, District 1
  - Price: Happy-hour cocktails ~99–100k VND (around 5–8pm).
  - [Google Maps](https://www.google.com/maps/search/?api=1&query=Summer%20Experiment%2C%2077%20L%C3%BD%20T%E1%BB%B1%20Tr%E1%BB%8Dng%2C%20District%201%2C%20Ho%20Chi%20Minh%20City) · [Source](https://www.the50.com/discovery/Establishments/Vietnam/Ho-Chi-Minh-City/Summer-Experiment.html)
- **The Triệu Institute** · District 1 (Bến Nghé) · *checked 2026*
  - The home bar of Lady Triệu Gin, distilled in Củ Chi from Vietnamese botanicals and named after the third-century warrior heroine Bà Triệu. A mostly female team runs two floors behind antique elephants and glowing red arches, and upstairs a rotavap works on the next batch of experimental botanicals.
  - Tip: Drink your way around Vietnam with the regional gin serves (Sapa Citrus Tea, Hoi An Spice Road, Dalat Flowerbomb, Mekong Delta Dry), or taste the Lady Triệu gins side by side. Downstairs, pair them with the Vietnamese sharing plates.
  - Hours: Mon–Thu & Sun 5pm–12am; Fri–Sat 5pm–1am
  - Address: 10 Mạc Thị Bưởi, Bến Nghé, District 1
  - [Google Maps](https://www.google.com/maps/search/?api=1&query=The%20Tri%E1%BB%87u%20Institute%2C%2010%20M%E1%BA%A1c%20Th%E1%BB%8B%20B%C6%B0%E1%BB%9Fi%2C%20District%201%2C%20Ho%20Chi%20Minh%20City) · [Source](https://thedotmagazine.com/the-ladies-powering-lady-trieu-gin-and-the-trieu-institute/)
- **Layla – Eatery & Bar** · District 1 (Bến Nghé, Đông Du) · $$$ · *checked 2026*
  - One of the pioneers of Saigon's craft-cocktail scene and Summer Experiment's older sibling, Layla is a big, buzzy upstairs room with an 11-metre bar, cocktails on tap, a pool table and a smoking terrace. It's on Tatler's Best 20 Bars of Vietnam 2026.
  - Tip: Try the Ant No Hard With This Margarita (overripe mango cordial, roasted bell-pepper oil, spicy ant-salt rim) or From Land 'N' Sea (tequila, soursop, samphire salt). Happy-hour cocktails are 99k VND; book at weekends.
  - Hours: Daily from 4pm until 1am (2am Fri–Sat)
  - Address: 2nd floor, 63 Đông Du, Bến Nghé, District 1
  - Price: Happy-hour cocktails 99k VND; signatures from about 350k (Vietcetera).
  - [Google Maps](https://www.google.com/maps/search/?api=1&query=Layla%20Eatery%20%26%20Bar%2C%2063%20%C4%90%C3%B4ng%20Du%2C%20District%201%2C%20Ho%20Chi%20Minh%20City) · [Source](https://thedotmagazine.com/the-best-cocktail-bars-in-ho-chi-minh-city/)
- **Emme House (Emme Bar)** · District 1 (Bến Nghé, Hàm Nghi) · *checked 2025*
  - A hidden apartment bar whose cocktail menu follows the stages of love, with original poems by resident poet Tín on the menu covers: about ten on the tables at any time, with new ones every two weeks. Owner Trực, founder of Red5 Studio and a.dau Kitchen, keeps the room intimate, with drinks made in full view at a long communal table.
  - Tip: Look for the discreet red-and-yellow sign, climb the dim stairs and ring the bell. Order Love Is a Game (vodka, mango, chili syrup, kombucha vinegar) or Just the Two of Us (Vietnamese sake, watermelon, raspberry, mắc khén tincture), and read the poem on your menu.
  - Hours: Daily 6pm–12am (Google listing via Wanderlog)
  - Address: Upstairs at 70 Hàm Nghi, Bến Nghé ward (District 1)
  - Price: Prices not published
  - [Google Maps](https://www.google.com/maps/search/?api=1&query=Emme%20House%2070%20H%C3%A0m%20Nghi%20District%201%20Ho%20Chi%20Minh%20City) · [Source](https://saigoneer.com/saigon-street-food-restaurants/28026-hẻm-gems-a-night-of-love,-poetry,-and-the-pursuit-of-the-sublime-at-emme-bar)
- **Ủ Bar** · Tân Định (District 1) · $$$ · *checked 2026*
  - A small speakeasy above Miên restaurant whose name means 'to ferment or steep': its cocktails are built on house-fermented Vietnamese ingredients and personal memories. Tatler named it a Best-in-Class bar in 2025 and put it on its Best 20 Bars of Vietnam 2026.
  - Tip: Book ahead, sit at the counter and tell the team what you like; the house signature is the Ủ Blossom. It's a calm, conversation-paced alternative to the downtown strip.
  - Hours: Tue–Sun 6pm–12am; closed Mon
  - Address: 2nd floor, 50A Đặng Dung, Tân Định, District 1 (above Miên restaurant)
  - Price: Cocktails from 260k VND (Feb 2025 listing).
  - [Google Maps](https://www.google.com/maps/search/?api=1&query=%E1%BB%A6%20Bar%2C%2050A%20%C4%90%E1%BA%B7ng%20Dung%2C%20T%C3%A2n%20%C4%90%E1%BB%8Bnh%2C%20Ho%20Chi%20Minh%20City) · [Source](https://www.tatlerasia.com/dining/drinks/tatler-best-of-vietnam-2026-best-20-bars-vn)
- **Le Foyer Bar** · District 3 (Lê Văn Sỹ) · *checked 2026*
  - Bartender Phạm Hồng Toàn opened this darkly lit counter bar down a Lê Văn Sỹ alley over the 2025 holidays, and The Dot picked it among the year's best openings for its 'theatrical cocktail precision'. The room is moody and spare, so nothing distracts from the show behind the bar.
  - Tip: Take a counter seat and watch Toàn work. It's small, less than a year old and publishes no hours, so check @lefoyerbar on Instagram and message ahead before riding over.
  - Hours: Not published; check @lefoyerbar on Instagram
  - Address: 297/22 Lê Văn Sỹ, District 3
  - Price: Prices not published
  - [Google Maps](https://www.google.com/maps/search/?api=1&query=Le%20Foyer%20Bar%20297/22%20L%C3%AA%20V%C4%83n%20S%E1%BB%B9%20District%203%20Ho%20Chi%20Minh%20City) · [Source](https://thedotmagazine.com/the-best-new-places-to-eat-and-drink-in-saigon-the-class-of-2025/)

#### Rooftops & hotel bars

- **★ Saigon Saigon Rooftop Bar (Caravelle Saigon)** · District 1 (Lam Sơn Square, by the Opera House) · $$$ · *checked 2026*
  - The Caravelle opened on Christmas Eve 1959, and its rooftop bar above the Opera House was where foreign correspondents drank through the Vietnam War. Freshly renovated, it reopened on 28 September 2026 with a sunset cocktail trolley and a live band every night.
  - Tip: Arrive around 5:45pm for the 'Ritual 1959' live cocktail trolley (with a DJ) as the sun goes down, then stay for the band from 8pm. Book a rail-side table through the hotel (+84 28 3823 4999); happy-hour deals in pre-2026 listicles may not have survived the reopening.
  - Music: Live band Mon–Sat 8–10:30pm and Sun 8–10pm; DJ with the cocktail trolley daily 5:45–7:45pm.
  - Hours: Daily 4pm–late
  - Address: 10th floor, Caravelle Saigon, 19–23 Lam Sơn Square (Công trường Lam Sơn), Sài Gòn Ward, District 1
  - Price: Current prices aren't published online; expect hotel '++' pricing (service charge and VAT added).
  - [Google Maps](https://www.google.com/maps/search/?api=1&query=Saigon%20Saigon%20Rooftop%20Bar%2C%20Caravelle%20Saigon%2C%2019%20Lam%20S%C6%A1n%20Square%2C%20District%201%2C%20Ho%20Chi%20Minh%20City) · [Source](https://www.caravellehotel.com/saigon-saigon-bar/)
- **M Bar (Hotel Majestic Saigon)** · District 1 (Đồng Khởi riverfront) · $$$ · *checked 2026*
  - On top of the 1925 Hotel Majestic at the river end of Đồng Khởi, M Bar has the best Saigon River view of the colonial-era hotel bars: boats sliding by at sunset, then a live band every night.
  - Tip: Ask for a river-facing table around 5:30pm and order the Majestic 1925 (orange-and-cinnamon-infused bourbon, Campari, sweet vermouth). The band starts at 8pm; dress smart casual.
  - Music: Live band nightly from 8pm (listings mention Filipino and flamenco bands).
  - Hours: Daily 3pm–12am
  - Address: 8th floor (New Wing), Hotel Majestic Saigon, 1 Đồng Khởi, District 1
  - Price: Cocktails 250–280k VND (signature Majestic 1925 280k), mocktails 250k, house wine 240k a glass, beer mostly 180k (250ml cans 140k, large draught 250k), soft drinks 160k, coffee 180k; plus service charge and VAT (M Bar drink list, May 2026)
  - [Google Maps](https://www.google.com/maps/search/?api=1&query=M%20Bar%2C%20Hotel%20Majestic%20Saigon%2C%201%20%C4%90%E1%BB%93ng%20Kh%E1%BB%9Fi%2C%20District%201%2C%20Ho%20Chi%20Minh%20City) · [Source](https://www.majesticsaigon.com/our-restaurant-bar/m-bar/)
- **Social Club Rooftop Bar (Hôtel des Arts Saigon)** · District 3 (Nguyễn Thị Minh Khai) · $$$ · *checked 2026*
  - An open-air Art Deco terrace 24 floors up, beside the hotel's rooftop infinity pool, with one of the city's best-value sunset happy hours. It won Best Rooftop Bar at the Gourmet Vietnam Awards 2025.
  - Tip: Arrive mid-afternoon for the daily happy hour, which runs until sunset (listed as half price 3–7pm), and grab a skyline-facing sofa. The pool is for hotel guests only. Tuesday is the Sapphire Hour ladies' night.
  - Music: DJs in the evening; Tuesday ladies' night; 'Social Ugly' party on the fourth Friday of each month; White Pool Party on the second Saturday, November–April.
  - Hours: Sun–Thu 3pm–12am; Fri–Sat 3pm–1am
  - Address: 24th floor, Hôtel des Arts Saigon – MGallery, 76–78 Nguyễn Thị Minh Khai, Xuân Hòa Ward (District 3)
  - Price: Drinks about 160–300k VND; half price at happy hour.
  - [Google Maps](https://www.google.com/maps/search/?api=1&query=Social%20Club%20Rooftop%20Bar%2C%20H%C3%B4tel%20des%20Arts%20Saigon%2C%2076%20Nguy%E1%BB%85n%20Th%E1%BB%8B%20Minh%20Khai%2C%20Ho%20Chi%20Minh%20City) · [Source](https://epicurevietnam.com/bars-clubs-1/saigon-1/a-rooftop-that-listens-to-the-city)

#### Craft beer

- **★ Heart of Darkness Brewery (Saigon Taproom)** · District 1 (Lý Tự Trọng) · $$ · *checked 2026*
  - Vietnam's benchmark craft brewery pours a deep, rotating list of its own small-batch IPAs, sours and specials at its District 1 taproom and beer garden. Its annual IPA Week is a beer-geek event: the August 2026 edition released eight collab IPAs with brewers from Hong Kong to the Cayman Islands.
  - Tip: Walk-ins are welcome, but book at weekends. Start with the Dream Sensations Hazy IPA and ask what's fresh from the small-batch tanks. Flying out? It also pours at Tân Sơn Nhất airport.
  - Hours: Daily 11am–12am
  - Address: 31D Lý Tự Trọng, Sài Gòn Ward, District 1
  - Price: The brewery's online shop lists its beers at 50–80k VND (BiA lager to Dream Sensations Hazy IPA).
  - [Google Maps](https://www.google.com/maps/search/?api=1&query=Heart%20of%20Darkness%20Craft%20Brewery%2C%2031D%20L%C3%BD%20T%E1%BB%B1%20Tr%E1%BB%8Dng%2C%20District%201%2C%20Ho%20Chi%20Minh%20City) · [Source](https://heartofdarknessbrewery.com/pages/taproom)
- **Pasteur Street Craft Beer (Original taproom & Đồng Khởi flagship)** · District 1 · $$ · *checked 2026*
  - Vietnam's craft-beer pioneer has brewed with local ingredients since 2015, from its Jasmine IPA to Passion Fruit Wheat. The small Original taproom down an alley off Pasteur street is the pilgrimage; the Đồng Khởi flagship, opened in May 2026, pours 16 taps and six-beer flights.
  - Tip: Order a six-beer flight at the flagship, or go to the Original for the history. If you end up on Bùi Viện, its taproom at 212 Đề Thám is the best beer on the strip and stays open until 2am on Fri–Sat.
  - Music: Weekly live music (Oct 2026 calendar): Thảo Điền taproom (120 Xuân Thủy) on Fridays and Saturdays; Lê Thánh Tôn (26A) and Ngô Thời Nhiệm (23A) taprooms on Saturdays.
  - Hours: Original: Mon–Thu & Sun 11am–11pm, Fri–Sat 11am–12am. Đồng Khởi flagship: daily 11am–12am.
  - Address: Original: 144/3 Pasteur, Sài Gòn Ward; flagship: 8 Đồng Khởi at the corner of Ngô Đức Kế (inside Grand Hotel Saigon)
  - Price: Six-beer flights at the flagship.
  - [Google Maps](https://www.google.com/maps/search/?api=1&query=Pasteur%20Street%20Brewing%20Company%2C%20144%20Pasteur%2C%20District%201%2C%20Ho%20Chi%20Minh%20City) · [Source](https://www.pasteurstreet.com/taprooms)
- **BiaCraft Xuân Thủy** · Thảo Điền (Thủ Đức, former District 2) · $$ · *checked 2026*
  - BiaCraft's original taproom is a loud, graffiti-walled terrace with 30 rotating taps of house and Vietnamese guest beers, and the anchor of any crawl along Thảo Điền's Xuân Thủy strip.
  - Tip: Go for 'Toss the Boss' (5–6pm daily): call the coin toss right and your drink is free. If you're staying central, BiaCraft Lê Ngô Cát (1 Lê Ngô Cát, District 3) has about 50 taps and air-con; Pasteur Street's Thảo Điền taproom (120 Xuân Thủy) is on the same street.
  - Hours: Mon–Thu & Sun 11am–12am; Fri–Sat 11am–1am
  - Address: 90 Xuân Thủy, An Khánh Ward (Thảo Điền)
  - Price: 'Toss the Boss' happy hour daily 5–6pm.
  - [Google Maps](https://www.google.com/maps/search/?api=1&query=BiaCraft%20Xu%C3%A2n%20Th%E1%BB%A7y%2C%2090%20Xu%C3%A2n%20Th%E1%BB%A7y%2C%20Th%E1%BA%A3o%20%C4%90i%E1%BB%81n%2C%20Ho%20Chi%20Minh%20City) · [Source](https://biacraft.com/)

#### Nhậu & street drinking

- **★ Ốc Oanh (Vĩnh Khánh food street)** (Ốc Oanh – phố ẩm thực Vĩnh Khánh) · District 4 (Khánh Hội) · $$ · *checked 2026*
  - Vĩnh Khánh, a kilometre of snail and seafood joints that Time Out named one of the world's coolest streets in 2025, is where Saigon does nhậu at full volume, and Michelin-listed Ốc Oanh is its star. Expect plastic stools, buckets of iced beer, salt-roasted crab claws, grilled prawns and snails cooked every way.
  - Tip: Come after 7pm, when the street hits full swing (weekends are busiest), share several plates and bring cash. Ốc Oanh is the busiest and most touristy stall, so if it's full, plenty of its neighbours are just as good. For 12–20k VND draught, try the local beer joints on Hoàng Diệu near the Khánh Hội bridge.
  - Hours: Daily from 1pm until late (listings vary from midnight to 3am)
  - Address: 534 Vĩnh Khánh, Khánh Hội Ward (District 4)
  - Price: Snail plates from about 100k VND, grilled crab claws 200–500k (Jul 2026 guide); cash only.
  - [Google Maps](https://www.google.com/maps/search/?api=1&query=%E1%BB%90c%20Oanh%2C%20534%20V%C4%A9nh%20Kh%C3%A1nh%2C%20District%204%2C%20Ho%20Chi%20Minh%20City) · [Source](https://guide.michelin.com/en/ho-chi-minh-region/ho-chi-minh/restaurant/oc-oanh)
- **Hoàng Sa canal-side nhậu strip** (Bờ kè Hoàng Sa) · Tân Định / Đa Kao (District 1) · $ · *checked 2026*
  - In the evenings the embankment along the Nhiêu Lộc–Thị Nghè canal fills with nhậu tables serving tamarind crab, grilled squid, snails and hotpot: the easiest place in central Saigon to drink like a local by the water. Pasteur Street's 140-seat Hoàng Sa taproom is on the same street.
  - Tip: Start with a pint at Pasteur Street Hoàng Sa (221), then walk along the canal to Ngon Bờ Kè – Hải Sản Hùng 33 (109A–117) for tamarind crab and grilled squid, or Thế Giới Ốc – 5Ku (119) for snails. Trường Sa, on the opposite bank, has more of the same.
  - Hours: Most nhậu spots open mid-afternoon until midnight or 1am. Pasteur Street Hoàng Sa: Mon–Thu & Sun 11am–11pm, Fri–Sat 11am–12am.
  - Address: Hoàng Sa street along the Nhiêu Lộc–Thị Nghè canal, e.g. Ngon Bờ Kè – Hải Sản Hùng 33 at 109A–117 Hoàng Sa and Pasteur Street's Hoàng Sa taproom at 221 Hoàng Sa
  - Price: Nhậu dishes about 70–180k VND each (2025 listings).
  - [Google Maps](https://www.google.com/maps/search/?api=1&query=Ho%C3%A0ng%20Sa%2C%20T%C3%A2n%20%C4%90%E1%BB%8Bnh%2C%20Qu%E1%BA%ADn%201%2C%20Ho%20Chi%20Minh%20City) · [Source](https://mia.vn/cam-nang-du-lich/quan-nhau-bo-ke-cuc-chill-19637)

#### Wine bars

- **Lửa** · Thảo Điền (Thủ Đức, former District 2) · *checked 2026*
  - A Japanese chef-and-sommelier duo built this leafy courtyard bar around organic, biodynamic and natural wines (about 100 references, with a by-the-glass list that changes monthly) and seasonal plates from small local farms. It was added to the Michelin Guide in 2023 and won Star Wine List's Sustainability Prize for Southeast Asia in 2026.
  - Tip: Book ahead and order by the glass with the homemade charcuterie; the house-brewed sake is a fixture. One 2025 guide gives the address as 2 Street 1, so check the map pin before you ride over. For another glass nearby, Thirty 59 (30 Đường số 59) pours small-producer wines and MAD (63 Xuân Thủy) suits a long courtyard dinner.
  - Hours: Mon–Sat 6pm–11pm; closed Sun
  - Address: 2 Đường số 11, Thảo Điền
  - [Google Maps](https://www.google.com/maps/search/?api=1&query=L%E1%BB%ADa%20wine%20bar%2C%202%20%C4%90%C6%B0%E1%BB%9Dng%20s%E1%BB%91%2011%2C%20Th%E1%BA%A3o%20%C4%90i%E1%BB%81n%2C%20Ho%20Chi%20Minh%20City) · [Source](https://vietcetera.com/en/ho-chi-minh-citys-top-5-bars-for-wine-lovers)

#### Sake & shochu bars

- **Bar Mitsuboshi** · District 1 (Japan Town, Lê Thánh Tôn) · $$$ · *checked 2026*
  - Walk through a beef-bowl eatery in Japan Town, climb the stairs and open an old wooden door onto six counter stools and a deep list of sweet-potato, rice and barley shochu and Japanese whisky. Owner Chinh also pours a homemade 'Vietnamese shochu' from his hometown, Bà Rịa, made from sticky rice and rock sugar fermented in dried wild bananas, in a plain room built for conversation rather than show.
  - Tip: Ask Chinh, who speaks Japanese and English, for a glass of the house sticky-rice shochu, then explore the sweet-potato and barley lists. Most guests are Japanese and it runs the Japanese way, with a table fee that brings an otoshi snack; with only six seats, arrive at the 7pm opening.
  - Hours: 7pm–11pm
  - Address: Upstairs at 15B/16 Lê Thánh Tôn (above a beef-bowl eatery), Bến Nghé ward (District 1)
  - Price: About 200–500k VND per person (Saigoneer, Mar 2026); a table fee with an otoshi snack applies
  - [Google Maps](https://www.google.com/maps/search/?api=1&query=Bar%20Mitsuboshi%2015B/16%20L%C3%AA%20Th%C3%A1nh%20T%C3%B4n%20District%201%20Ho%20Chi%20Minh%20City) · [Source](https://saigoneer.com/saigon-street-food-restaurants/28835-hẻm-gems-atop-a-beef-bowl-eatery,-the-hidden-cozy-world-of-shochu-bar-mitsuboshi)
- **KURA Sake Bar** (蔵 KURA) · Bình Thạnh (alley 40 Phạm Viết Chánh) · *checked 2025*
  - Sana, from Nara Prefecture, runs this narrow alley bar on her own, stocking rare Japanese sakes she chooses herself and pairing them with small plates of aged, fermented and smoked food, because aged sake and aged food 'create an amazing chemistry'. It's a quiet, personal counter in a residential Bình Thạnh alley.
  - Tip: Tell Sana whether you like karakuchi (dry) or amakuchi (sweet) and let her choose; she saves the stranger, stronger sakes for later. It's tiny and closes at 11pm, so go early in the evening.
  - Hours: 6pm–11pm
  - Address: 40/28 Phạm Viết Chánh, Bình Thạnh
  - Price: Prices not published
  - [Google Maps](https://www.google.com/maps/search/?api=1&query=KURA%20bar%2040/28%20Ph%E1%BA%A1m%20Vi%E1%BA%BFt%20Ch%C3%A1nh%20B%C3%ACnh%20Th%E1%BA%A1nh%20Ho%20Chi%20Minh%20City) · [Source](https://saigoneer.com/saigon-street-food-restaurants/28062-hẻm-gems-kura-bar,-a-veritable-treasure-trove-of-rare-japanese-sakes-amid-saigon)

### Saigon: Music (Âm nhạc)

Saigon's music runs on two tracks. One is deeply local: phòng trà lounges where star vocalists sing bolero and Trịnh Công Sơn ballads to a hushed, seated room, and the South's own đờn ca tài tử and cải lương, which young musicians are now carrying into bars. The other is a scrappy DIY scene of jazz rooms, indie gig bars and underground techno crews spread across District 1, District 3, Bình Thạnh and Thảo Điền, where most nights are small, cheap and announced only days ahead.

#### Jazz & live bands

- **★ Saxn'Art Club** · Thảo Điền (An Khánh ward) · $$$ · *checked 2026*
  - Vietnam's best-known jazz club, run by Trần Mạnh Tuấn, one of the country's two leading jazz saxophonists, who folds Vietnamese melodies into bebop and contemporary jazz. It has left its famous Lê Lợi address downtown and now plays themed weekend shows in a Thảo Điền alley.
  - Tip: Open Fri–Sun only, with limited tables: book by phone (0938 903 888 / 0818 678 139) or Zalo (0772 993 781), and check saxnartclub.com for each night's theme, e.g. 'A Night of Jazz, Soul & Fusion' on Sat 10 Oct 2026. Allow 20–30 minutes by Grab from District 1, and ignore guides that still send you to 28 Lê Lợi, which is closed.
  - Music: Jazz, soul and Vietnamese-jazz fusion with Trần Mạnh Tuấn and resident vocalists; themed shows Fri–Sun, 9–11pm.
  - Hours: Fri–Sun from 8:30pm, shows 9–11pm; closed Mon–Thu
  - Address: BB4, Alley 188 Nguyễn Văn Hưởng, An Khánh ward (Thảo Điền)
  - Price: Cover includes one drink (amount not published; it was 200k VND at the old Lê Lợi club); cocktails are premium-priced
  - [Google Maps](https://www.google.com/maps/search/?api=1&query=Saxn%27Art%20Club%2C%20Alley%20188%20Nguy%E1%BB%85n%20V%C4%83n%20H%C6%B0%E1%BB%9Fng%2C%20Th%E1%BA%A3o%20%C4%90i%E1%BB%81n%2C%20Ho%20Chi%20Minh%20City) · [Source](https://saxnartclub.com/su-kien/)
- **Acoustic Bar** · District 3 (Xuân Hòa ward) · $$ · *checked 2026*
  - For nearly two decades Saigon's favourite sing-along live bar: a dim, neon-lit, tightly packed room where a house band and rotating local groups belt out rock classics, ballads and Vietnamese and English hits, sometimes with sax, to locals and travellers.
  - Tip: There's no cover; you just buy drinks. On Fri–Sat arrive before 9pm to get a seat. Make it a double bill with an original-band gig at Tone Lab in the same 6 Ngô Thời Nhiệm complex. Phone 0816 777 773.
  - Music: House band plus rotating local bands nightly: rock, pop, ballads and Vietnamese remixes. Sets from about 8:30–9pm (a January 2026 listing gives Thu–Sat 8:30pm).
  - Hours: Daily 7pm–midnight; bands usually start around 9–9:30pm
  - Address: 1st floor, 6E1 Ngô Thời Nhiệm, Xuân Hòa ward, District 3
  - Price: No cover; drinks roughly 70–200k VND (sources differ)
  - [Google Maps](https://www.google.com/maps/search/?api=1&query=Acoustic%20Bar%2C%206E1%20Ng%C3%B4%20Th%E1%BB%9Di%20Nhi%E1%BB%87m%2C%20District%203%2C%20Ho%20Chi%20Minh%20City) · [Source](https://wanderlog.com/place/details/1082544/acoustic-bar-ng%C3%B4-th%E1%BB%9Di-nhi%E1%BB%87m)

#### Phòng trà & salons

- **★ Không Tên Music Lounge** (Phòng trà Không Tên) · District 1 (Bến Thành ward) · $$$ · *checked 2026*
  - Saigon's best-known phòng trà, going for nearly two decades: a plush listening room where Vietnamese star vocalists sing ballads, bolero and pop to a live band rather than playback. It's the classic local night out that few visitors try.
  - Tip: Pick a night from phongtrakhongten.vn/lich-dien and book a table by phone (028 3827 3778) or Zalo (0867 611 911), asking for a central seat. Every show is themed, and titles of pre-1975 songs signal bolero and old-Saigon ballads. Dress smart-casual and treat it as a concert, not a bar.
  - Music: Live band with rotating Vietnamese headliners, 8–10pm: themed nights of ballads, bolero/nhạc vàng and pop. Upcoming as of 5 Oct 2026: 9–11, 16–18, 22 and 25 Oct, and 3 Nov.
  - Hours: Shows 8–10pm on scheduled nights
  - Address: 112 Lê Thánh Tôn, Bến Thành ward, District 1
  - Price: About 300k–800k VND on regular nights, 1–2M VND for star or special nights (Người Lao Động, May 2026)
  - [Google Maps](https://www.google.com/maps/search/?api=1&query=Ph%C3%B2ng%20tr%C3%A0%20Kh%C3%B4ng%20T%C3%AAn%2C%20112%20L%C3%AA%20Th%C3%A1nh%20T%C3%B4n%2C%20District%201%2C%20Ho%20Chi%20Minh%20City) · [Source](https://phongtrakhongten.vn/lich-dien/)
- **Bến Thành Music Lounge** (Phòng trà Bến Thành) · District 1 (Bến Thành Theatre) · $$$ · *checked 2026*
  - A phòng trà opened in November 2022 inside Bến Thành Theatre and styled on 1950s–60s 'Pearl of the Far East' Saigon. Vocalists such as Bùi Anh Tuấn, Myra Trần, Lân Nhã and Quang Dũng sing intimate, band-backed sets ranging from French-era romance songs to modern ballads.
  - Tip: Book as soon as the monthly line-up appears on its Facebook page, because star and charity nights sell out within days. Ask for a central table, as some seats are cramped or have poor sightlines. Non-headline nights are acoustic and cheaper.
  - Music: Evening shows: acoustic nights plus scheduled star nights of Vietnamese ballads, nhạc Trịnh, bolero, French chansons and contemporary pop.
  - Hours: Evening shows; schedule published monthly
  - Address: Bến Thành Theatre (upper floor), 6 Mạc Đĩnh Chi, District 1
  - Price: About 300k–800k VND on regular nights, 1–2M VND for big names (Người Lao Động, May 2026); drinks extra
  - [Google Maps](https://www.google.com/maps/search/?api=1&query=Ph%C3%B2ng%20tr%C3%A0%20B%E1%BA%BFn%20Th%C3%A0nh%2C%206%20M%E1%BA%A1c%20%C4%90%C4%A9nh%20Chi%2C%20District%201%2C%20Ho%20Chi%20Minh%20City) · [Source](https://thanhnien.vn/phong-tra-ben-thanh-diem-hen-am-nhac-moi-tai-tphcm-1851516716.htm)
- **Cà Phê Thứ Bảy (Saturday Coffee)** (Cà Phê Thứ Bảy – Trung Nguyên Legend) · District 1 (Sài Gòn ward) · $ · *checked 2026*
  - A non-profit Saturday music salon that composer Dương Thụ has founded and hosted for over a decade. Classically trained singers and pianists perform 20th-century Vietnamese art songs alongside Mozart or Puccini, with the composer's own commentary: a rare, low-cost way into Vietnam's art-song tradition.
  - Tip: Doors open at 7pm and the music starts at 8pm. There's no ticket: order from the café menu and put at least 50k VND into the artists' fund. Talks are in Vietnamese, so check the week's programme on Saigoneer's events page. Sessions now run in the former Phòng trà Đồng Dao space.
  - Music: Saturdays at 8pm: piano-accompanied Vietnamese chamber-style songs and classical repertoire, introduced by Dương Thụ.
  - Hours: Saturdays: doors 7pm, music 8pm
  - Address: Trung Nguyên Legend Coffee World, 164 Pasteur, Sài Gòn ward, District 1
  - Price: Free entry; buy a drink and give at least 50k VND to the artists' fund
  - [Google Maps](https://www.google.com/maps/search/?api=1&query=Trung%20Nguy%C3%AAn%20Legend%20Coffee%20World%2C%20164%20Pasteur%2C%20District%201%2C%20Ho%20Chi%20Minh%20City) · [Source](https://www.saigoneer.com/saigon-events/28944-slan-tiếng-sáo-thiên-thai-a-selection-of-100-vietnamese-chamber-style-songs-of-the-20th-century-classical-music-trung-nguyên-legend-coffee-world)
- **Cat&Mouse Live Music** · District 3 (Xuân Hòa ward) · $$$$ · *checked 2026*
  - A ticketed music lounge that opened with rock-band nights in September 2025 and now puts top V-pop and ballad singers in a close-up room almost every weekend. Its 2026 bills have included Đàm Vĩnh Hưng, Hà Nhi, Erik, Văn Mai Hương, Trung Quân and Bùi Anh Tuấn. It costs more than Không Tên and Bến Thành and books bigger names.
  - Tip: Buy on Ticketbox, where each show has Standard, VIP and Super VIP tiers. Seats are allocated at random within the tier you buy, and the ticket covers VAT but not food or drinks. The biggest names sell fast: online sales for the 8 Nov show and the first Thanh Duy night had already closed by 6 Oct.
  - Music: Headline sets by Vietnamese pop and ballad singers, 20:00–23:00. Coming up: Hoàng Hải + Myra Trần (Fri 9 Oct), Tăng Phúc + Phạm Quỳnh Anh (Sat 24 Oct), Phương Linh + Dee Trần (Sun 25 Oct), Thanh Duy + Phạm Trần Phương (Fri 30 Oct), Thành Vá (Sat 7 Nov) and Neko Lê + Mew Amazing (Sun 8 Nov).
  - Hours: Show nights 20:00–23:00, mostly Fri–Sun; next dates 9, 24, 25 and 30 Oct and 7–8 Nov 2026
  - Address: 37B Phạm Ngọc Thạch, Xuân Hòa ward, District 3
  - Price: Standard 600,000–900,000 VND, VIP 800,000–1,800,000, Super VIP 1,000,000–2,000,000, depending on the night (Ticketbox, Oct 2026); food and drinks extra
  - [Google Maps](https://www.google.com/maps/search/?api=1&query=Cat%26Mouse%20Live%20Music%2C%2037B%20Ph%E1%BA%A1m%20Ng%E1%BB%8Dc%20Th%E1%BA%A1ch%2C%20District%203%2C%20Ho%20Chi%20Minh%20City) · [Source](https://ticketbox.vn/catmouse-tang-phuc-pham-quynh-anh-26618)

#### Indie gigs & clubs

- **★ The Observatory** · District 1 (Bến Thành ward) · $$ · *checked 2026*
  - Saigon's flagship underground club since 2013, with a club room and an open-air garden lounge. Since The Lighthouse closed in October 2025 it has been the city's anchor for techno, house and leftfield, hosting Vietnamese crews such as Arcan, Lệch and Snug Night alongside touring DJs.
  - Tip: Fri–Sat are the big dance nights; Thursdays and Sundays are looser. Check RA's Ho Chi Minh City guide for the weekend's crew takeover and buy early-bird tickets, as some parties drop the door price after 3:30am. The club has moved several times, so ignore older addresses.
  - Music: Techno, house, electro, ambient and leftfield; parties typically 10pm–6am (e.g. Arcan invites Messiahwaits with Goss, ILONA, Phát Paris and Smoothie Boiz, Fri 22 May 2026).
  - Hours: Thu & Sun about 8pm–3am, Fri–Sat about 8pm–6am, closed Mon–Wed (Google listing; check RA for the night)
  - Address: 85 Cách Mạng Tháng 8, Bến Thành ward, District 1
  - Price: Big parties about 200k VND early bird / 300k at the door / 100k after 3:30am (May 2026)
  - [Google Maps](https://www.google.com/maps/search/?api=1&query=The%20Observatory%2C%2085%20C%C3%A1ch%20M%E1%BA%A1ng%20Th%C3%A1ng%208%2C%20District%201%2C%20Ho%20Chi%20Minh%20City) · [Source](https://ra.co/guide/vn/hochiminhcity)
- **★ Yoko Café** · District 3 (Xuân Hòa ward) · $$ · *checked 2026*
  - A cosy, wood-panelled bar named after Yoko Ono that has anchored Saigon's rock and indie scene for two decades. Its multi-band bills mix Vietnamese and expat acts in front of a friendly crowd of locals and expats.
  - Tip: Check Saigoneer's events calendar or Yoko's Facebook for the week's line-up, and buy the pre-sale ticket through the Google Form in the listing (about 50k cheaper than the door). Openers go on around 8:30pm and headliners around 11pm.
  - Music: Live bands mainly Thu–Sat from about 8:30–9:30pm: rock, indie, punk and blues. Ticketed showcases such as All Mixed Up Vol.10 (Anesthetic Youth, XOI, Neon White, Party Mums; Sat 3 Oct 2026), 'Solo Sessions' singer-songwriter nights and the odd Sunday-afternoon fest.
  - Hours: Gig nights usually 8pm to about 11:30pm; main live nights Thu–Sat
  - Address: 22A Nguyễn Thị Diệu, Xuân Hòa ward, District 3
  - Price: Showcases 150k VND pre-sale / 200k at the door; some nights about 70k cover; drinks are affordable
  - [Google Maps](https://www.google.com/maps/search/?api=1&query=Yoko%20Cafe%2C%2022A%20Nguy%E1%BB%85n%20Th%E1%BB%8B%20Di%E1%BB%87u%2C%20District%203%2C%20Ho%20Chi%20Minh%20City) · [Source](https://www.saigoneer.com/saigon-events/29235-all-mixed-up-vol-10-yoko-cafe)
- **Luu Bar (Luu Club)** · Bình Thạnh (Thạnh Mỹ Tây ward) · $$$ · *checked 2026*
  - A neighbourhood cocktail bar co-owned by DJ Ouch! that turns into a packed mini-club every Friday and Saturday, booking local and Asia-touring house and techno DJs. It's one of the busiest rooms on RA's Saigon listings.
  - Tip: Wednesday's 'Open CUE' open decks and Thursday's 'Luu Session' are quieter nights to try the umeboshi highball or smoky mezcal Negroni before the weekend crush. Weekend Luu Club entry has usually been free.
  - Music: Luu Club every Fri & Sat 10pm–5am: house, techno and electro (e.g. DJ Ouch!, Tommysarng and Moose Man, 4 Jul 2026); Wed open decks; Thu Luu Session.
  - Hours: Luu Club Fri–Sat 10pm–5am; Wed and Thu event nights
  - Address: 108 Phạm Viết Chánh, Thạnh Mỹ Tây ward, Bình Thạnh
  - Price: Signature cocktails 190–350k VND, classics 190–250k, Sapporo draught 100k (March 2026 menu); weekend entry usually free
  - [Google Maps](https://www.google.com/maps/search/?api=1&query=Luu%20Bar%2C%20108%20Ph%E1%BA%A1m%20Vi%E1%BA%BFt%20Ch%C3%A1nh%2C%20B%C3%ACnh%20Th%E1%BA%A1nh%2C%20Ho%20Chi%20Minh%20City) · [Source](https://www.saigoneer.com/saigon-events/28892-luu-club-maggie-tra,-yasmin,-levisng-luu-bar)
- **Blue Monkey Bar Saigon** · Thảo Điền (An Khánh ward) · $$ · *checked 2026*
  - The heaviest stage in town: home of the monthly X-Metal showcase and No Headliner alt-electronic parties, and the room touring acts now play. Sydney hardcore band Speed made their Vietnam debut here in June 2026.
  - Tip: Buy pre-sale through the Google Form in the Saigoneer listing to save about 70k; X-Metal tickets include a drink. It's on the same street as Saxn'Art, 20–30 minutes by Grab from District 1.
  - Music: Monthly X-Metal (death, black, grind; 8pm); No Headliner alt-electronic parties (5–11pm); tribute nights; touring hardcore and reggae acts (ragga/jungle MC General Levy is booked for 9 Oct 2026).
  - Hours: Show nights, usually from 7–8pm
  - Address: 199M Nguyễn Văn Hưởng, An Khánh ward (Thảo Điền)
  - Price: Local bills 200–350k VND (X-Metal includes a drink); touring acts 600–800k VND
  - [Google Maps](https://www.google.com/maps/search/?api=1&query=Blue%20Monkey%20Bar%20Saigon%2C%20199M%20Nguy%E1%BB%85n%20V%C4%83n%20H%C6%B0%E1%BB%9Fng%2C%20Th%E1%BA%A3o%20%C4%90i%E1%BB%81n%2C%20Ho%20Chi%20Minh%20City) · [Source](https://saigoneer.com/saigon-events/29184-x-metal-golden-blood-blue-monkey-saigon)

#### Traditional music & stage shows

- **★ AO Show at the Saigon Opera House (Lune Production)** (À Ố Show – Nhà hát Thành phố Hồ Chí Minh) · District 1 (Sài Gòn ward, Lam Sơn Square) · $$$$ · *checked 2026*
  - Lune Production's hour-long bamboo circus sets quiet village life against the racing city through acrobatics, contemporary dance and on-stage musicians whose score echoes Southern work songs, all inside the 1900 French-colonial Opera House. It is now the only Lune show resident in Saigon.
  - Tip: Book on luneproduction.com (online sales close 2 hours before curtain) or at the box office a few days ahead, as some nights nearly sell out; the middle rows of the stalls give the best value. Arrive by about 17:30 and bring a layer for the strong air-con. Photos and video are banned.
  - Music: Live musicians on traditional instruments, with a score drawn from Southern work songs and folk melodies; about 1 hour, no interval.
  - Hours: 18:00 on most nights, mainly Mon–Fri plus some weekends; October 2026 dates: 5–9, 11–16, 18–23 and 26–29 Oct. No show when HBSO or other events use the house
  - Address: Saigon Opera House, 7 Công Trường Lam Sơn, Sài Gòn ward, District 1
  - Price: 800,000 / 1,250,000 / 1,750,000 VND by seat zone (official site, Oct 2026)
  - [Google Maps](https://www.google.com/maps/search/?api=1&query=Saigon%20Opera%20House%2C%207%20C%C3%B4ng%20Tr%C6%B0%E1%BB%9Dng%20Lam%20S%C6%A1n%2C%20District%201%2C%20Ho%20Chi%20Minh%20City) · [Source](https://luneproduction.com/ao-show)
- **Trần Hữu Trang Cải Lương Theatre** (Nhà hát Cải lương Trần Hữu Trang – Rạp Hưng Đạo) · District 1 (Bến Thành ward, Phạm Ngũ Lão side) · $$ · *checked 2026*
  - Home of the city's state cải lương company, which stages full productions of the South's own sung theatre, built on đờn ca tài tử melodies and the vọng cổ aria, with stars such as NSND Quế Trân and NSND Mỹ Hằng for devoted local audiences.
  - Tip: New productions go on sale on Ticketbox a few weeks ahead, roughly once a month, and 200k balcony seats are a bargain. Shows are in Vietnamese, so read the synopsis first; comic pieces such as 'Gánh cải trạng nguyên' are the easiest way in. Clapping mid-song after a well-sung vọng cổ phrase is normal.
  - Music: Full-length cải lương with live traditional accompaniment. Recent: 'Câu thơ yên ngựa' (Fri 18 Sep 2026, 20:00) and 'Gánh cải trạng nguyên' (Sat 30 May 2026).
  - Hours: 20:00 on announced dates, usually a Friday or Saturday
  - Address: 136 Trần Hưng Đạo, Bến Thành ward, District 1
  - Price: Balcony 200,000 VND; stalls 400,000–1,200,000 VND (Sep 2026 production)
  - [Google Maps](https://www.google.com/maps/search/?api=1&query=Nh%C3%A0%20h%C3%A1t%20Tr%E1%BA%A7n%20H%E1%BB%AFu%20Trang%2C%20136%20Tr%E1%BA%A7n%20H%C6%B0ng%20%C4%90%E1%BA%A1o%2C%20District%201%2C%20Ho%20Chi%20Minh%20City) · [Source](https://ticketbox.vn/vo-cai-luong-cau-tho-yen-ngua-1005-24105)
- **Hai Tô Bar** · Bình Thạnh (Thạnh Mỹ Tây ward) · *checked 2026*
  - A small Mekong-themed bar where the young collective Đờn Ca Đó Đây plays đàn kìm (moon lute) and đàn tranh (zither) and sings đờn ca tài tử and cải lương while you sip drinks made with Southern fruits. It's a rare chance to hear the UNESCO-listed music in a bar rather than on a formal stage.
  - Tip: Go on a Saturday night and order something made with trái giác (wild grape) or chùm ruột (star gooseberry). The troupe calls itself nomadic and its sets are informal, so check Đờn Ca Đó Đây's social posts before setting out, and search the bar's name on Maps since no street number is published.
  - Music: Saturday nights: live đờn ca tài tử and cải lương by Đờn Ca Đó Đây, with regulars stepping up to sing a lý or vọng cổ.
  - Hours: Saturday nights (start time not published)
  - Address: Huỳnh Mẫn Đạt St., Thạnh Mỹ Tây ward, Bình Thạnh (no street number published)
  - [Google Maps](https://www.google.com/maps/search/?api=1&query=Hai%20T%C3%B4%20Bar%2C%20Hu%E1%BB%B3nh%20M%E1%BA%ABn%20%C4%90%E1%BA%A1t%2C%20B%C3%ACnh%20Th%E1%BA%A1nh%2C%20Ho%20Chi%20Minh%20City) · [Source](https://saigoneer.com/saigon-music-art/29082-from-tiktok-to-bars,-a-group-of-young-artists-brings-đờn-ca-tài-tử-to-unexpected-venues)
- **Chào Show – The Sound of Vietnam** · District 1 (Sài Gòn ward) · $$$$ · *checked 2026*
  - A polished 90-minute concert played entirely live on more than 30 traditional instruments, from drums and horns to lithophones and the bamboo t'rưng. Composer Trần Mạnh Hùng's 12-part suite 'Giang Sơn Cẩm Tú' travels from North to South, with immersive surround sound, a 180-degree LED screen and a regional tasting menu.
  - Tip: Book ahead on Ticketbox or through the show (+84 869 030 473). Tickets are non-refundable, so note any dietary needs when booking (a vegetarian menu is available). Dress smart-casual. Children under 100 cm aren't admitted, professional cameras are banned, and latecomers wait for a break between pieces.
  - Music: 100% live with no backing tracks: contemporary arrangements of Northern, Central and Southern folk material on 30+ traditional instruments, through an immersive surround-sound system; about 90 minutes.
  - Hours: Tue–Sun: check-in 18:45, food 19:00, show 19:15–20:45; closed Mondays
  - Address: 6 Nguyễn Siêu, Sài Gòn ward, District 1
  - Price: 1,300,000 VND including a regional tasting set and drinks (Ticketbox, Oct 2026)
  - [Google Maps](https://www.google.com/maps/search/?api=1&query=Ch%C3%A0o%20Show%2C%206%20Nguy%E1%BB%85n%20Si%C3%AAu%2C%20District%201%2C%20Ho%20Chi%20Minh%20City) · [Source](https://chaoshow.vn)
- **HBSO – Ho Chi Minh City Ballet Symphony Orchestra & Opera** (Nhà hát Giao hưởng Nhạc Vũ Kịch TP.HCM) · District 1 (Sài Gòn ward, Lam Sơn Square) · $$$ · *checked 2026*
  - The city's resident orchestra, ballet and opera company plays about two weekend programmes a month in the 468-seat Opera House, often pairing the classics with Vietnamese composers and soloists.
  - Tip: Buy on Ticketbox, since HBSO's own website was down in October 2026. The cheapest seats are fine in this small hall. Next up: Sat 10 Oct 2026, 20:00, 'A Night of Virtuoso Concertos', with Dvořák's Carnival Overture, Hoàng Cương's Violin Concerto (Stéphane Trần Ngọc) and Brahms' Piano Concerto No. 1 (Lưu Hồng Quang).
  - Music: Western classical, ballet and opera with a Vietnamese thread. The 2026 season has included Beethoven 9, a full Swan Lake (Aug) and Madama Butterfly in concert (Sep).
  - Hours: Sat or Sun at 20:00 (about 2 hours)
  - Address: Saigon Opera House, 7 Công Trường Lam Sơn, Sài Gòn ward, District 1
  - Price: Concerts 650,000–850,000 VND; opera/ballet 800,000–1,100,000 VND; Swan Lake 1,100,000–1,800,000 VND (Ticketbox, 2026)
  - [Google Maps](https://www.google.com/maps/search/?api=1&query=Saigon%20Opera%20House%2C%207%20C%C3%B4ng%20Tr%C6%B0%E1%BB%9Dng%20Lam%20S%C6%A1n%2C%20District%201%2C%20Ho%20Chi%20Minh%20City) · [Source](https://ticketbox.vn/anightofvirtuosoconcertos-26649)
- **Golden Dragon Water Puppet Theatre** (Nhà hát Múa rối nước Rồng Vàng) · District 1 (Bến Thành ward) · $$ · *checked 2026*
  - Saigon's dedicated water-puppet house: a theatre of about 200 seats built around a water stage. A dozen classic scenes, from buffalo herding and boat racing to Lê Lợi returning the sword and the Four Sacred Beasts, are voiced and scored live by a folk band and singers.
  - Tip: Arrive 30 minutes early, as latecomers may not be admitted and the small house fills; front rows give the clearest view of the water. Water puppetry is a Northern art, so if you've already seen it in Hanoi, spend the evening on cải lương or tài tử instead.
  - Music: Live traditional ensemble (drums, flutes, percussion) with singers voicing every scene; about 45–50 minutes.
  - Hours: Daily 18:30 (every evening in October 2026)
  - Address: Workers' Cultural Palace (Cung Văn hóa Lao động), 55B Nguyễn Thị Minh Khai, Bến Thành ward, District 1
  - Price: 350,000 VND on Ticketbox (Oct 2026); box office about 300,000–350,000 VND, cash only
  - [Google Maps](https://www.google.com/maps/search/?api=1&query=Nh%C3%A0%20h%C3%A1t%20M%C3%BAa%20r%E1%BB%91i%20n%C6%B0%E1%BB%9Bc%20R%E1%BB%93ng%20V%C3%A0ng%2C%2055B%20Nguy%E1%BB%85n%20Th%E1%BB%8B%20Minh%20Khai%2C%20District%201%2C%20Ho%20Chi%20Minh%20City) · [Source](https://ticketbox.vn/mua-roi-nuoc-rong-vang-83345)

#### Vinyl & listening bars

- **Vintage Culture Saigon** · District 1 (Cầu Ông Lãnh ward) · *checked 2025*
  - A hidden third-floor bar lined with records that feels like a friend's private listening room. It plays classic rock, blues and soul on vinyl, names cocktails after songs like 'Hotel California' and 'Stairway to Heaven', and holds a 4.9 rating from nearly 1,000 Google reviews.
  - Tip: Go early on a weeknight, and book if you want a seat at the bar by the shelves. You can ask for a favourite album from the collection.
  - Music: Vinyl all night (classic rock, blues and soul), with requests and occasional themed nights.
  - Hours: Mon–Thu 6pm–1am; Fri–Sun 6pm–2am
  - Address: 3rd floor, 39A Cô Bắc, Cầu Ông Lãnh ward, District 1
  - Price: No published prices; some reviewers find it pricey
  - [Google Maps](https://www.google.com/maps/search/?api=1&query=Vintage%20Culture%20Saigon%2C%2039A%20C%C3%B4%20B%E1%BA%AFc%2C%20District%201%2C%20Ho%20Chi%20Minh%20City) · [Source](https://www.tatlerasia.com/lifestyle/travel/saigon-ho-chi-minh-city-listening-bars)
- **Kanpai Saigon** · Thảo Điền (An Khánh ward) · *checked 2025*
  - A Japanese-style hi-fi listening bar in a cedar-panelled room. Vintage JBL speakers set into a record wall play jazz, funk, soul and city pop while you drink highballs, Japanese whisky and sake, with an omakase sushi counter alongside.
  - Tip: Arrive 5–7pm for 50% off selected drinks, or book an omakase seating at 6pm or 8pm. Closed Mondays. Start here, then head to Saxn'Art for its 9pm set.
  - Music: Vinyl selections (jazz, funk, soul, city pop) on vintage JBLs; occasional live music.
  - Hours: Tue–Thu & Sun 5pm–midnight; Fri–Sat 5pm–1am; closed Monday (Google listing)
  - Address: 44 Ngô Quang Huy, Thảo Điền (An Khánh ward)
  - Price: Happy hour 5–7pm: 50% off selected drinks (2025)
  - [Google Maps](https://www.google.com/maps/search/?api=1&query=Kanpai%20Saigon%2C%2044B%20Ng%C3%B4%20Quang%20Huy%2C%20Th%E1%BA%A3o%20%C4%90i%E1%BB%81n%2C%20Ho%20Chi%20Minh%20City) · [Source](https://mixmag.asia/feature/saigon-kanpai-vinyl-listening-sushi-sake-bar)
- **La Scène – Bistro Café x Listening Bar** · Bình Thạnh (Thạnh Mỹ Tây ward) · *checked 2026*
  - A bistro café with a listening-bar heart and a weekly rhythm built around records: a bring-your-own-vinyl wine night, live jazz on Thursdays and occasional all-night sessions from serious collectors.
  - Tip: On Wednesdays (Vinyl & Wine Flight, 6–10pm), bring an LP you love for a free glass of wine. RSVP via m.me/lascene.vn or its Resos booking page, and if you buy a bottle, a cold-cut or cheese platter is on the house. Thursday's Jazz & Friends comes with 30% off wine bottles.
  - Music: Wed bring-a-record vinyl night 6–10pm; Thu 'Jazz & Friends' live jazz 6–9pm; occasional all-night selector sessions such as Cosmic Meltdown.
  - Hours: Evenings: Wed vinyl night 6–10pm, Thu jazz 6–9pm (other hours not published)
  - Address: 27 Trần Quang Long, Thạnh Mỹ Tây ward, Bình Thạnh
  - Price: Free glass of wine with a record and RSVP on Wednesdays; 30% off wine bottles on jazz Thursdays
  - [Google Maps](https://www.google.com/maps/search/?api=1&query=La%20Sc%C3%A8ne%20Bistro%20Cafe%20x%20Listening%20Bar%2C%2027%20Tr%E1%BA%A7n%20Quang%20Long%2C%20B%C3%ACnh%20Th%E1%BA%A1nh%2C%20Ho%20Chi%20Minh%20City) · [Source](https://www.saigoneer.com/saigon-events/29002-vinyl-wine-flight-la-scène-bistro-cafe-x-listening-bar)

#### Karaoke

- **ICOOL Karaoke** (Karaoke ICOOL) · Tân Định (District 1), with branches citywide · $$ · *checked 2026*
  - The easiest way to try Vietnam's private-room karaoke. This large, legitimate chain has more than 25 branches, rents rooms by the hour and lists hours of 9am–3am, so you can still get a room after the bars close. It's a safe alternative to the hostess-karaoke places that trap visitors.
  - Tip: Walk in, or call 028 7777 9936 (hotline 1900 779 936) to hold a room for a group. Agree the room size and hourly rate at the desk, and ask the price before accepting a fruit platter. Handy branches: 90A–92 Mạc Đĩnh Chi (Tân Định), 129A Cách Mạng Tháng 8 (District 3), 39–41 Phan Chu Trinh (Bình Thạnh) and 113 Trần Não (near Thảo Điền). Autumn 2026 deals include free singing time with some after-midnight combos, a 10% ride subsidy (up to 150k VND per trip) and 10% off at partner restaurants Sushi Taco and RASA hotpot.
  - Music: Private-room karaoke, from small groups to big parties.
  - Hours: Daily 09:00–03:00 (official branch listing)
  - Address: 90A–92 Mạc Đĩnh Chi, Tân Định ward, District 1 (one of more than 25 branches)
  - Price: Hourly room rates vary by branch, room size and time and aren't published. A 2026 listicle quotes about 290,000 VND/hour at ICOOL Cách Mạng Tháng 8; student rates are 59,000 VND/hour before 7pm and 79,000 after (Sept 2026 promotion). Beer, food, fruit, service charge and VAT are extra.
  - [Google Maps](https://www.google.com/maps/search/?api=1&query=ICOOL%20Karaoke%2C%2090A%20M%E1%BA%A1c%20%C4%90%C4%A9nh%20Chi%2C%20T%C3%A2n%20%C4%90%E1%BB%8Bnh%2C%20Ho%20Chi%20Minh%20City) · [Source](https://karaoke.com.vn/phong-hat/icool-mac-dinh-chi/)
- **Alibi – Bar & Bites (Wednesday Karaoke Club)** · District 3 (Xuân Hòa ward) · $ · *checked 2026*
  - If you'd rather sing to a whole bar than book a private room, this friendly District 3 bar runs an open-mic Karaoke Club every Wednesday for an expat-leaning crowd: power ballads, throwbacks and guilty pleasures, with 30% off all cocktails all night. You don't need a group or a booking, so it suits solo travellers.
  - Tip: Arrive around 8pm to get your name on the list early. Alibi also runs a Thursday quiz (7:30pm, with a 2-hour Sapporo free-flow for 380k VND) and a Monday Cocktail Cinema. Karaoke ends at 11:40pm; to keep singing, ICOOL's rooms stay open until 3am.
  - Music: Open-mic karaoke every Wednesday, 8pm–11:40pm.
  - Hours: Karaoke Club Wed 20:00–23:40; quiz night Thu 19:30–23:50
  - Address: 93C Võ Văn Tần, Xuân Hòa ward, District 3
  - Price: No entry fee listed; 30% off all cocktails on karaoke night; Sapporo free-flow 380,000 VND for 2 hours on quiz night
  - [Google Maps](https://www.google.com/maps/search/?api=1&query=Alibi%20Bar%20%26%20Bites%2C%2093C%20V%C3%B5%20V%C4%83n%20T%E1%BA%A7n%2C%20District%203%2C%20Ho%20Chi%20Minh%20City) · [Source](https://saigoneer.com/saigon-events/28815-wednesday-karaoke-club-alibi)

#### Upcoming gigs & festivals

- **Mỹ Tâm – See The Light Day 2** (Mỹ Tâm Live Concert 'See The Light – Day 2') · Thủ Đức (Hiệp Bình ward, Vạn Phúc City) · *checked 2026*
  - Vietnam's best-loved pop diva brings 'See The Light' to Saigon for one night. The first show drew about 40,000 people in Hanoi in December 2025, and this second one expects 20,000–30,000.
  - Tip: Ticket tiers, the seating map and the on-sale date will be announced on Mỹ Tâm's official channels. Expect a fast sell-out and buy only from the official seller. Vạn Phúc City is out in Thủ Đức, so leave District 1 early and plan the ride home, because Grab prices surge after big shows.
  - Music: Mỹ Tâm's 'See The Light' stadium concert; the first show was in Hanoi on 13 Dec 2025
  - Hours: Sat 21 Nov 2026, Vạn Phúc City, Thủ Đức (start time TBA)
  - Address: Vạn Phúc City urban area (Khu đô thị Vạn Phúc), Quốc lộ 13, Hiệp Bình ward, Thủ Đức; stage location TBA
  - Price: Ticket tiers, seating map and on-sale date not yet announced (Người Quan Sát, 1 Oct 2026)
  - [Google Maps](https://www.google.com/maps/search/?api=1&query=V%E1%BA%A1n%20Ph%C3%BAc%20City%2C%20Hi%E1%BB%87p%20B%C3%ACnh%2C%20Th%E1%BB%A7%20%C4%90%E1%BB%A9c%2C%20Ho%20Chi%20Minh%20City) · [Source](https://nguoiquansat.vn/my-tam-thong-bao-tin-vui-luc-nua-dem-319489.html)
- **Những Thành Phố Mơ Màng Year End 2026 – Saigon** (Những Thành Phố Mơ Màng (NTPMM) Year End 2026 – Sài Gòn) · District 7 (Tân Mỹ ward) · $$$ · *checked 2026*
  - Vietnam's best-loved indie-pop festival brand closes the year with an afternoon-to-night show at SECC. Past year-end line-ups have ranged from Đen, tlinh and HIEUTHUHAI to Chillies, The Cassette and Wren Evans.
  - Tip: The 499k opening round has sold out. The Spotlight (755k) and Final (899k) rounds go on sale on Ticketbox, so follow NTPMM's pages for release times. Ages 16+; 10–15-year-olds need an adult guardian, who also needs a ticket. Tickets can't be refunded. Allow plenty of time for the ride to District 7. The Hanoi edition is on 17 Jan 2027.
  - Music: Multi-act Vietnamese indie, pop, rock, R&B and hip-hop line-up (TBA)
  - Hours: Sat 12 Dec 2026, SECC, District 7; Ticketbox lists 16:00–22:30, exact times TBA
  - Address: Saigon Exhibition and Convention Center (SECC), 799 Nguyễn Văn Linh, Tân Mỹ ward (District 7)
  - Price: General admission 499,000 VND (opening round, sold out), 755,000 (Spotlight round) and 899,000 (Final round) (Ticketbox, Oct 2026)
  - [Google Maps](https://www.google.com/maps/search/?api=1&query=SECC%2C%20799%20Nguy%E1%BB%85n%20V%C4%83n%20Linh%2C%20District%207%2C%20Ho%20Chi%20Minh%20City) · [Source](https://ticketbox.vn/ntpmm-year-end-saigon-2026-26646)
- **Voice of Nature – La Music × Fantasy Trio** (VOICE of NATURE – Nhạc viện TP.HCM) · District 1 (Bến Thành ward) · $$$ · *checked 2026*
  - La Music's annual wordless concert tells nature's story in three chapters (Grandeur, Destruction, Rebirth) without narration. The young Fantasy Trio and La String Orchestra play among a living forest of real plants built on stage, in the city's conservatory hall.
  - Tip: Buy on Flip.vn; there are four seat zones from 500k. Saigoneer lists two nights (16 and 17 Oct), but on 6 Oct Flip was selling only the Saturday show.
  - Music: Chamber and string-orchestra music with no narration: Fantasy Trio (San Trần, piano; Hoàng Yến, flute; Quân Hoàng, clarinet) with La String Orchestra led by violinist Hồ Nguyên
  - Hours: Fri 16 & Sat 17 Oct 2026, 19:30–21:30, HCMC Conservatory of Music
  - Address: HCMC Conservatory of Music (Nhạc viện TP.HCM), 112 Nguyễn Du, Bến Thành ward, District 1
  - Price: 500,000 / 700,000 / 900,000 / 1,200,000 VND by zone (Mầm / Chồi / Lá / Rừng; Flip.vn, Oct 2026)
  - [Google Maps](https://www.google.com/maps/search/?api=1&query=Nh%E1%BA%A1c%20vi%E1%BB%87n%20TP.HCM%2C%20112%20Nguy%E1%BB%85n%20Du%2C%20District%201%2C%20Ho%20Chi%20Minh%20City) · [Source](https://www.saigoneer.com/saigon-events/29240-concert-voice-of-nature-hcmc-conservatory-of-music)

#### Records & festivals

- **Times Records** (Hãng Đĩa Thời Đại) · Tân Định (District 1) · $$$ · *checked 2025*
  - An independent Saigon label that presses new Vietnamese releases on vinyl. Its wood-toned shop (piano included) is the place to buy local LPs, CDs and cassettes you won't find abroad, along with international pop, rock, jazz and oldies.
  - Tip: No shop hours are published, so message the label through hangdiathoidai.com or its socials before crossing town. Combine it with a morning in Tân Định.
  - Address: 6 Nguyễn Văn Nguyễn, Tân Định ward, District 1
  - Price: New Vietnamese LPs mostly about 1–1.35M VND on the label's online shop (Oct 2026); CDs and cassettes cheaper
  - [Google Maps](https://www.google.com/maps/search/?api=1&query=H%C3%A3ng%20%C4%90%C4%A9a%20Th%E1%BB%9Di%20%C4%90%E1%BA%A1i%2C%206%20Nguy%E1%BB%85n%20V%C4%83n%20Nguy%E1%BB%85n%2C%20T%C3%A2n%20%C4%90%E1%BB%8Bnh%2C%20Ho%20Chi%20Minh%20City) · [Source](https://www.hangdiathoidai.com/about)

### Saigon: Games (Trò chơi)

Saigon is a superb city for video-game shooters: Vikings and CyberCore arenas put 360–390Hz screens and RTX 40- and 50-series rigs in front of you for well under US$1 an hour, though the best sit outside District 1 and by law every cyber closes from 22:00 to 08:00. Real-world shooting is tightly regulated, so it centres on the military-run live-fire range at the Củ Chi Tunnels plus a few licensed air-rifle, paintball and archery clubs; we found no walk-in laser tag, licensed airsoft or console lounge with recent evidence. Actor-led horror escape rooms, a central Timezone arcade and a tournament-grade bowling alley round out the play.

#### Shooting, paintball & archery

- **★ Củ Chi Tunnels Shooting Range (Bến Đình)** (Trường bắn súng thể thao quốc phòng – Địa đạo Bến Đình, Củ Chi) · Củ Chi (Nhuận Đức commune), a day trip northwest of the city · $$$$ · *checked 2026*
  - Saigon's classic live-fire experience: at the military-run range inside the Củ Chi Tunnels relic site you fire real war-era weapons such as the AK-47, M16, carbine, K54 and the RPD or M60 machine gun, right after crawling the tunnels. Guns are locked to mounts and fire single shots under an instructor, and the crack of rounds carries across the whole site.
  - Tip: It's 18+ only, with no alcohol or stimulants beforehand; you buy a minimum of 10 rounds at the range counter (cash or card) and wear the earmuffs provided. Arrive at the 7:00 opening, crawl the tunnels first, then shoot. The speedboat up the Saigon River takes about 1h20 against 2–3 hours by road (on a budget, take bus 13 to Củ Chi bus station, then a local bus or Grab). For a team shooter, the Bến Dược side runs a paintball 'war game': about 250k for uniform, referee and 50 balls, roughly 60 minutes (tel. 096 143 33 81).
  - Hours: Site open daily 7:00–17:00
  - Address: Ấp Bến Đình, Nhuận Đức commune (former Củ Chi district), HCMC
  - Price: About 75,000 VND (≈US$3) per bullet in 2026, minimum 10 rounds (750,000 VND), paid on the day; foreigner entry 135,000 VND. Bullets were 60,000 VND in Nov 2024.
  - [Google Maps](https://www.google.com/maps/search/?api=1&query=Tr%C6%B0%E1%BB%9Dng%20b%E1%BA%AFn%20%C4%90%E1%BB%8Ba%20%C4%91%E1%BA%A1o%20B%E1%BA%BFn%20%C4%90%C3%ACnh%2C%20Nhu%E1%BA%ADn%20%C4%90%E1%BB%A9c%2C%20C%E1%BB%A7%20Chi%2C%20Ho%20Chi%20Minh%20City) · [Source](https://www.lesrivesexperience.com/faq/)
- **Phú Thọ Paintball Club** (CLB Súng sơn Phú Thọ) · District 11 (Phú Thọ ward) · $$ · *likely open*
  - Saigon's most central paintball: staff kit you out in full protective gear and brief you before every game on the club's obstacle field. It's run by the team behind the Saigon Shooting club, whose site frames paintball markers as licensed 'sports weapons' under Law 42/2024, and since we found no walk-in laser tag or licensed airsoft in town, it's the city's best team shooter.
  - Tip: Book by phone (094 303 60 36), especially for weekends, and if you're solo ask whether they can fit you into a group game. Keep your mask on at all times on the field and ask about the minimum age when you book. Work on a new park over part of the old Phú Thọ racecourse along Lê Đại Hành began in November 2025, so confirm the field is running before you go; Củ Chi's war game is the fallback.
  - Hours: Tue–Sun 8:00–21:30 (Aug 2025 listing)
  - Address: Gate 1: 2B Lê Đại Hành; Gate 2: 219 Lý Thường Kiệt, District 11
  - Price: Per player: G-250 250k (full gear + 100 balls), G-350 350k (200 balls), G-500 500k (300 balls); extra balls 2,000 VND each, minimum 100 (Aug 2025)
  - [Google Maps](https://www.google.com/maps/search/?api=1&query=CLB%20S%C3%BAng%20s%C6%A1n%20Ph%C3%BA%20Th%E1%BB%8D%2C%202B%20L%C3%AA%20%C4%90%E1%BA%A1i%20H%C3%A0nh%2C%20Qu%E1%BA%ADn%2011%2C%20Ho%20Chi%20Minh%20City) · [Source](https://vinwonders.com/vi/wonderpedia/news/ban-sung-son-tphcm/)
- **Saigon Sniper** (CLB Bắn súng thể thao Saigon Sniper) · District 1 (Bến Nghé) · $ · *likely open*
  - Founded in 2012 and based since 2014 at the Nguyễn Bỉnh Khiêm sports club, this 10-lane indoor air-rifle range has fed talent to the city's shooting team and welcomes tourists and first-timers, making it the easiest target-shooting fix in central Saigon. Staff coach beginners on stance, grip and trigger control.
  - Tip: Start with the 10-pellet try-out before buying a bigger pack, and call ahead (090 833 06 93), as the range also hosts company team-building groups. The rifles stay on the range under supervision: Vietnam classes air guns as licensed sports weapons. Staying in Thảo Điền? The similar Saigon Shooting club (47 Đường số 66, Thảo Điền, and Lê Thị Riêng Park, 875 Cách Mạng Tháng Tám) includes a briefing and supervision; its site lists 8:30–21:30 daily.
  - Hours: Daily 9:00–21:00 (Aug 2025 listing)
  - Address: 25 Nguyễn Bỉnh Khiêm (Nguyễn Bỉnh Khiêm sports club), District 1; second branch at 8 Phan Đăng Lưu, Bình Thạnh
  - Price: 35,000 VND for 10 pellets; 170,000 for 60; 600,000 for 240 (Jan 2025 listing)
  - [Google Maps](https://www.google.com/maps/search/?api=1&query=Saigon%20Sniper%2C%2025%20Nguy%E1%BB%85n%20B%E1%BB%89nh%20Khi%C3%AAm%2C%20Qu%E1%BA%ADn%201%2C%20Ho%20Chi%20Minh%20City) · [Source](https://vinwonders.com/vi/wonderpedia/news/ban-sung-o-sai-gon/)
- **Tinh Võ Archery Club** (CLB Bắn cung Tinh Võ) · Chợ Lớn (District 5) · $ · *checked 2025*
  - Walk-in target archery in Chợ Lớn, coached by what the club calls a roster of national-level coaches, and cheap enough for a half-hour session between dim sum and pagoda-hopping.
  - Tip: Weekday sessions start only at 13:00; weekends from 9:00. Instructors guide and supervise every session, so follow their range calls, and call +84 909 177 038 before bringing a group. Pair it with NétGen Hub on An Dương Vương for a Chợ Lớn games afternoon.
  - Hours: Mon–Fri 13:00–21:00; Sat–Sun 9:00–21:00
  - Address: 1108 Võ Văn Kiệt (or alley 107 Trần Hưng Đạo), District 5
  - Price: 50,000 VND per 30 min; 80,000 VND per hour; monthly membership 499,000 VND (2025 listings)
  - [Google Maps](https://www.google.com/maps/search/?api=1&query=CLB%20B%E1%BA%AFn%20cung%20Tinh%20V%C3%B5%2C%201108%20V%C3%B5%20V%C4%83n%20Ki%E1%BB%87t%2C%20Qu%E1%BA%ADn%205%2C%20Ho%20Chi%20Minh%20City) · [Source](https://www.bancungtinhvo.com/)

#### Esports & PC gaming

- **★ Vikings Esports Stadium Nguyễn Thị Thập** · District 7 (Tân Hưng ward) · $ · *checked 2026*
  - The Saigon 'esports stadium' of the Hanoi-born Vikings chain packs 200-plus PCs into priced zones, topped by a Competitive Zone of i7-12700F and RTX 4060 Ti rigs on BenQ Zowie XL2566K 360Hz panels with Endgame Gear XM1R mice, plus a Super Zone on 380Hz ASUS ROG Strix screens. It's the best-documented shooter setup in the city for CS2, Valorant and PUBG, and it hosted the final of Vikings' own Valorant cup in October 2025.
  - Tip: Skip the 10k/hour Standard Zone (GTX 1660 Super): for shooters take Super (RTX 3060, 380Hz, 14k) or Competitive (RTX 4060 Ti, 360Hz, 18k). Bring your passport and the cashier will set up a Vikings ID with 10 free minutes; prepaid time stays at the branch where you top up. Reserve a Competitive seat on 086 712 2247 or through the Vikings fanpage or Zalo, and order a rice plate (about 45–50k) to your desk via the VOS app.
  - Hours: Not published online; by law, public game venues close 22:00–08:00
  - Address: 300 Nguyễn Thị Thập, Tân Hưng ward (District 7)
  - Price: PC per hour: 10k (Standard), 12k (Pro), 14k (Super, 380Hz), 18k (Competitive, 360Hz), 24k VND (Stream Room)
  - [Google Maps](https://www.google.com/maps/search/?api=1&query=Vikings%20Esports%2C%20300%20Nguy%E1%BB%85n%20Th%E1%BB%8B%20Th%E1%BA%ADp%2C%20T%C3%A2n%20H%C6%B0ng%2C%20Ho%20Chi%20Minh%20City) · [Source](https://www.vikings.vn/cyber/nguyen-thi-thap)
- **Net Vikings Thảo Điền** · Thảo Điền (An Khánh ward, former District 2) · $ · *checked 2026*
  - The expat village's serious shooter room, opened in late 2025: a Master Zone of i7-12700F and RTX 4060 rigs on AOC AG256FS 390Hz screens and Duo seats on 360Hz ASUS ROG Swift panels. The 'Net Vikings' format puts food, drinks, showers and a convenience store under one roof.
  - Tip: The Master Zone (16.9k/hour) is the pick for CS2 and Valorant; the Private Zone (Ryzen 7 9700X, RTX 4060 Ti, 27-inch 2K 300Hz) costs 18.9k. If it's full, the sister Net Vikings at 67 Trần Não is a short ride away. Book a Master seat for evenings on 034 790 2247, and bring your passport to set up a Vikings ID.
  - Hours: Not published online; by law, public game venues close 22:00–08:00
  - Address: 10 Đường số 66, An Khánh ward (Thảo Điền)
  - Price: PC per hour: 12.5k (Pro, 240Hz), 14.5k (Super), 16.9k (Master 390Hz or Duo 360Hz), 18.9k VND (Private)
  - [Google Maps](https://www.google.com/maps/search/?api=1&query=Net%20Vikings%20Th%E1%BA%A3o%20%C4%90i%E1%BB%81n%2C%2010%20%C4%90%C6%B0%E1%BB%9Dng%20s%E1%BB%91%2066%2C%20An%20Kh%C3%A1nh%2C%20Ho%20Chi%20Minh%20City) · [Source](https://www.vikings.vn/cyber/net-vikings-thao-dien)
- **Vikings Cyber Lê Quang Định** (Vikings Lê Quang Định) · Bình Thạnh (Gia Định ward) · $ · *checked 2026*
  - Vikings' other big Saigon arena has 214 PCs over two floors, every main monitor at 240Hz or faster, a Competitive Zone of i7-12700F and RTX 4060 rigs on BenQ XL2566K 360Hz panels, and Stream Room seats built around a Ryzen 7 7800X3D. It's the chain's big arena on the north side of town, handy from Bình Thạnh and Phú Nhuận.
  - Tip: Standard seats (i3-12100F with RX 5600 XT, 240Hz) cost 10k/hour, but for CS2 and Valorant the Super Zone (RTX 4060 on a 380Hz ASUS ROG Strix XG259QN) at 14k is the best value. One Vikings ID works chain-wide, though prepaid time stays where you top up. Book on 097 982 2247.
  - Hours: Not published online; by law, public game venues close 22:00–08:00
  - Address: Floors 1–2, 259 Lê Quang Định, Gia Định ward (Bình Thạnh)
  - Price: PC per hour: 10k (Standard), 12k (Pro), 14k (Super, 380Hz), 18k (Competitive, 360Hz), 24k VND (Stream Room)
  - [Google Maps](https://www.google.com/maps/search/?api=1&query=Vikings%20L%C3%AA%20Quang%20%C4%90%E1%BB%8Bnh%2C%20259%20L%C3%AA%20Quang%20%C4%90%E1%BB%8Bnh%2C%20Gia%20%C4%90%E1%BB%8Bnh%2C%20Ho%20Chi%20Minh%20City) · [Source](https://www.vikings.vn/cyber/le-quang-dinh)
- **NétGen Hub An Dương Vương (CyberCore)** (NétGen Hub An Dương Vương) · District 5 (An Đông ward) · *checked 2026*
  - CyberCore's newest NétGen Hub opened for Tết 2026 with 104 identical rigs (RTX 5060, i5-12400F, 32GB RAM, 27-inch 240–260Hz screens), so there's no budget tier to get stuck in. Its five zones include a couples' area, a co-working corner and sofa lounges.
  - Tip: Rates and hours aren't published online, so message the NétGen Hub or CyberCore fanpage before crossing town. CyberCore's first NétGen Hub, at N3 Trường Sơn (Hòa Hưng ward, old District 10), has 100 PCs split into 'tryhard', chill, study and couple zones plus a VIP room. Tinh Võ archery is a short ride away for a Chợ Lớn double bill.
  - Hours: Not published online; by law, public game venues close 22:00–08:00
  - Address: 485 An Dương Vương, An Đông ward (District 5)
  - Price: Hourly rates not published online
  - [Google Maps](https://www.google.com/maps/search/?api=1&query=N%C3%A9tGen%20Hub%2C%20485%20An%20D%C6%B0%C6%A1ng%20V%C6%B0%C6%A1ng%2C%20Qu%E1%BA%ADn%205%2C%20Ho%20Chi%20Minh%20City) · [Source](https://cybercore.vn/project/netgenhub-an-duong-vuong/)

#### Escape rooms, arcades & bowling

- **Real Escape** · Phú Nhuận & District 10 · $$ · *checked 2025*
  - Nine story-driven escape rooms where live actors stalk you through richly detailed sets, from The Cemetery and The Cursed Hotel to The Tailor, Dark Ritual and The Killing Camp, added in October 2025. Horror is the house style, so expect chases and jump scares alongside the puzzles.
  - Tip: Minimum two players; book a day ahead via the Real Escape Facebook page or hotline 090 269 83 62. Games run in Vietnamese, so ask when booking whether an English-speaking host and hints are available. Mon–Thu is cheapest, and the 2-hour combo costs barely more than a single hour.
  - Hours: Daily 9:00–22:00
  - Address: 170D/6 Phan Đăng Lưu, Phú Nhuận; 662/3 Sư Vạn Hạnh, District 10
  - Price: 1 hour 169–199k per person (Mon–Thu cheapest, holidays dearest); 2-hour combo 269–289k (official price list, late 2025)
  - [Google Maps](https://www.google.com/maps/search/?api=1&query=Real%20Escape%2C%20170D/6%20Phan%20%C4%90%C4%83ng%20L%C6%B0u%2C%20Ph%C3%BA%20Nhu%E1%BA%ADn%2C%20Ho%20Chi%20Minh%20City) · [Source](https://realescape.vn/)
- **Timezone Vincom Đồng Khởi** · District 1 (Sài Gòn ward) · $$ · *checked 2026*
  - Timezone's 14th Vietnamese centre opened in the basement of Vincom Đồng Khởi in July 2025, making it the most central arcade in the city. It suits a rainy-afternoon high-score session between cafés rather than serious PC play.
  - Tip: You play on a reloadable Powercard (minimum top-up 100k); register it to keep your credit and Timezone Rewards. For ten-pin bowling and VR, go to Timezone Estella Place in An Phú (4F, 88 Song Hành, 9:30–22:00); AEON Mall Tân Phú and SC VivoCity have the other big HCMC centres.
  - Hours: Daily 10:00–22:00
  - Address: B2, Vincom Center Đồng Khởi, 72 Lê Thánh Tôn, District 1
  - Price: Powercard top-up from 100,000 VND; replacement card 60,000 VND
  - [Google Maps](https://www.google.com/maps/search/?api=1&query=Timezone%20Vincom%20%C4%90%E1%BB%93ng%20Kh%E1%BB%9Fi%2C%2072%20L%C3%AA%20Th%C3%A1nh%20T%C3%B4n%2C%20S%C3%A0i%20G%C3%B2n%20ward%2C%20Ho%20Chi%20Minh%20City) · [Source](https://www.timezonegames.com/en-vn/venues/hcm-city/vincom-dong-khoi/)
- **Megabowl Bowling Center** (Megabowl Việt Nam) · Cát Lái (Thủ Đức, former District 2) · *checked 2026*
  - Saigon's tournament-grade alley: 16 international-standard lanes with weekly in-house tournaments, coaching courses, video games and a food court. It's where the city's serious bowlers train, and it hosts a free Para Bowling club for disabled players.
  - Tip: Lanes open only at 12:30 Mon–Thu, so go Fri–Sun for daytime games, and call +84 983 288 328 to check tournament nights, when lanes can fill up. It's a long Grab ride east of the centre; for casual bowling nearer Thảo Điền, Timezone Estella Place has lanes.
  - Hours: Mon–Thu 12:30–22:30; Fri–Sun 10:00–22:30
  - Address: 2F, The CBD Premium Home, 125 Đồng Văn Cống, Cát Lái ward
  - Price: Lane prices not published online
  - [Google Maps](https://www.google.com/maps/search/?api=1&query=Megabowl%2C%20125%20%C4%90%E1%BB%93ng%20V%C4%83n%20C%E1%BB%91ng%2C%20C%C3%A1t%20L%C3%A1i%2C%20Ho%20Chi%20Minh%20City) · [Source](https://megabowl.com.vn/gioi-thieu/)

### Saigon: Books & literary (Sách & văn chương)

Saigon reads in public: a pedestrian Book Street beside the Central Post Office, alley houses that sell used books by the kilo, and a small but growing set of English and French shops run by translators and book-mad couples. Its literary landmarks are remarkably intact: you can still drink at Graham Greene's Continental, walk the rue Catinat of The Quiet American and pass the lycée where Marguerite Duras studied. Readings are mostly in Vietnamese, and this season's big dates, the Tết Book Street Festival and Vietnam Poetry Day, both fall in February 2027.

#### Bookshops & book streets

- **★ Ho Chi Minh City Book Street (Nguyễn Văn Bình)** (Đường sách TP.HCM (đường Nguyễn Văn Bình)) · District 1 (beside the Central Post Office and Notre-Dame) · $ · *checked 2026*
  - Vietnam's first book street, opened in January 2016, is a tree-shaded pedestrian lane beside the Central Post Office with about 30 stalls of publishers, second-hand dealers and book cafés. It hosts around 300 launches, talks and readings a year, and in September 2026 Tuổi Trẻ reported that 1000 Libraries magazine had named it one of the world's ten most famous book markets.
  - Tip: Go at the weekend, when most launches and author talks run (largely in Vietnamese); the street's own website is stale, so search Tuổi Trẻ for 'Đường sách' to see what's on. For English reading, look for Little Cats, a used foreign-language bookstall on the street. It's pedestrian-only: walk over from the Post Office or park at Diamond Plaza or Nhà Văn hóa Thanh niên.
  - Hours: Daily from about 8am into the evening (listings give 21:00–22:00; stalls vary)
  - Address: Nguyễn Văn Bình street, Sài Gòn ward (District 1)
